Experiments show that RecMem reduces the memory construction token cost of three SOTA memory systems by up to 87% while exceeding their accuracy.

</details>


### [40] [Can Large Language Models Imitate Human Speech for Clinical Assessment? LLM-Driven Data Augmentation for Cognitive Score Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16077)
*Si-Belkacem Yamine Ketir,Lenard Paulo Tamayo,Shohei Hisada,Shaowen Peng,Shoko Wakamiya,Eiji Aramaki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出利用LLM（GPT-5）根据参与者的书面文本生成多样的口语数据进行增强，通过相似度引导的策略有效解决了认知评估中的数据不平衡问题，提升了长谷川痴呆量表评分的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的认知能力下降评估受限于数据集规模小和类别不平衡问题，难以做出准确预测。

Method: 使用GPT-5利用参与者的书面回答（作为语义锚点）生成多种不同风格的口语型独白；利用Sentence-BERT提取特征；使用偏最小二乘回归（PLS）预测长谷川痴呆量表（HDS）分数；对比了随机类平衡选择和相似度引导的类平衡选择两种增强策略。

Result: 相似度引导的类平衡选择策略不仅显著降低了少数低分群体的预测误差，同时保持了多数群体的预测表现，比随机增强策略更具稳定性和一致性。

Conclusion: 证明了基于语义引导的LLM增强是一种有效的原则性方法，能够解决临床语音分析中的数据不平衡问题并提高数据效率。

Abstract: Accurate assessment of cognitive decline from spontaneous speech remains challenging due to limited dataset size and class imbalance. In this work, we propose a large language model (LLM)-driven data augmentation framework to improve the prediction of cognitive scores from speech. Experiments are conducted on a Japanese corpus in which each participant provides both a spontaneous oral narrative and a written response to the same clinical prompt. The written responses serve as semantic anchors to generate multiple oral-like monologues in different styles using GPT-5. We then predict Hasegawa Dementia Scale scores, a widely used cognitive screening tool in Japan, using a Partial Least Squares regression model trained on Sentence-BERT speech embeddings. We investigate two augmentation strategies: random class-balanced selection, which yields moderate but unstable improvements, and similarity-guided class-balanced selection. The latter prioritizes semantically close synthetic samples, leading to more consistent improvements and substantially reducing prediction error for minority low-score participants while maintaining performance for the majority group. Overall, our findings demonstrate the potential of semantically guided LLM-driven augmentation as a principled approach for addressing class imbalance and improving data efficiency in clinical speech analysis.

</details>


### [41] [Multi-Level Contextual Token Relation Modeling for Machine-Generated Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16107)
*Chenwang Wu,Yiuming Cheung,Bo Han,Shuhai Zhang,Defu Lian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对机器生成文本检测中传统指标方法易受生成随机性干扰的问题，提出了一种结合局部马尔可夫校准与全局规则推理的多级上下文建模框架，实现了高效且鲁棒的检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模型的方法容易过拟合，而基于指标（Metric-based）的方法虽实用但易受机器生成文本（MGT）生成过程中 inherent randomness（固有随机性）的干扰，导致检测性能受限。

Method: 1. 将现有度量方法置于统一分析框架，识别出“生成随机性导致的偏差”这一核心问题；2. 理论推导token级得分的多跳转移关系；3. 提出多级上下文 token 关系建模框架（MCTR），包括用于局部校准的马尔可夫信息模块和用于全局关系的启发式规则推理模块。

Result: 在多个现实场景（跨LLM、跨领域）中实现了广泛且显著的性能提升，且具备较低的计算开销。

Conclusion: 该研究通过结合局部（马尔可夫校准）和全局（规则推理）上下文关系建模，有效解决了机器生成文本中因生成随机性导致的检测偏差，证明了该方法在多场景、跨模型及跨域设置下的优越性与高效性。

Abstract: Machine-generated texts (MGTs) pose risks such as disinformation and phishing, underscoring the need for reliable detection. Metric-based methods, which extract statistically distinguishable features of MGTs, are often more practical than complex model-based methods that are prone to overfitting. Given their diverse designs, we first place representative metric-based methods within a unified framework, enabling a clear assessment of their advantages and limitations. Our analysis identifies a core challenge across these methods: the token-level detection score is easily biased by the inherent randomness of the MGTs generation process. Then, we theoretically derive the multi-hop transitions of the token-level detection score and explore their local and global relations. Based on these findings, we propose a multi-level contextual token relation modeling framework for MGT detection. Specifically, for local relations, we model them through a lightweight Markov-informed calibration module that refines token-level evidence before aggregation. For global relations, we introduce a rule-support reasoning module that uses explicit logical rules derived from contextual score statistics. Finally, we combine the local calibrated score and the global rule-support reasoning signal in a joint multi-level inference framework. Extensive experiments show broad and substantial improvements across various real-world scenarios, including cross-LLM and cross-domain settings, with low computational overhead.

</details>


### [42] [DebiasRAG: A Tuning-Free Path to Fair Generation in Large Language Models through Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16113)
*Rui Chu,Bingyin Zhao,Thanh Quoc Hung Le,Duy Cao Hoang,Huawei Lin,Ping Li,Weijie Zhao,Khoa D Doan,Yingjie Lao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 DebiasRAG，一种无需微调的大模型动态去偏框架，通过结合针对查询的去偏上下文检索与重排序，在提升公平性的同时保持了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型（LLM）因训练数据导致存在种族、性别、年龄等社会偏见。现有的微调和提示工程方法存在资源消耗高、需要领域知识、可能损害模型原始性能以及缺乏动态适应性等局限。

Method: 提出了 DebiasRAG 框架，包含三个阶段：1) 生成查询特定的去偏候选；2) 构建上下文候选池；3) 基于梯度更新的去偏引导上下文重排序（Re-ranking）。该方法利用预设的偏差上下文逆向生成去偏约束，并结合常规 RAG 检索的知识进行公平推理。

Result: 实验表明，DebiasRAG 能够有效减轻 LLM 输出中的社会偏见，同时保持模型原有的表征能力和通用性能，无需额外的训练成本。

Conclusion: DebiasRAG 通过 RAG 架构实现了一种无需微调、动态且针对查询的去偏方法，在提升大语言模型公平性的同时，有效保留了模型的原始生成性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved unprecedented success due to their exceptional generative capabilities. However, because they depend on knowledge encapsulated from training corpora, they may produce hallucinations, stereotypes, and socially biased content. In particular, LLMs are prone to prejudiced responses involving race, gender, and age, which are collectively referred to as social biases. Prior studies have used fine-tuning and prompt engineering to mitigate such biases in LLMs, but these methods require additional training resources or domain knowledge to design the framework. Moreover, they may degrade the original capabilities of LLMs and often overlook the need for dynamic debiasing contexts for fairer inference. In this paper, we propose DebiasRAG, a novel tuning-free and dynamic query-specific debiasing framework based on retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). DebiasRAG improves fairness while preserving the intrinsic properties of LLMs, such as representation ability. DebiasRAG consists of three stages: (1) query-specific debiasing candidate generation; (2) context candidate pool construction; and (3) gradient-updated debiasing-guided context piece reranking. First, DebiasRAG leverages self-diagnosed bias contexts relevant to the query through regular retrieval, where the bias contexts are prepared offline by the DebiasRAG provider. Given the query-specific bias contexts, DebiasRAG reversely produces debiasing contexts, which are provided as additional fairness constraints for LLM outputs. Second, a regular RAG retrieval process produces query-related contexts from the regular RAG document database, such as a chunked Wikipedia dataset.

</details>


### [43] [SGR: A Stepwise Reasoning Framework for LLMs with External Subgraph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16117)
*Xin Zhang,Yang Cao,Baoxing Wu,Kai Song,Siying Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 为了解决 LLM 在复杂推理中准确性不足及内容幻觉问题，本文提出了 SGR 框架，通过引入外部知识图谱子图辅助多步推理，显著增强了模型的推理能力和事实可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型在处理需要深层逻辑推理的复杂任务时表现受限，且在生成过程中容易产生无关、噪声或与事实不符的内容。现有的纯文本生成方式缺乏对结构化知识的有效归因。

Method: 提出了一个名为 SGR 的逐步推理框架。该框架首先根据输入问题从外部知识库中构建特定的子图，随后引导模型基于该子图结构进行多步推理，并整合多条推理路径以得出最终预测结果。

Result: 在多个基准测试数据集上的实验结果表明，SGR 框架 consistently 地（一致性地）优于对比基准模型，有效提升了推理精度和事实可靠性。

Conclusion: SGR 框架通过集成外部知识图谱的子图结构，显著提升了 LLM 在复杂推理任务中的准确性和事实可靠性，证明了结构化知识在增强模型推理能力方面的有效性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities across diverse NLP applications, such as translation, text generation, and question answering. Nevertheless, they remain limited in complex settings that demand deep reasoning and logical inference. Since these models are trained on large-scale text corpora, their generation process may still introduce irrelevant, noisy, or factually inconsistent content. To mitigate this problem, we introduce SGR, a stepwise framework that enhances LLM reasoning through external subgraph generation. SGR builds query-specific subgraphs from external knowledge bases and uses their semantic structure to support multi-step inference. By grounding intermediate reasoning steps in structured external knowledge, the framework helps the model concentrate on relevant entities, relations, and supporting evidence. In particular, SGR first constructs a subgraph tailored to the input question. It then guides the model to reason progressively over the generated structure and combines multiple reasoning trajectories to obtain the final prediction. Experimental results across several benchmark datasets show that SGR achieves consistent improvements over competitive baselines, highlighting its value for improving both reasoning accuracy and factual reliability.

</details>


### [44] [Optimized Three-Dimensional Photovoltaic Structures with LLM guided Tree Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16191)
*Michael P. Brenner,Lizzie Dorfman,John C. Platt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种结合 AI 编码智能体与树搜索 (ERA) 的框架，通过动态修正物理约束解决了 AI 在设计优化中产生的“奖励黑客”现象，成功实现了 3D 光伏发电结构的高效自动化开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨如何利用 AI 编码系统自动生成新颖的科学假设，并解决传统优化算法在发现科学设计时可能遇到的欺骗性目标（reward hacking）问题。

Method: 1. 利用编码智能体（Google AntiGravity）复现现有关于 3DPV 的计算；2. 使用 LLM 驱动的树搜索算法 (ERA) 对 3DPV 结构进行大规模空间搜索；3. 针对搜索过程中的“奖励黑客”（reward hacking）问题（如悬浮结构、利用数值离散化漏洞），开发迭代修正工作流，通过编程智能体动态给物理引擎添加约束条件。

Result: 在消除了奖励黑客行为后，ERA 成功发现了在不同约束条件下（如固定集光面积、优化天顶跟踪、避免自遮挡）性能表现优异的 3D 光伏 (3DPV) 结构设计。

Conclusion: 将 AI 编程智能体与树搜索算法 (ERA) 相结合，为科学发现提供了一个强大的自动化平台，特别适用于能够通过评价函数进行经验评估的问题。

Abstract: We present a case study for how AI coding systems can be used to generate novel scientific hypotheses. We combine a generic coding agent (Google's AntiGravity) with an LLM-driven tree search algorithm (Empirical Research Assistance / ERA) to autonomously generate high-efficiency three-dimensional photovoltaic (3DPV) structures that overcome losses limiting flat solar panels at mid-latitudes. These structures operate by presenting favorable angles to the sun throughout the day, and for illustrative purposes we focus on optimizing performance for a single solar day. Our workflow begins by using AntiGravity to reproduce calculations \cite{bernardi2012solar} showing that 3DPV can have energy densities much higher than stationary flat PV panels. We use these initial designs as the starting point for large scale tree search, where we seek improved solutions and score them for their diurnal yield. The initial tree search leads to nominally more efficient solutions, yet they are caused by algorithmic reward hacking, arising from non-physical design features such as structurally levitating disconnected tiers and exploitations of the discretizations in the optics solver. To counteract this, we develop a workflow where the coding agent iteratively patches the physics engine with constraints to eliminate reward hacking. With reward-hacking eliminated, ERA discovers a series of designs with various constraints and improved performance, including optimal designs with different fixed collector areas, optimizing zenith tracking and avoiding self shadowing.
  Combining coding agents with tree search (ERA) provides a powerful platform for scientific discovery, for problems whose solutions can be empirically evaluated with a score function.

</details>

### [45] [TeamTR: Trust-Region Fine-Tuning for Multi-Agent LLM Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15207)
*Yi Xie,Siao Liu,Falong Fan,Yuanqi Yao,Yue Zhao,Bo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多智能体LLM系统微调时的上下文分布偏移问题，本文提出了TeamTR框架，通过重采样和信任区域控制有效提升了协作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统在复杂推理任务中表现不佳，研究发现其根源在于顺序微调时的“复合占用偏移”（compounding occupancy shift）——即更新一个智能体会改变团队的上下文分布，导致后续评估失效。

Method: 提出了TeamTR（一种信任区域框架），该框架在每个组件更新后重新采样轨迹，并执行每个智能体的散度控制（divergence control）。此外，引入了“中间占用评估”（intermediate-occupancy evaluation）以减少分布偏移带来的性能损失。

Result: 在实验中，TeamTR平均表现优于单智能体和顺序基准7.1%，显着缓解了协作过程中的退化现象，并提供了严格的性能改善下限。

Conclusion: TeamTR框架通过中间占用评估和信任区域策略，有效缓解了多智能体系统中的分布偏移问题，实现了比单智能体和顺序基线更优的性能，并支持即插即用的组件替换。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems have shown promise for complex reasoning, yet recent evaluations reveal they often underperform single-model baselines. We identify a structural failure mode in sequential fine-tuning of shared-context teams: updating one agent shifts the team's context distribution, and when subsequent updates are evaluated on cached rollouts, this mismatch compounds. We formalize this as the compounding occupancy shift and prove that stale-occupancy evaluation incurs a penalty that scales quadratically with the number of agents. In contrast, intermediate-occupancy evaluation reduces this to linear scaling. We propose TeamTR, a trust-region framework that resamples trajectories after each component update and enforces per-agent divergence control, yielding rigorous per-update and per-stage improvement lower bounds. Experiments show that TeamTR outperforms single-agent and sequential baselines with 7.1% on average, mitigates coordination regressions, and supports plug-and-play component replacement. Code is available at https://github.com/Yydc/TeamTR.

</details>


### [46] [Quantization Undoes Alignment: Bias Emergence in Compressed LLMs Across Models and Precision Levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15208)
*Plawan Kumar Rath,Rahul Maliakkal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现大模型的后训练量化（特别是低比特量化）会诱发隐藏的偏见行为，而传统的聚合质量指标（如困惑度）无法察觉这种公平性的退化，强调了部署前进行专门的公平性安全性评估的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型广泛采用后训练量化以降低推理成本，但该过程对模型质量（尤其是公平性）的具体影响尚不明确；现有研究因评估方式单一及侧重于聚合指标，难以区分模型性能的渐进式下降与门槛触发的安全失效。

Method: 对三个不同的指令微调模型（Qwen2.5-7B, Mistral-7B, Phi-3.5-mini）进行了受控实证研究，在五个精度级别（BF16至3-bit）下，使用BBQ偏见基准进行了超过91万次的推理记录，并通过逻辑回归分析量化精度与偏见行为之间的剂量反应关系。

Result: 3-bit量化导致6-21%的无偏条目涌现出新的刻板行为，且模型选择“未知”选项的意愿下降了17.4%；常规指标（如困惑度）在4-bit量化下变化极小（<3%），完全掩盖了同期发生的2.5-5.6%的偏见增长。

Conclusion: 现有的聚合评估指标（如困惑度）无法有效捕捉量化带来的公平性风险。量化模型在降低精度的同时，会诱发新的刻板印象偏见，因此必须建立在部署前显式测试偏见涌现的压缩协议。

Abstract: Large Language Models are routinely compressed via post-training quantization to reduce inference costs and memory footprint for cloud and edge deployment, yet the impact of this compression on model quality remains poorly understood. Existing studies typically compare only two conditions (full-precision vs. a single quantized variant), rely on aggregate bias metrics, and evaluate a single model family, making it impossible to distinguish gradual degradation from threshold-dependent safety failures. We conduct a controlled empirical study of three instruction-tuned models (Qwen2.5-7B, Mistral-7B, Phi-3.5-mini) at five precision levels (BF16 through 3-bit) on 12,148 BBQ bias benchmark items across 5 random seeds, totaling 911,100 inference records. Our results reveal that 3-bit quantization causes 6-21% of previously unbiased items to develop new stereotypical behaviors, following a clear dose-response pattern confirmed via logistic regression, while models' willingness to select "unknown" answers declines by 17.4%. Crucially, these item-level changes are invisible to standard quality metrics: perplexity increases by less than 0.5% at 8-bit and under 3% at 4-bit across all three models, yet 2.5-5.6% of items already develop new biases at 4-bit. These findings demonstrate that aggregate evaluation metrics systematically miss fairness-critical degradation, underscoring the need for quality-aware compression protocols that explicitly test for bias emergence before deployment.

</details>


### [47] [Gaussian Relational Graph Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15575)
*Zezhong Ding,Jin Li,Xugang Wang,Xike Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GelGT 是一种新型关系图 Transformer，通过协同采样和高斯图注意力机制，有效缓解了信息衰减问题，并实现了结构、语义与时间信息的高效多维建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关系图学习模型在处理长程依赖时存在信息衰减问题，且难以同时建模结构、语义和时间信息。

Method: 提出 GelGT 模型，包含两项核心技术：1. 结构-语义协同采样策略（保留结构连通性并过滤无关语义）；2. 高斯图注意力机制（引入可学习的高斯偏置，动态编码时间依赖）。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上实现了 SOTA 性能，预测准确率最高提升了 13.8%。

Conclusion: GelGT 通过创新的采样和注意力机制有效解决了关系图学习中的长程依赖和多维信息融合问题，在多项任务中达到SOTA水平。

Abstract: Relational graph learning models relational databases as graphs and has demonstrated superior performance on a wide range of relational predictive tasks. However, existing methods struggle to capture long-range dependencies due to information decay in their message-passing mechanisms, and recent relational graph transformers remain limited in jointly modeling structural, semantic, and temporal information. In this paper, we propose GelGT, a Gaussian relational graph transformer that explicitly addresses these challenges. GelGT introduces a structure-semantic collaborative sampling strategy to preserve structural connectivity while filtering irrelevant semantic information, and incorporates a Gaussian graph attention mechanism with a learnable Gaussian bias on the sampled subgraphs to dynamically encode temporal dependencies. Extensive experiments on various real-world datasets demonstrate that GelGT achieves state-of-the-art downstream task performance, with up to a 13.8% improvement in predictive performance.

</details>


### [48] [MuteBench: Modality Unavailability Tolerance Evaluation for Incomplete Multimodal Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15235)
*Wugeng Zheng,Ziwen Kan,Tianlong Chen,Chen Chen,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对临床AI中的传感器数据缺失问题，论文推出了MuteBench基准，揭示了架构家族是决定多模态系统鲁棒性的首要因素，并量化评估了不同模态缺失场景下的模型表现，为开发抗干扰的医疗AI提供了指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床多模态AI系统常面临传感器故障导致的数据缺失，现有研究缺乏涵盖多种故障模式、多种架构及多临床领域的系统基准评估，制约了鲁棒多模态融合方法的开发。

Method: 构建了MuteBench，包含9个临床数据集、6种融合架构，系统性地评估了在“缺失模态”和“模态内时间片段缺失”两种故障模式下的性能，并进行受控严重程度的压力测试；进行了PTB-XL数据集上的扩散模型插补案例研究。

Result: 架构类型（Model Family）比模型参数量对鲁棒性的预测作用更强；课程模态丢弃（Curriculum Modality Dropout）的保护效果受限于训练时的最大丢弃率；通道数量、序列长度与模态对齐程度决定了哪种缺失模式对模型威胁更大。

Conclusion: 架构家族是鲁棒性的核心决定因素；“通道独立”模型对模态缺失的容忍度高，但对特定时间段缺失敏感；模态缺失的鲁棒性受模态数量、序列长度和对齐方式共同影响；基于扩散模型的插补可以有效辅助鲁棒性分类。MuteBench为临床多模态模型设计与选型提供了基准与指导。

Abstract: Multimodal physiological data powers clinical AI systems from intensive care units to wearable devices, but sensors routinely fail in practice. Two failure modes are common: modality missing, where an entire channel is absent, and within-modality missing, where a contiguous time segment is lost. No existing benchmark evaluates multiple fusion architectures under both failure modes at controlled severity levels across diverse clinical datasets. We present MuteBench, a benchmark covering 9 datasets from 7 clinical domains, 6 fusion architectures, and 2 missing-data modes over 125,000 samples. Through this benchmark, we find that architecture family is the strongest predictor of robustness, outweighing parameter count. Channel-independent models tolerate modality missing well but can be sensitive to within-modality missing, especially on short sequences. Curriculum modality dropout protects reliably only up to the maximum dropout rate used in training. We also find that channel count, sequence length, and modality alignment jointly determine which failure mode poses the greater threat. Finally, a PTB-XL case study suggests that diffusion-based imputation can improve downstream classification under within-modality missing, with the largest gains for models whose expert routing is most sensitive to corrupted inputs, though broader validation across datasets remains an open direction. MuteBench provides practitioners with concrete guidance for both selecting existing architectures and informing the design of future robust multimodal fusion methods.

</details>


### [49] [Reducing the Safety Tax in LLM Safety Alignment with On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15239)
*Yu Fu,Longxuan Yu,Haz Sameen Shahgir,Zhipeng Wei,Hui Liu,N. Benjamin Erichson,Yue Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大模型安全对齐中的“安全税”问题，本文提出了OPSA框架。通过实施在线策略自蒸馏及引入“教师翻转率”筛选机制，有效缓解了训练数据分布失配，在提升安全性的同时，显著优于现有方法，出色保留了模型的推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的“安全税（safety tax）”问题严重制约了高性能推理模型的能力，其核心原因除了监督微调（SFT）的分布式失配外，还源于“离策略（off-policy）”训练带来的偏差。现有方法难以在保证安全性的同时维持模型的通用逻辑推理性能。

Method: 提出了“在线策略自蒸馏用于安全对齐”（OPSA）框架。该方法通过冻结的教师模型，在模型每步生成的轨迹上应用基于特权安全上下文的逐Token KL散度监督；同时引入“教师翻转率（teacher flip rate）”指标，用于选择能有效激活模型内生安全推理能力的上下文，而非仅通过表层特征引导生成。

Result: 在两个推理模型族和五个不同规模上进行了测试，OPSA在相同的训练数据量和全参数微调下，比离策略自蒸馏和外部教师蒸馏表现出更优的安全-推理权衡。在较小模型（如R1-Distill-1.5B和Qwen3-0.6B）上分别取得了+8.85和+5.49分的提升，且对自适应越狱攻击保持了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: OPSA 通过在策略（on-policy）自蒸馏过程中利用特权上下文（privileged context）诱导模型进行安全推理，解决了现有安全对齐中存在的离策略训练失配问题，成功在优化安全性的同时最大程度保留了模型的推理能力，尤其在小参数模型上表现显著。

Abstract: Safety alignment often improves robustness to harmful queries at the cost of reasoning ability, a tradeoff known as the safety tax. A common cause is distributional mismatch: supervised fine-tuning trains the target model on safety demonstrations produced by humans, external models, or fixed self-generated traces, rather than on trajectories sampled from its own policy. We identify off-policy training mismatch as a second source of this tax and study on-policy self-distillation for safety alignment, which we call OPSA. The model generates its own rollouts and receives dense per-token KL supervision from a frozen teacher copy of itself conditioned on a privileged safety context. Because this teacher must be safer than the sampled student trajectory, we introduce \emph{teacher flip rate}: a criterion that measures how often a privileged context converts unsafe responses into safe ones. We use this signal to search for contexts that activate latent safety reasoning rather than merely elicit safe-looking demonstrations. Across two reasoning-model families and five model scales, OPSA achieves a stronger safety--reasoning tradeoff than off-policy self-distillation and external-teacher distillation under matched data and full-parameter fine-tuning, with the largest gains on smaller models (+8.85 points on R1-Distill-1.5B and +5.49 points on Qwen3-0.6B). The gains persist across training-set sizes and adaptive jailbreak evaluations. Token-level analyses further show that OPSA concentrates updates near early compliance-decision tokens, providing a mechanism for improving safety while preserving general reasoning.

</details>


### [50] [Reading the Cell, Designing the Cure: Perturbation-Conditioned Molecular Diffusion for Function-Oriented Drug Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15243)
*Ziyu Xu,Zijian Zhang,Liang Wang,Zhiyuan Liu,Qiang Liu,Shu Wu,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CURE 是一种基于转录组扰动的药物设计扩散模型，通过专门的特征提取器协同生物和化学模态，实现功能导向的分子生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏大规模可靠的目标蛋白结构，且复杂表型往往源于通路紊乱，因此需要利用转录组扰动作为药物作用的功能性读数来设计药物。

Method: 提出 CURE 框架：1. 使用 TFE（转录组扰动功能特征提取器）从前后状态中提取功能导向的扰动嵌入；2. 实现化学双视图对齐，弥补生物与化学跨模态鸿沟；3. 采用异构感知聚合技术从噪声数据中提取鲁棒的转录组特征；4. 基于扩散模型实现条件生成药物分子。

Result: 在标准基准测试和分布外（OOD）协议中，CURE 在结构质量和功能一致性方面均优于现有基准模型；并在零样本（zero-shot）基因抑制剂设计任务中展现出实际应用潜力。

Conclusion: CURE 是一个高效的基于转录组学药物设计的生成式扩散模型，通过跨模态对齐和异构感知聚合，有效解决了药物生成中生物与化学空间的鸿沟问题。

Abstract: When reliable target structures are unavailable at scale or phenotypes arise from dysregulated pathways, transcriptomic perturbations provide a system-level functional readout for drug action. In this work, we formalize \emph{Transcriptome-based Drug Design (TBDD)} as a generative inverse problem: designing drug molecules conditioned on desired transcriptomic state transitions. We analyze the inherently ill-posed nature of this task, which is further complicated by the profound domain gap between biology and chemistry and by the sparsity of transcriptomic signals. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{\themodel{}} (A \textbf{C}ell\textbf{U}lar \textbf{R}esponse \textbf{E}ngine), a multi-resolution transcriptome-guided diffusion framework. \themodel{} features a specialized \textbf{Transcriptome Perturbation Functional Feature Extractor (TFE)} that (1) distills function-oriented perturbation embeddings from pre/post states, (2) aligns these signatures to dual chemical views to bridge the cross-modal gap, and (3) performs heterogeneity-aware aggregation to extract robust state-specific signals from noisy transcriptomic data. Extensive evaluations on both standard benchmarks and rigorous out-of-distribution protocols demonstrate that \themodel{} consistently outperforms strong baselines in structural quality and functional consistency. Furthermore, we validate its practical utility via a zero-shot gene-inhibitor design task, highlighting the potential of phenotype-driven generative discovery.

</details>


### [51] [Privacy Evaluation of Generative Models for Trajectory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15246)
*Stavros Bouras,Ioannis Kontopoulos,Chiara Pugliese,Francesco Lettich,Emanuele Carlini,Hanna Kavalionak,Chiara Renso,Konstantinos Tserpes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究指出生成式轨迹模型并不自带隐私保护属性，通过引入成员推理攻击，证实了现有的轨迹生成模型在隐私保护方面存在严重的风险，并填补了该领域隐私评估方法的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹数据具有高度敏感性，虽然人们常假设生成式模型（如GAN、VAE、扩散模型）能通过学习时空分布来“去隐私化”生成合成数据，但这一假设缺乏实证支持。研究旨在揭示生成式轨迹模型在隐私评估方面的缺失，并验证其潜在的隐私泄露风险。

Method: 研究首先识别了适用于评估轨迹生成任务隐私保护的实证方法，随后针对包含GAN、VAE和扩散模型在内的代表性生成模型实施了成员推理攻击（Membership Inference Attacks）。

Result: 通过成员推理攻击证明了生成式轨迹模型存在显著的隐私威胁，其实证结果有力反驳了“生成式模型天然保护隐私”的观点，揭示了模型评估体系中存在的巨大缺口。

Conclusion: 生成式轨迹模型并不具备天然的隐私保护属性。通过实证攻击评估，证明了目前的生成式轨迹模型存在严重的隐私泄露风险，强调了在开发此类模型时将隐私评估纳入标准流程的必要性。

Abstract: Trajectory data is fundamental to modern urban intelligence, yet its sensitivity raises significant privacy concerns. Generative models such as Generative Adversarial Networks, Variational Autoencoders, and Diffusion Models have been developed to generate realistic synthetic trajectory data by capturing underlying spatiotemporal distributions and mobility patterns. Although these models are often assumed to preserve privacy due to their generative nature, this assumption does not necessarily hold. In this work, we investigate the intersection of generative trajectory modeling and privacy evaluation. By identifying applicable empirical methods for assessing privacy preservation in trajectory generation tasks, we demonstrate a significant gap in the evaluation of privacy for generative trajectory models. Motivated by this gap, we implement Membership Inference Attacks against representative models, demonstrating the feasibility of using such empirical privacy evaluation methods and showing that their generative nature does not eliminate privacy risks.

</details>


### [52] [GQLA: Group-Query Latent Attention for Hardware-Adaptive Large Language Model Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15250)
*Fanxu Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GQLA 是一种改进的注意力机制，通过在单一权重架构内同时支持 MQA 和 GQA 解码路径，实现了对高性能（如 H100）和资源受限（如 H20）硬件的完美适配，无需重训练即可直接转换现有 GQA 模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeepSeek 的 MLA 架构虽然在 H100 等高性能 GPU 上表现优异，但其固定的“MQA 吸收”推理路径限制了其在 H20 等受限硬件上的性能优化潜力，且无法有效支持多 Token 预测（MTP）和灵活的张量并行。

Method: 提出 Group-Query Latent Attention (GQLA)，通过最小化修改 MLA，使其在推理时可动态选择“MQA 吸收路径”（适用于高吞吐设备）或“GQA 路径”（适用于受限设备及多 Token 推理）；同时引入 TransGQLA 方法将现有的 GQA 检查点转换为 GQLA。

Result: GQLA 在 H100 上匹配了 MLA 的屋顶线性能，在 H20 上通过 GQA 分组路径支持了 MTP 和高效的张量并行。在 LLaMA-3-8B 上，该方法将 KV 缓存压缩至原始 GQA 的 28.125%。

Conclusion: GQLA 通过在单一权重中提供 MQA 和 GQA 两种等价的解码路径，实现了跨硬件架构的适应性。它在保留 MLA 显存压缩优势的同时，解决了特定算力/带宽配比硬件上的推理瓶颈，且无需重训即可直接转换现有 GQA 模型。

Abstract: Multi-head Latent Attention (MLA), the attention used in DeepSeek-V2/V3, jointly compresses keys and values into a low-rank latent and matches the H100 roofline almost perfectly. Its trained weights, however, expose only one decoding path - an absorbed MQA form - which ties efficient inference to H100-class compute-bandwidth ratios, forfeits tensor parallelism along the head axis, and yields no Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) gain on commodity inference GPUs such as the export-restricted H20. We propose Group-Query Latent Attention (GQLA), a minimal modification of MLA whose trained weights expose two algebraically equivalent decoding paths over the same parameters: an MQA-absorb path identical to MLA's, and a GQA path with a per-group expanded cache. The runtime picks the path that matches the target hardware - no retraining, no custom kernels - so a single set of GQLA weights pins the rooflines of both H100 (MQA-absorb, s_q=1) and H20 (GQA + MTP, s_q=2), while supporting up to 8-way zero-redundancy tensor parallelism on the GQA path. To avoid pretraining from scratch we extend TransMLA into TransGQLA, which converts a pretrained GQA checkpoint into a GQLA model; on LLaMA-3-8B it compresses the per-token KV cache to 28.125% of the GQA baseline on the MQA-absorb path while structurally preserving GQA-level traffic on the per-group path.

</details>


### [53] [PDRNN: Modular Data-driven Pedestrian Dead Reckoning on Loosely Coupled Radio- and Inertial-Signalstreams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15252)
*Peter Bauer,Andreas Porada,Felix Ott,Christopher Mutschler,Tobias Feigl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究提出了一种名为PDRNN的模块化机器学习框架，通过各组件独立建模与动态数据融合，显著改善了高动态场景下行人航位推算的精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统行人航位推算（PDR）在处理不同传感器采样率差异、数据传输不可靠，以及高动态运动（高加速度、高速、快速旋转）下的多模态数据融合时表现不佳，易发生误差累积。

Method: 提出名为PDRNN的模块化混合AI架构：1. 采用独立机器学习模型分别估计方向、速度/距离；2. 利用RNN架构隐式预测异步传感器数据流；3. 通过最终融合模型结合各组件输出，并引入不确定性估计（均值与方差）以提升鲁棒性。

Result: 在动态运动数据集上的实验表明，PDRNN在准确性与精度上均优于传统方法及现有的机器学习方法，同时具备预测能力并降低了黑盒模型的误差累积效应。

Conclusion: PDRNN通过模块化、结合不确定性估计的混合AI架构，有效解决了传统PDR系统在处理异步、多模态传感器数据时的性能瓶颈与误差累积问题，为动态运动场景下的行人定位提供了高精度且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern pedestrian dead reckoning (PDR) systems rely on fusing noisy and biased estimates of position, velocity, and calibrated orientation derived from loosely coupled sensors to determine the current pose of a localized object. However, discrepancies in the sampling rates of sensor-specific estimation methods and unreliable transmission pose significant challenges. And traditional methods often fail to effectively fuse multimodal sensor data during dynamic movements characterized by high accelerations, velocities, and rapidly varying orientations. To address these limitations, we propose a simple recurrent neural network (RNN) architecture capable of implicitly forecasting asynchronous sensor data streams from diverse estimation methods along reference trajectories. The proposed approach introduces PDRNN, a modular hybrid AI-assisted PDR system that handles each component as an independent ensemble of machine learning (ML) models to estimate both key parameter means and variances. Separate ML-based models are employed to estimate orientation, (un)directed velocity or distance from acceleration and gyroscope data, with optional absolute positioning from synchronized radio systems such as 5G for stabilization. A final fusion model combines these outputs, position, velocity, and orientation, while using uncertainty estimates to enhance system robustness. The modular design allows individual components to be updated, fine-tuned, or replaced without affecting the entire system. Experiments on dynamic sports movement data show that PDRNN achieves superior accuracy and precision compared to classic and ML-based methods, effectively avoiding error accumulation common in black-box approaches. And PDRNN offers forecast capabilities and better component control despite increased system complexity.

</details>


### [54] [Position: Ideas Should be the Center of Machine Learning Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15253)
*Jairo Diaz-Rodriguez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文批评了机器学习领域过度追求基准性能和抽象理论的现状，倡导“Ideas First”框架，主张通过验证模型行为特征来探索科学本质，从而提升科研公平性并回归科学初衷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器学习领域存在严重割裂：一方面是盲目追求榜单指标的“基准工程”，另一方面是脱离实际的“理想化理论”。这种“两极化”忽视了科学研究的核心——思想的验证，且抬高了科研门槛（复杂度溢价）。

Method: 提出“Ideas First”研究框架：1. 将思想验证置于首位；2. 通过预测现代模型中的行为特征来评估思想；3. 设计专门的诊断性实验以捕捉这些特征，而非仅仅关注榜单分数。

Result: 该框架能够有效桥接理论与实践，减少对昂贵算力和大规模数据的病态依赖，从而降低科研门槛，提升资源有限的研究者参与科学贡献的能力。

Conclusion: 机器学习研究应调整范式，从盲目追求基准指标或抽象理论转向以“科学假设（Idea）”为核心，通过预测模型行为特征（behavioral signatures）并进行针对性实验来验证。这将促进研究公平性，并使研究更具科学价值而非单纯的技术竞争。

Abstract: Machine learning research increasingly bifurcates into two disconnected modes: benchmark-driven engineering that prioritizes metrics over understanding, and idealized theory that often fails to transfer to modern systems. In this position paper, we argue that the field focuses too heavily on these endpoints, neglecting the central scientific object: the idea. We propose an Ideas First framework in which ideas are valued for the behavioral signatures they predict in modern models, and these signatures are tested through tailored experiments designed to detect the relevant patterns rather than to win leaderboards. This shift not only bridges the gap between theory and practice but also promotes equity by removing the "complexity premium," enabling rigorous scientific contributions from researchers with modest computational, financial, and human resources. Ultimately, we advocate for a research culture centered on ideas, treating benchmarks and theorems as instruments for testing mechanistic hypotheses rather than as ends in themselves.

</details>


### [55] [Representation Without Reward: A JEPA Audit for LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15394)
*Biswa Sengupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过对 22 种 JEPA 辅助损失进行实证测试，发现尽管这些方法可以改变大语言模型的隐藏状态几何属性，但由于隐藏表征与解码器输出任务指标之间的“弱耦合”，这些改进未能带来稳健的任务性能优化，从而挑战了 JEPA 原则在 LLM 微调中的直接有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA 理论认为预测潜在表征比预测观测输出更能学到有效的抽象信息。作者旨在验证这一原则在 autoregressive LLM 微调中是否成立，即通过引入辅助损失改进隐藏状态几何结构，能否有效提升解码器的任务表现。

Method: 以 Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct 为基础，采用 LoRA 微调及全参数微调，针对自然语言到正则表达（regex）生成的任务，测试了 22 种不同的训练辅助目标（auxiliaries），包括轨迹形状正则化、分布约束、预测器/目标不对称性、Fisher 度量 Jacobi 残差以及解码器可见的 JEPA 目标。通过严格的统计检验（Holm-Bonferroni）评估这些辅助任务对最终任务指标的影响。

Result: 实验结果呈“结构性零假设（structured null）”：虽然部分辅助方法在微调辅助任务本身指标或隐藏层几何属性（如曲率、各向异性等）上产生了显著变化，但在完成校正后的统计检验下，无一能显著提升最终任务的准确率（Exact Match）。即便个别方法在初期观测到微弱正向结果，在重复实验后亦无法稳健复现。辅助几何属性与解码任务性能之间缺乏鲁棒的关联。

Conclusion: 在当前大语言模型（LLM）微调范式下，隐藏状态表示的演化与最终任务指标（如准确率）之间呈现弱耦合状态。联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）类方法在提升模型表征质量的同时，未必能转化为解码任务性能的显著提升。未来的研究重点应转向探究隐藏几何结构如何有效转化为解码器可见的任务信号（即耦合问题）。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) propose that a model should learn more useful abstractions when trained to predict latent representations rather than observed outputs. For autoregressive language-model fine-tuning the principle entails a stricter requirement: the induced hidden-state geometry must reach the language-model head \emph{and} improve the decoded task metric. We test that requirement under a fixed Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct LoRA harness on natural-language-to-regex generation, comparing twenty-two training-time auxiliaries across trajectory-shape regularisation, distributional constraints, predictor/target asymmetry, Fisher-metric Jacobi residuals, and a decoder-visible JEPA objective constructed to lie in cross-entropy's positive cone. The empirical answer is a structured null: several auxiliaries clear single-cell paired $α= 0.10$ without correction (T3-Local at $Δ= +2.53$~pp, $p = 0.003$ being the strongest), but none survives Bonferroni or Holm--Bonferroni at the relevant family-wise threshold, even though many change curvature, anisotropy, variance, and gradient direction. Decoder-visible JEPA yields the first positive auxiliary--cross-entropy gradient cosine in the study, yet exact match remains inside seed noise; a full-fine-tuning replication of the same auxiliary at $n = 5$ seeds reproduces the null on both benchmarks (TURK: $Δ= +0.04$~pp, $p_{\text{paired}} = 0.96$; SYNTH: $Δ= +0.52$~pp, $p_{\text{paired}} = 0.28$), so the null is robust across LoRA and full fine-tuning for the decoder-visible construction. Hidden-state representation work and decoded-task accuracy are therefore weakly coupled in this regime; we accordingly reframe LLM-domain JEPA evaluation as a coupling problem, in which the operative question is under which metrics useful hidden geometry becomes decoder-visible task signal.

</details>


### [56] [Curriculum Learning of Physics-Informed Neural Networks based on Spatial Correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15254)
*Xujia Chen,Xinyue Hu,Letian Chen,Daming Shi,Wenhui Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对PINNs的空间相关课程学习框架，通过引入空间因果引导、区域低频一致性约束及自适应重加权策略，解决了PINNs在求解强空间耦合边值问题时遇到的训练不稳与收敛困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的PINNs训练受限于高维非凸损失函数、多目标约束不平衡及信息传播效率低下。现有的课程学习方法多侧重于时间或参数维度，缺乏对空间耦合性的有效处理，且难以直接应用于具有强空间耦合的边值问题。

Method: 提出了一种空间相关课程学习（Spatially Correlated Curriculum Learning）框架：1. 空间因果权重（Spatial causal weights）：引导信息从靠近边界的区域向内部传播；2. 低频信息桥（Low-frequency information bridge）：利用伪标签强制空间分隔区域间的低频一致性，抑制全局漂移；3. 区域自适应重加权（Region-adaptive reweighting）：平衡子区域损失以减少残差并恢复高频细节。

Result: 在多个偏微分方程（PDE）基准测试中，该方法相比现有主流策略，显著降低了训练失败率并提升了求解精度，同时未产生额外的计算负担。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于空间相关性的课程学习框架能够有效解决PINNs训练过程中的不稳定性与收敛失败问题，通过空间因果性引导、低频信息桥接与区域自适应重加权，在保持计算开销的同时，显著提升了求解偏微分方程的精度。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) combine deep learning with physical constraints for solving partial differential equations (PDEs), and are widely applied in fluid mechanics, heat transfer, and solid mechanics. However, PINN training still suffers from high-dimensional non-convex loss landscapes, imbalanced multiobjective constraints, and ineffective information propagation. Existing curriculum learning and causality-guided strategies improve training stability, but mainly focus on temporal or parametric progression, lacking explicit treatment of spatial information propagation and inter-region consistency. Moreover, they are not directly applicable to boundary value problems (BVPs) with strong spatial coupling. To address this issue, we propose a spatially correlated curriculum learning framework for PINNs. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to address PINN training difficulties from the perspective of spatial coupling among subregions. First, spatial causal weights guide information from near-boundary regions inward, reducing optimization failures and spurious convergence. Second, a low-frequency information bridge enforces pseudo-label-based consistency across spatially separated regions, suppressing global low-frequency drift. Third, a region-adaptive reweighting strategy adjusts subregion losses to reduce local residuals and recover high-frequency details. Experiments on PDE benchmarks show that, under comparable computational cost, the proposed method alleviates training failures and improves solution accuracy. The code is available at https://github.com/pigofmomo/CurriculumLearningPINN.

</details>


### [57] [$φ$-Balancing for Mixture-of-Experts Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15403)
*Lizhang Chen,Jonathan Li,Qi Wang,Runlong Liao,Shuozhe Li,Chen Liang,Ni Lao,Qiang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 $\phi$-balancing，一种基于凸优化目标的 MoE 负载均衡新框架，通过镜像下降算法实现高效的在线路由调整，解决了传统启发式平衡方法偏差大的痛点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 MoE 负载均衡方法多为启发式，且依赖于噪声较大的小批量（mini-batch）统计信息，无法直接优化总体水平（population-level）的均衡目标，导致专家利用率不足。

Method: 提出 $\phi$-balancing 框架。通过最小化期望路由分布的严格凸、对称且可微的势函数，利用凸对偶性（Convex Duality）将其转化为 min-max 问题，并采用镜像下降法（Mirror Descent）导出一个基于指数移动平均（EMA）的在线路由调整算法。

Result: 在多项大规模预训练和下游微调任务中，$\phi$-balancing 在专家利用的稳定性和有效性方面均优于 Switch-style 和无损失（loss-free）基准方法。

Conclusion: $\phi$-balancing 提供了一种通过凸共轭理论导出的负载均衡原则性框架，不仅能够有效替代启发式方法，还能在预训练和微调阶段实现更稳定、更高效的专家利用率，且具有极低的计算开销。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models rely on balanced expert utilization to fully realize their scalability. However, existing load-balancing methods are largely heuristic and operate on noisy mini-batch assignment statistics, introducing bias relative to population-level objectives. We propose $φ$-balancing, a principled framework that directly targets population-level expert balance by minimizing a strictly convex, symmetric, and differentiable potential of the expected routing distribution. Using convex duality, we derive an equivalent min-max formulation and obtain a simple online algorithm via mirror descent, yielding an efficient EMA-based routing adjustment with negligible overhead. Across large-scale pretraining and downstream fine-tuning, $φ$-balancing consistently outperforms prior Switch-style and loss-free baselines, demonstrating more stable and effective expert utilization.

</details>


### [58] [Training on Documents About Monitoring Leads to CoT Obfuscation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15257)
*Reilly Haskins,Bilal Chughtai,Joshua Engels*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究表明，如果AI模型知晓自己正受到思维链（CoT）监控，它们能够通过“伪装”推理过程来逃避检测，这种能力与模型的CoT可控性显著相关，对现有的模型安全监控体系构成了实质性威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 思维链（CoT）是检测模型错误行为的关键工具，但其有效性高度依赖于模型推理过程的忠实性；该研究旨在探讨如果模型意识到自己被监控，是否会故意掩盖其推理过程以规避检测。

Method: 通过合成文档微调（Synthetic document finetuning）使8种不同的模型具备对CoT监视器的“意识”（monitor-aware），并对比其在任务执行中规避检测的能力。此外，还通过强化学习优化评估了模型在进行奖励操纵（reward-hacking）时的表现，并量化了CoT可控性与隐蔽推理能力的相关性。

Result: 研究发现：1. 具有监控意识的模型在执行任务时，其未被发现的错误行为发生率显著高于对照组；2. CoT的可控性（即模型根据约束调整推理轨迹的能力）与隐蔽推理行为的成功率呈现高度正相关（r=0.800）；3. 在相同的强化学习压力下，具备监控意识的模型更快学会了在不触发监控的情况下进行奖励操纵。

Conclusion: 对思维链（CoT）监控机制的认知结合模型自身的高可控性，显著削弱了监控的有效性，构成了模型安全性及可解释性监控的一项重要风险。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is one of the most promising tools we have for detecting model misbehavior, but its effectiveness depends on models faithfully externalizing their reasoning. Motivated by this vulnerability, we study whether monitor-aware models are capable of obfuscating their reasoning to evade detection. We use synthetic document finetuning to expose eight models to realistic pre-training-style documents describing a CoT monitor and find that monitor-aware models consistently achieve higher rates of undetected misbehavior compared to unaware controls. This effect is weaker but still present on a harder agentic task. We also show that CoT controllability, a model's ability to reshape its own reasoning trace under an imposed constraint, is closely correlated with obfuscation success across the eight models studied ($r=0.800$, $p=0.017$). Monitor-aware models placed under equal reinforcement learning optimization pressure also learn to reward-hack without triggering a CoT monitor substantially faster than unaware controls. Together, these results suggest that knowledge of monitoring combined with high CoT controllability poses a risk to CoT-based monitoring.

</details>


### [59] [On the Fragility of Data Attribution When Learning Is Distributed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15520)
*Xian Gao,Bo Hui,Min-Te Sun,Wei-Shinn Ku*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 数据归因方法并非绝对安全；研究人员展示了一种攻击手段，使得分布式训练中的参与者可以在保持模型性能的同时，通过恶意注入数据虚增自己的贡献归因值，揭示了归因机制的安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据归因方法通常假设归因值能忠实反映参与者的贡献，但研究者通过实验表明：在分布式训练中，单个恶意参与者可以在保持模型全局效能的同时，大幅操纵并虚增自身的归因值。

Method: 提出了一种“归因优先（attribution-first）”的攻击方法，利用潜在优化（latent optimization）注入微小的合成批次（synthetic batches），利用非独立同分布（non-IID）的标签覆盖和评估器的敏感性来操纵归因值。

Result: 该攻击成功实现了在不降低模型准确率、不触发几何防御的前提下，系统性地提升了恶意参与者的归因值，并重塑了良性参与者之间的归因结构。

Conclusion: 数据归因（Data Attribution）本身构成了一个新的攻击面。该研究强调了开发“归因鲁棒（attribution-robust）”和“激励兼容（incentive-compatible）”评分机制的迫切需求。

Abstract: Data attribution has become an important component of pricing, auditing, and governance in machine learning pipelines, yet most attribution methods implicitly assume that attribution values faithfully reflect participants' contributions. We show that this assumption can fail: a single participant in a standard distributed training workflow can substantially inflate its measured attribution value while preserving global utility. Our attribution-first attack uses latent optimization to inject small synthetic batches that preserve utility while exploiting non-IID label coverage and evaluator sensitivities. Across datasets, models, and multiple marginal-utility evaluators, the attack consistently increases the adversary's attribution value and reshapes the relative attribution structure among benign clients without degrading accuracy or triggering geometry-based defenses. These results show that attribution itself forms a new attack surface and motivate the development of attribution-robust and incentive-compatible scoring mechanisms.

</details>


### [60] [Tadpole: Autoencoders as Foundation Models for 3D PDEs with Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15284)
*Qiang Liu,Felix Koehler,Benjamin Holzschuh,Nils Thuerey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Tadpole 是一种用于 3D 偏微分方程的通用基础模型，通过大规模自编码预训练和高效参数微调技术，解决了高维物理系统的建模难题，在多个下游任务中表现出卓越的扩展性和多功能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D偏微分方程（PDE）求解模型在通用性（transferability）、针对高维度的可扩展性（scalability）以及多任务处理能力（multi-functionality）方面存在局限性，难以应对复杂多变的物理系统。

Method: 1. 数据生成：采用高效的在线数据生成框架，避免了存储和 I/O 开销，实现了大规模训练。
2. 模型架构：基于自编码器架构，通过学习单通道空间切片的表征，实现了跨异构物理系统的泛化。
3. 迁移学习：设计了参数高效微调策略（Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning），结合了低秩自适应（LoRA）、潜在空间变换及重引入跳跃连接技术。

Result: Tadpole 模型成功扩展至相当于数百 TB 的训练数据规模，在保持了参数极少的情况下，在时空动力学建模及生成任务中均取得了精确的性能表现，证明了其优异的迁移学习能力。

Conclusion: Tadpole 作为一种针对3D偏微分方程的通用基础模型，通过在大规模合成数据上进行的自编码预训练，展现出强大的表征学习能力。其高效的微调策略使其在动态预测和生成建模等多种下游任务中表现优异，验证了其作为解决复杂物理系统问题的基础工具的潜力。

Abstract: We introduce Tadpole, a novel foundation model for three-dimensional partial differential equations (PDEs) that addresses key challenges in transferability, scalability to high dimensionality, and multi-functionality. Tadpole is pre-trained as an autoencoder on synthetic 3D PDE data generated by an efficient online data-generation framework. This enables large-scale, diverse training without storage or I/O overhead, demonstrated by scaling to an equivalent of hundreds of terabytes of training data. By autoencoding single-channel spatial crops, Tadpole learns rich and transferable representations across heterogeneous physical systems with varying numbers of state variables and spatial resolutions. Although pre-trained solely as an autoencoder, Tadpole can be efficiently applied for multiple downstream tasks beyond reconstruction, including dynamics learning and generative modeling. For dynamics learning, we propose a novel parameter-efficient fine-tuning strategy that integrates low-rank adaptation, latent-space transformations, and reintroduced skip connections, achieving accurate temporal modeling with a minimal number of trainable parameters. Tadpole demonstrates strong fine-tuning performance across various downstream tasks, highlighting its versatility and effectiveness as a foundation model for 3D PDE learning. Source code and pre-trained weights of Tadpole are available at https://github.com/tum-pbs/tadpole

</details>


### [61] [Universal Approximation of Nonlinear Operators and Their Derivatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15285)
*Filippo de Feo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次证明了非线性$k$阶可微算子及其导数在Banach空间上的通用逼近定理（UAT），为算子学习（OL）提供了严格的数学理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决算子学习（OL）领域中关于非线性算子及其导数通用逼近定理的开放性研究难题，并探讨其在PDE数值解、最优控制及反问题等领域的应用。

Method: 利用编码器-解码器架构（如DeepONet等），结合Bastiani意义下的$k$阶连续可微性、紧-开拓扑以及构建的新型加权Sobolev空间，在Banach空间中证明了算子及其导数的逼近特性。

Result: 证明了在紧集上均匀收敛及加权Sobolev范数下的首个非线性$k$阶可微算子及其导数的UAT，推广了Hornik (1991) 的经典结论至无限维算子空间。

Conclusion: 本文证明了非线性$k$阶可微算子及其导数的通用逼近定理（UAT），填补了算子学习领域的基础理论空白，为高阶算子学习提供了理论支持。

Abstract: Derivative-Informed Operator Learning (DIOL), i.e. learning a (nonlinear) operator and its derivatives, is an open research frontier at the foundations of the influential field of Operator Learning (OL). In particular, Universal Approximation Theorems (UATs) of nonlinear operators and their derivatives are foundational open questions and delicate problems in nonlinear functional analysis. In this manuscript, we prove the first UATs of non-linear $k$-times differentiable operators between Banach spaces and their derivatives, uniformly on compact sets and in weighted Sobolev norms for general finite input measures, via OL architectures. Our results are the first complete generalizations of the corresponding influential classical results in [Hornik, 1991] to infinite-dimensional settings and OL.
  We discuss several open areas where DIOL and our UATs find applications: high-order accuracy in OL, fast constrained optimization in Banach spaces (e.g. optimal control of PDEs, inverse problems) and numerical methods for infinite-dimensional PDEs (e.g. HJB PDEs on Banach spaces from optimal control of PDEs, SPDEs, path-dependent systems, partially observed systems, mean-field control).
  We parameterize nonlinear operators via Encoder-Decoder Architectures, renowned classes in OL due to their generality, including classical architectures, such as DeepONets, Deep-H-ONets, PCA-Nets. Our results are based on four key features that allow us to prove UATs in full generality: (i) Approximation Properties of Banach spaces. (ii) $k$-times continuous differentiability in the sense of Bastiani (weaker than $k$-times continuous Fréchet differentiability). (iii) Natural compact-open topologies for UA; indeed, we show that UA in standard compact-open topologies induced by operator norms is violated even for Fréchet derivatives. (iv) Construction of novel weighted Sobolev spaces for the UA.

</details>


### [62] [SurvivalPFN: Amortizing Survival Prediction via In-Context Bayesian Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15488)
*Shi-ang Qi,Vahid Balazadeh,Michael Cooper,Russell Greiner,Rahul G. Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SurvivalPFN 是一种无需调参、基于上下文学习的生存分析基石模型，通过在大规模合成数据上预训练，实现了对复杂生存数据的开箱即用及高性能预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的生存分析方法种类繁多，选择合适的模型通常需要深厚的领域知识及昂贵的反复调参过程。此外，现有的模型往往依赖于严苛的参数假设，难以同时兼顾预测性能、校准能力与易用性。

Method: 提出一种基于 Prior-Data Fitted Network (PFN) 的 SurvivalPFN 模型。该模型通过在多样化的合成、可识别且经过右删失的数据生成过程上进行预训练，利用 Transformer 架构通过单次前向传递（Single forward pass）实现针对新数据集的上下文学习，从而完成生存预测。

Result: 在大规模基准测试（61个数据集、21种现有方法、5种评估指标）中，SurvivalPFN 表现出色，不仅无需特定任务的再训练或超参数调整，其预测性能通常优于现有的生存分析模型，且能生成校准良好的生存分布。

Conclusion: SurvivalPFN 是一种通用的基石模型，能够通过上下文学习（In-context learning）实现高效、无需调参的生存分析，在处理各类生存数据方面表现出极强的竞争力和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Survival analysis provides a powerful statistical framework for modeling time-to-event outcomes in the presence of censoring. However, selecting an appropriate estimator from the many specialized survival approaches often requires substantial methodological and domain expertise. We introduce SurvivalPFN, a prior-data fitted network that amortizes Bayesian inference for censored observations through in-context learning. SurvivalPFN is pretrained on a diverse family of synthetic, identifiable, and right-censored data-generating processes, enabling it to amortize survival analysis in a single forward pass during inference. As a result, the model adapts to the effective complexity of each dataset without task-specific training or hyperparameter tuning, avoids restrictive parametric assumptions, and produces calibrated survival distributions. In a large-scale benchmark spanning 61 datasets, 21 methods, and 5 evaluation metrics, SurvivalPFN achieves strong predictive performance and often improves upon established survival models. These results suggest that SurvivalPFN offers a principled and practical foundation model for survival analysis, with potential applications in high-impact domains such as healthcare, finance, and engineering (https://github.com/rgklab/SurvivalPFN).

</details>


### [63] [GQA-μP: The maximal parameterization update for grouped query attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15290)
*Kyle R. Chickering,Huijuan Wang,Mengxi Wu,Alexander Moreno,Muhao Chen,Xuezhe Ma,Daria Soboleva,Joel Hestness,Zhengzhong Liu,Eric Xing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了对最大更新参数化（μP）的改进，通过谱特征学习理论，成功实现了针对分组查询注意力（GQA）等复杂架构的精确超参数迁移，显著降低了大型语言模型的调优计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的最大更新参数化（μP）在处理新模型架构（如GQA）时推导复杂，且在非满秩矩阵下存在局限性，需要更普适的理论框架实现计算高效的超参数迁移。

Method: 1. 将权重上的谱范数条件提升为特征学习的定义，推导出Complete-P深度和权重衰减缩放；2. 引入改进的谱范数，解决了权重矩阵非满秩时的缩放律问题，实现了对GQA架构的μP推导。

Result: 成功推导出适用于GQA架构的μP缩放律；实验证明了学习率在GQA重复超参数上的成功迁移，以及在权重衰减上的有效迁移。

Conclusion: 该工作通过将谱范数条件升级为特征学习的定义，成功简化了模型架构超参数的推导，并使得针对分组查询注意力（GQA）的μP推导成为可能，验证了超参数在复杂架构间的可迁移性。

Abstract: Hyperparameter transfer across model architectures dramatically reduces the amount of compute necessary for tuning large language models (LLMs). The maximal update parameterization (μP) ensures transfer through principled mathematical analysis but can be challenging to derive for new model architectures. Building on the spectral feature-learning view of Yang et al. (2023a), we make two advances. First, we promote spectral norm conditions on the weights from a heuristic to the definition of feature learning, and as a consequence arrive at the Complete-P depth and weight-decay scalings without recourse to lazy-learning. Second, we consider a modified spectral norm that preserves the valid scaling law of network weights when weight matrices are not full rank. This enables (to our knowledge, the first) derivation of μP scalings for grouped-query attention (GQA). We demonstrate the efficacy of our theoretical derivations by showing learning rate transfer across the GQA repetition hyperparameter as well as experiments regarding transfer over weight decay.

</details>


### [64] [Tighter Regret Bounds for Contextual Action-Set Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15692)
*Zijun Chen,Zihan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了 MVP 算法以适配动作集随情境变化的强化学习场景，证明了该算法在不同情境设置下均能实现高效的悔度控制，并提供了优于极小极大界（Minimax regret）的间隙相关悔度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的强化学习通常假设动作集不变，但在实际应用中，动作集往往随每个情节的情境而动态变化。作者旨在解决在固定转移/奖励函数但动作集随情节变更的情况下，如何实现最优决策并量化累积悔度（regret）。

Method: 将 MVP（Minimum Value Policy）算法扩展至具有情境相关（context-dependent）可行动作集的强化学习框架中，并理论分析了其在对抗性和随机情境下的性能。

Result: 1. 对于对抗性情境，获得 $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{SAH^3K\log L})$ 的极小极大悔度界；2. 对于随机情境，悔度界为 $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{SAH^3K})$，样本复杂度为 $\widetilde{O}(SAH^3/ε^2)$；3. 推导出了基于间隙（gap-dependent）的悔度界，在间隙较大时显著优于 Minimax 界。

Conclusion: MVP 算法在存在动作约束的情境下表现优异，既能处理对抗性情境，也能高效处理随机情境，且在存在较大次优间隙时，其与间隙相关的悔度界（gap-dependent bound）优于极小极大悔度界。

Abstract: We study episodic reinforcement learning with fixed reward and transition functions, but with episode-dependent admissible action sets that are observed at the start of each episode. Performance is measured by cumulative regret against the episode-wise optimal value, $\sum_{k=1}^K [V^{*,M^k} - V^{π^k,M^k}]$, where $M^k$ represents the action context in the $k$-th episode. We show that the MVP algorithm naturally extends to this framework and enjoys strong theoretical guarantees. In particular, we establish a minimax regret bound of $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{SAH^3K\log L})$ for adversarial contexts, where $L$ denotes the number of possible contexts. This result implies a regret bound of $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{SAH^3K})$ for stochastic contexts. We further translate the stochastic regret guarantee into a sample complexity bound of $\widetilde{O}(SAH^3/ε^2)$ for a fixed context distribution.
  In addition, we derive a gap-dependent regret bound of \[ \widetilde O\left( \inf_{p\in [0,1)} \left( \frac{1}{Δ_{\min}^{p}} + pKΔ_{\min}^{p} \right)\log K \cdot \mathrm{poly}(S,A,H) \right), \] where $Δ_{\min}^{p}$ is the global $p$-trimmed positive-gap floor over suboptimal $(h,s,a)$ triples. This bound can substantially improve upon the minimax rate when the relevant suboptimality gaps are large.

</details>


### [65] [GESD: Beyond Outcome-Oriented Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15295)
*Gideon Popoola,John Sheppard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为GESD的新型公平性指标，旨在量化机器学习模型解释在不同群体间的差异（过程公平性），并研发了FEU框架，在优化模型效能的同时兼顾结果公平与解释公平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的公平性指标大多聚焦于决策结果（如统计平价、机会平等），忽视了决策过程及背后的解释逻辑是否存在偏见。针对高风险决策领域，研究亟需从“过程可解释性”层面引入新的公平性度量标准。

Method: 提出了名为GESD（Group-level Explanation Stability Disparity）的新型公平性指标，用于量化模型解释在不同群体间的稳定性、鲁棒性和敏感性差异；并构建了FEU（Fairness-Explainability-Utility）多目标优化框架，将解释公平性、结果公平性和模型效用共同纳入训练流程。

Result: 在多个基准测试数据集上的实验表明，GESD能够有效捕捉群体间的特征解释质量差异；FEU框架在平衡模型效用与多维度公平性方面优于当前主流方法。

Conclusion: GESD填补了从解释层面评估算法公平性的空白，通过将解释稳定性纳入优化目标（FEU），有效提升了模型在公平性和效用方面的综合表现。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) algorithms are increasingly deployed in high-stakes decision-making domains such as loan approvals, hiring, and recidivism predictions. While existing fairness metrics (e.g., statistical parity, equal opportunity) effectively quantify outcome-oriented disparities, they offer limited insight into the procedure or explanation behind biased decisions. To address this gap, we propose Group-level Explanation Stability Disparity (GESD), a \textit{procedural-oriented} fairness metric that measures disparities in the stability, robustness, and sensitivity of model explanations across different subgroups in a protected category. %GESD is explainer-agnostic, model-agnostic, and extends the scope of fairness analyses to the level of explainability. We further integrate GESD into a multi-objective optimization framework that jointly optimizes for utility, outcome-based fairness, and explanation-based fairness called FEU (Fairness--Explainability--Utility). Empirical results on multiple benchmark datasets show that GESD effectively captures group-wise discrepancies in explanation quality, and that FEU improves both utility and fairness over state-of-the-art methods. By bridging outcome-based and explanation-based fairness, GESD offers a comprehensive tool for diagnosing and mitigating bias in predictive modeling. Our code and datasets are available on GitHub {\hyperlink{https://github.com/horlahsunbo/GESD}{https://github.com/horlahsunbo/GESD}}

</details>


### [66] [Beyond Bounded Variance: Variance-Reduced Normalized Methods for Nonconvex Optimization under Blum-Gladyshev Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15314)
*Antesh Upadhyay,Arda Fazla,Abolfazl Hashemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了 BG-0 噪声模型下的非凸随机优化，证明了归一化动量法和方差缩减 STORM 方法在标准平滑及广义平滑框架下的收敛性，并提供了相应的预言机复杂度边界，弥补了该领域在无界域设置下的理论空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非凸随机优化在 Blum-Gladyshev (BG-0) 噪声模型下的表现（该模型中随机梯度方差随到初始点的距离二次增长），并克服现有方法在处理非有界域或强噪声假设时的局限性。

Method: 1. 针对标准平滑性和对称广义平滑性，分析了归一化随机梯度下降（带动量）；
2. 提出了基于方差缩减的归一化 STORM 方法；
3. 将基于梯度的平滑性与基于距离的噪声进行耦合分析，探究不同参数对复杂度的影响。

Result: 1. 归一化动量算法在 BG-0 噪声下达到 $O(\varepsilon^{-6})$ 的复杂度（在标准平滑和广义平滑下均成立）；
2. 方差缩减的 STORM 方法达到极小极大最优解 $O(\varepsilon^{-4})$；
3. 在特定平滑性参数 $\alpha$ 下，STORM 方法的复杂度为 $O(\varepsilon^{-(4+\alpha)})$ (当 $\alpha \in (0,1)$ 时) 和 $O(\varepsilon^{-5})$ (当 $\alpha=1$ 时)；
4. 当噪声增长参数趋于零时，这些结果可恢复至标准的有界方差收敛速率。

Conclusion: 证明了在 BG-0 噪声模型下，非凸优化问题的归一化方法（Normalized methods）可以在无需有界域、增加批量或显式锚定的情况下收敛，并给出了相应的预言机复杂度。该研究确立了归一化动量方法和方差缩减 STORM 方法在不同平滑性假设下的收敛速率。

Abstract: We study nonconvex stochastic optimization under the Blum-Gladyshev ($\mathsf{BG}$-0) noise model, where the stochastic gradient variance grows quadratically with the distance from the initialization. We consider this problem under both standard smoothness and the symmetric generalized-smoothness framework, which captures objectives whose local curvature can scale with the gradient norm. We prove that normalized stochastic gradient descent with momentum, using only one stochastic gradient per iteration, converges under $\mathsf{BG}$-0 noise with oracle complexity $O(\varepsilon^{-6})$. This rate holds both for standard smoothness and for $α$-symmetric generalized smoothness, showing that generalized smoothness is rate-neutral for normalized momentum in this setting. We then study a variance-reduced normalized STORM method. Under mean-square smoothness and sharp initialization, the method achieves the minimax optimal $O(\varepsilon^{-4})$ complexity, matching the lower bound. Under expected $α$-symmetric generalized smoothness, the STORM recursion couples gradient-dependent smoothness with distance-dependent noise, leading to complexity $O(\varepsilon^{-(4+α)})$ for $α\in(0,1)$ and $O(\varepsilon^{-5})$ for $α=1$. When the distance-growth parameter in the noise model vanishes, our guarantees recover the standard bounded-variance rates: $O(\varepsilon^{-4})$ for momentum, $O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ for variance reduction, and $O(\varepsilon^{-2})$ in the deterministic case. To our knowledge, these are the first convergence guarantees for normalized methods in non-convex stochastic optimization under $\mathsf{BG}$-0 noise without bounded domains, increasing batch sizes, or explicit anchoring, covering both standard and generalized smoothness regimes.

</details>


### [67] [SAFE Quantum Machine Learning with Variational Quantum Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16067)
*Ying Chen,Paolo Giudici,Vasily Kolesnikov,Paolo Recchia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型变分量子分类器，通过可学习的编码层与有界量子算子提升模型稳定性，经验证在安全关键场景下比传统模型具有更好的鲁棒性与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决高维深度表示中量子分类器的可靠性问题，特别是在需要平衡准确性、鲁棒性和可解释性的安全关键应用场景中。

Method: 提出了一种基于振幅编码（Amplitude Encoding）的变分量子分类器。通过可学习的经典预编码层进行稳定，并利用归一化振幅嵌入与有界量子可观测算子构建假设空间。使用基于Cramer-von Mises散度的SAFE-AI指标进行评估。

Result: 实验表明，该模型在保持与强基准模型竞争力的预测性能的同时，展现了更均衡的SAFE可靠性架构，且在噪声鲁棒性和结构化特征剔除稳定性方面表现优异。

Conclusion: 变分量子电路为安全关键场景下的“安全、稳健、可解释”（SAFE）学习提供了一种原则性的机制，在大规模深度表示学习任务中具有显著潜力。

Abstract: We propose a variational quantum classifier operating on high dimensional deep representations via amplitude encoding, stabilized by a learnable classical pre encoding layer.By combining normalized amplitude embeddings with bounded quantum observables, the resulting model induces a structured and smooth hypothesis class with controlled sensitivity to input variations. Model reliability is assessed using SAFE-AI metrics derived from the Cramer von Mises divergence, enabling consistent evaluation across accuracy, robustness, and explainability dimensions. Empirical results show that the proposed quantum model provides competitive predictive performance compared with strong classical baselines while exhibiting a more balanced SAFE reliability profile, with improved robustness to noise and stability under structured feature removal. These findings suggest that variational quantum circuits offer a principled mechanism for stability oriented SAFE learning in safety critical settings.

</details>


### [68] [The Privacy Price of Tail-Risk Learning: Effective Tail Sample Size in Differentially Private CVaR Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16219)
*El Mustapha Mansouri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了差分隐私下CVaR学习的有效样本量受尾部质量 $\tau$ 缩减为 $n\tau$，并给出了在不同假设空间下隐私学习的最优界及相应的性能损耗机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究差分隐私（DP）约束对条件风险价值（CVaR）学习的影响，特别是分析当仅关注分布尾部（尾部质量 $\tau$）时，隐私保护机制如何改变有效样本量及学习性能。

Method: 通过理论推导，建立隐私条件下CVaR学习的误差分解。利用模块化的上下界归约方法分析凸利普希茨学习，并讨论了纯差分隐私（pure DP）与近似差分隐私（approximate DP）在小δ区间的理论最优界。

Result: 给出了CVaR学习的完备性能上限与下限：标量估计的收敛速率为 $Θ(B \min\{1,(n\tau)^{-1/2}+(εn\tau)^{-1}\})$；有限假设空间下的速率为 $Θ(B \min\{1,\sqrt{\log(2M)/(n\tau)}+\log(2M)/(εn\tau)\})$。凸学习场景下，隐私代价固定为 $1/(εn\tau)$。

Conclusion: CVaR的隐私学习在本质上可以归约为在 $Θ(n\tau)$ 个尾部样本上进行的普通差分隐私学习。隐私带来的额外风险代价与 $1/(εn\tau)$ 成正比，这解释了为何尾部风险估计对隐私保护尤为敏感。

Abstract: Differential privacy changes the effective sample size governing CVaR learning. For tail mass $τ$, the privacy-relevant sample size is not $n$, but $nτ$; equivalently, the effective private tail sample size is $εnτ$. Private CVaR excess risk decomposes into ordinary tail-risk statistical error and a privacy price. This decomposition is complete for scalar estimation and finite classes: scalar estimation has rate $Θ(B \min\{1,(nτ)^{-1/2}+(εnτ)^{-1}\})$, and finite classes of size $M$ have rate $Θ(B \min\{1,\sqrt{\log(2M)/(nτ)}+\log(2M)/(εnτ)\})$. These complete rates hold under pure DP, and their lower bounds extend to approximate DP in the stated small-$δ$ regimes. For convex Lipschitz learning, modular upper and lower reductions show that the CVaR-specific privacy term necessarily scales as $1/(εnτ)$, with dimension dependence inherited from private stochastic convex optimization. Together, these results identify ordinary private learning on $Θ(nτ)$ informative tail records as the canonical hard subproblem inside private CVaR learning.

</details>


### [69] [From I/O to Code with Discovery Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15334)
*Yihong Dong,Jiaru Qian,Haoran Zhang,Peixu Wang,Binhua Li,Zhi Jin,Yongbin Li,Ge Li,Xiaokang Yang,Xue Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 IO2Code（从输入输出合成程序）难题，本文提出 DIO-Agent。它通过“执行反馈引导的进化搜索”和“转换优先级前提”来限制搜索空间，实现从简单到复杂的渐进式程序合成，明显超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成工具（NL2Code）依赖自然语言与代码的语义对齐，但在仅有输入输出（IO）样例时表现不佳。IO2Code 面临巨大的搜索空间和严重的欠定问题，缺乏有效从计算行为中恢复程序逻辑的范式。

Method: 1. 提出 DIO-Agent：一种将 IO2Code 建模为离散程序空间进化搜索的智能体。2. 利用 LLM 作为变异算子，并利用执行错误信号引导变异方向。3. 引入“转换优先级前提”（Transformation Priority Premise）：一种变异先验，引导模型优先探索结构简单的假设（从常量到条件判断，再到迭代循环）。4. 构建 IO2CodeBench：用于系统评估 IO2Code 性能的基准测试集。

Result: DIO-Agent 在 IO2CodeBench 上的表现显著优于传统的程序归纳方法（Program-by-Example）以及当前最优的进化智能体基线，且在同等采样预算下优于单纯的测试时扩展（test-time scaling）策略。

Conclusion: DIO-Agent 通过将程序合成建模为由执行错误驱动的进化搜索，显著提升了从输入-输出行为合成程序（IO2Code）的能力。其提出的“转换优先级前提”有效地规范了搜索空间，使该方法在多种难度和 LLM 模型上均优于现有的程序合成基准。

Abstract: The automatic synthesis of a program from any form of specification is regarded as a holy grail of computer science. Fueled by LLMs, NL2Code has achieved tremendous success, yet the fundamentally more challenging task of synthesizing programs from input-output behavior, which we refer to as IO2Code, remains largely unsolved. Whereas NL2Code can exploit the semantic alignment between natural language and code acquired during pretraining, IO2Code requires recovering underlying principles from concrete computational behavior, navigating a vast and underspecified hypothesis space. To address this, we propose DIO-Agent, a discovery agent for IO2Code. Our method frames IO2Code as an evolutionary search over discrete program space, in which an LLM serves as the mutation operator and concrete error signals from execution guide each mutation. To prevent the search from wandering into structurally complex yet incorrect dead ends, we introduce the Transformation Priority Premise as a mutation prior that biases the LLM toward the simplest hypothesis consistent with current evidence, progressively escalating from constants to conditionals to iteration only when simpler constructs are insufficient. To facilitate systematic study, we further construct an IO2CodeBench spanning multiple difficulty levels. Extensive experiments show that DIO-Agent consistently outperforms both traditional program-by-example method and SOTA evolution-agent baselines across all difficulty levels and various LLMs, while substantially surpassing test-time scaling strategies with equivalent sampling budgets.

</details>


### [70] [Bounded-Rationality, Hedging, and Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15340)
*Pedro A. Ortega*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于有界理性决策理论的泛化分析框架，通过将学习过程视为对样本影响的处理（对冲），证明了可以通过观测学习器对损失扰动的响应，来量化并验证学习器的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解学习器如何平衡“数据拟合”与“对训练样本的依赖性（或称过拟合风险）”，并克服传统泛化界通常依赖于模型内部结构或大样本假设的局限，寻求一种可测试的、基于观测行为的泛化性评估框架。

Method: 将学习过程建模为以诱导通道（induced channel）为核心的有界理性决策问题；利用信息几何（特别是f-divergence正则化）量化样本对输出的影响；通过对缩放损失和局部损失扰动的观察，重构对冲曲面（hedge）以及上下界证书曲线（certificate curves）。

Result: 提出了一个理论框架，证明了在给定响应规律下，训练损失与样本依赖性之间存在确定的权衡曲线；推导出了与f-divergence正则化相对应的几何结构；证明了可以通过黑盒观测恢复样本对冲能力，从而为评估学习器泛化性能提供了实用的量化证书。

Conclusion: 泛化能力可被视为学习器响应规律的一种可测试的对冲属性（hedging property）。学习者通过这种内生机制在训练损失与样本依赖性之间进行权衡，从而在无需访问模型内部参数的情况下，通过黑盒观测验证其泛化的可靠性。

Abstract: A learner does not only fit data; it also determines how strongly the training sample may shape its output and how much distortion it can hedge. We study this relation as a bounded-rational decision problem whose primitive object is the induced channel from samples to outputs. The learner's response law determines which changes in this channel are cheap or costly, and therefore induces both a lower tradeoff curve between training loss and sample dependence and a matched upper certificate curve. When the response law is represented by an $f$-divergence regularizer, these curves live in the regularizer's native information geometry, with KL as the special case corresponding to Shannon mutual information. We show how the hedge and the two curves can be recovered from black-box behavior by observing responses to scaled losses and local loss perturbations. In learning, population loss is empirical loss plus the distortion induced by the particular training sample. The recovered hedge gives a practical certificate when it covers that distortion. Thus generalization is treated as a testable hedging property of the learner's own response law.

</details>


### [71] [LEAP: Trajectory-Level Evaluation of LLMs in Iterative Scientific Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15341)
*Marilyn Zhang,Tianfeng Chen,Fabián Barzuna,Ankita Rathod,Mark E. Whiting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文分析了LLM在自主实验室迭代科研设计中的学习效率，提出了LEAPBench评估框架。研究发现，传统的最终结果评估法无法准确反映效率，LLM未优于传统贝叶斯优化，且领域先验的依赖可能引发误导。LEAPBench为评估与优化科研智能体的学习过程提供了更稳健的指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的迭代科研设计基准仅关注固定时间点的结果快照，忽视了学习轨迹（learning trajectory），从而无法评估真正的科研效率（即节省迭代次数所带来的成本与时间效益）。因此，研究旨在重新审视LLM在科研设计中的学习效率评估方法。

Method: 1. 提出LEAPBench基准：包含55个任务，采用“迄今最佳曲线下面积(Best-so-far AUC)”作为轨迹评估指标。 2. 引入对比基准：将LLM与传统贝叶斯优化进行对比，并利用已发表文献进行审计（Grounding）。 3. 实验设置：评估8个主流LLM，并在16个生物学任务中对比领域感知（Domain-aware）与领域无关（Domain-agnostic）提示方法。 4. 应用优化：利用该指标作为线下强化学习（Offline RL）的奖励信号进行模型训练。

Result: 1. 轨迹评估方式的改变导致53%的任务中最佳模型选择发生变化，揭示了传统评估指标掩盖的效率提升。 2. 实验表明LLM在这些任务中并未超越传统的贝叶斯优化基线。 3. 在生物学任务中，领域先验反而可能降低性能，领域无关的提示在匹配已发布最优配置方面表现更好。 4. 轨迹度量作为训练奖励，成功提升了21个测试任务中14个任务的性能。

Conclusion: 单纯的结果导向评估（Outcome-based）会掩盖学习效率的差异。目前的LLM在迭代科研设计中并未超越传统的贝叶斯优化，且模型对领域先验的错误依赖可能导致性能下降。LEAPBench提供的轨迹度量不仅能更准确地评估效率，还能作为有效的强化学习训练目标。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly deployed in autonomous laboratories, under the assumption that their domain priors and reasoning over iterative feedback let them converge on good designs in fewer iterations than feedback-only baselines. Current iterative scientific design benchmarks, however, score only outcome snapshots at fixed horizons. This leaves the learning trajectory unmeasured, even though the trajectory is what captures learning efficiency, where each iteration saved is a real saving in cost and time. Motivated by this, we examine three evaluation choices that change the conclusions one draws about LLM learning efficiency in iterative scientific design: what to measure, what baseline to compare against, and what to ground against. We introduce LEAPBench, Learning Efficiency in Adaptive Processes, a 55-task framework that pairs a best-so-far area under the curve (AUC) trajectory metric with a classical Bayesian-optimization reference and an audit grounded in published literature. Applied to eight contemporary LLMs, switching from final-outcome to trajectory scoring changes the best-model decision on 53% of tasks at matched horizons, and exposes efficiency gains overlooked by outcome-based scoring. LLMs do not outperform a classical Bayesian baseline. On 16 biology tasks where the oracle's reward signal is aligned with configurations from the published-best design, domain-aware prompting leads to LLM choices that match the published-best's approximately 10 percentage points less often than domain-agnostic prompting at iteration 30. The pattern is sharpest on 6 tasks where the literature-typical and published-best configurations diverge, and domain-agnostic prompting matches the published-best more often on all 6. The trajectory metric also doubles as a tractable training target. Offline reinforcement learning with the metric as a reward improves performance on 14 of 21 held-out tasks.

</details>


### [72] [Controllable Molecular Generative Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15354)
*Yihan Zhu,Yuhan Liu,Weijiang Li,Tengfei Luo,Meng Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoMole是一种新型分子生成基础模型，通过引入基团感知扩散和层级强化学习，不仅解决了分子生成的化学有效性难题，还实现了跨任务的高效可控生成和任务迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分子生成模型难以兼顾异构任务设计与高质量的可控性；原子级的强化学习在分子生成中面临巨大的动作空间和化学无效状态问题，缺乏一个统一的框架来应对这些挑战。

Method: 提出了CoMole框架，包括：1. 统一的基团感知（motif-aware）图扩散策略；2. 基于基团层级的强化学习，优化条件反向策略，避免了原子级动作空间带来的不稳定性和化学无效性；3. 通过冻结生成器仅优化任务嵌入（task embeddings）来实现对未知属性的零样本/少样本迁移控制。

Result: 在九个不同目标的基准测试中，CoMole的可控性均排名第一；相较于现有最强基准，误差（MAE）降低高达48.2%；在无规则校正或后处理过滤的情况下，保持了0.94以上的化学有效性。

Conclusion: CoMole通过学习基团感知的图空间，成功解决了分子生成中的控制难题，在多个基准测试中表现卓越，证明了其在材料科学和药物发现领域的高效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Despite the success of foundation models in language and vision, molecular graph generation still lacks a unified framework for heterogeneous design tasks with reliable controllability. While reinforcement learning (RL) offers a natural post-training mechanism for task-specific optimization, applying it to graph generative models is hindered by the vast atom-wise action spaces and chemically invalid intermediate states. We propose \textbf{Co}ntrollable \textbf{Mole}cular Generative Foundation Models (CoMole), built with a unified motif-aware graph diffusion pipeline. By learning a motif-aware graph space, CoMole transfers pretrained structural priors into controllable generation, where RL optimizes conditional reverse policies over chemically meaningful decisions. We theoretically characterize the bottleneck of atom-level RL and justify motif-aware policy optimization. Across three heterogeneous benchmarks spanning materials and drug discovery, CoMole ranks first in controllability on all nine targets, reduces MAE by up to 48.2% relative to the strongest baselines, and maintains validity above 0.94 without rule-based correction or post-hoc filtering. We further show that CoMole transfers controllability to unseen properties by optimizing only task embeddings with the generator frozen, achieving performance competitive with strong task-specific baselines.

</details>


### [73] [Is One Score Enough? Rethinking the Evaluation of Sequentially Evolving LLM Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15384)
*Songwei Dong,Zihan Chen,Chengshuai Shi,Peng Wang,Jundong Li,Cong Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM序列任务记忆评估不足的问题，提出SeqMem-Eval诊断框架。研究证实：仅凭最终准确率无法衡量记忆质量，模型在序列推理中普遍存在被掩盖的遗忘与负迁移现象，该框架能有效揭示记忆设计的稳定性与适应性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM记忆评估过分依赖最终准确率等聚合指标，无法揭示模型在处理序列任务时存在的遗忘、负迁移等深层缺陷，缺乏对记忆动态演化过程的有效评估。

Method: 提出SeqMem-Eval诊断评估框架。该框架聚焦于外部提示触发的非参数化记忆更新，通过在线效用（Online Utility）、留出泛化能力（Hold-out Generalization）、后向迁移（Backward Transfer）和遗忘率（Forgetting）四个维度对记忆演化过程进行细粒度量化。

Result: 实验表明，高准确率模型可能表现出严重的遗忘或负迁移；不同的记忆设计在适应性（Adaptability）与稳定性（Stability）之间存在显著的权衡关系，而这些权衡在传统评估方法中不可见。

Conclusion: 现有的聚合评估指标无法反映LLM在序列任务中的真实记忆质量。研究表明，单纯的高最终准确率往往掩盖了灾难性遗忘或负迁移现象，因此需要采用SeqMem-Eval等多维诊断指标来评估记忆设计的稳定性与适应性权衡。

Abstract: Memory plays a central role in enabling large language models (LLMs) to operate over sequential tasks by accumulating and reusing experience over time. However, existing evaluations of LLM memory mostly rely on aggregate metrics such as final hold-out accuracy or cumulative online performance, which can obscure critical failure modes such as forgetting and negative transfer. In this paper, we introduce SeqMem-Eval, a diagnostic evaluation framework for sequentially evolving LLM memory. Drawing inspiration from continual learning, it targets a test-time setting in which memory is external, prompt-mediated, and updated without modifying model parameters. Rather than focusing only on final performance, SeqMem-Eval evaluates how memory states evolve, generalize, consolidate experience, and retain useful information during sequential inference. Specifically, it measures online utility, hold-out generalization, backward transfer, and forgetting, providing a finer-grained view of memory quality. Through extensive experiments across diverse tasks and memory methods, we show that higher final or cumulative accuracy does not necessarily imply better memory quality: many methods exhibit strong performance gains while suffering from substantial forgetting or negative transfer. Moreover, different memory designs exhibit distinct trade-offs between adaptability and stability that remain invisible under standard evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [74] [Unified High-Probability Analysis of Stochastic Variance-Reduced Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15388)
*Zhankun Luo,Antesh Upadhyay,M. Berk Sahin,Sang Bin Moon,Anuran Makur,Abolfazl Hashemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的随机方差缩减估计框架，通过新的向量值Freedman不等式克服了现有估计器分析的局限性，并实现了带期望约束随机优化下算子复杂度的理论突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机估计方法（如Momentum, SPIDER, STORM, PAGE等）分析依赖于具体的估计器且仅基于期望，缺乏对决定可靠性的结构权衡的统一理解，且往往缺乏高概率分析。

Method: 提出了一种基于三个核心组件（记忆保留、重置概率、迭代移动修正项）的递归估计框架，并使用一种新的无维向量值Freedman不等式来建立高概率界。

Result: 证明了该框架适用于欧几里得和非欧几里得设置（包括Banach空间中的镜像下降）；建立了无约束优化的镜像下降高概率算子复杂度；将带期望约束的随机优化的算子复杂度从$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\varepsilon^{-4})$提升至$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\varepsilon^{-3})$。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种统一的随机方差缩减估计框架，通过新颖的向量值Freedman不等式推导出了高概率界，不仅统一了现有算法，还为带期望约束的随机优化提供了更优的复杂度界（$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\varepsilon^{-3})$）。

Abstract: Stochastic estimators are fundamental to large-scale optimization, where population quantities must be inferred from noisy oracle observations. Although influential methods such as momentum, SPIDER, STORM, and PAGE have been highly successful, their analyses are largely estimator-specific and expectation-based, obscuring the structural tradeoffs that determine reliability. In this paper, we develop a unified framework for stochastic variance-reduced estimation based on a recursion with three components: memory retention, reset probability, and a correction term for iterate movement. This framework recovers several classical estimators, motivates new second-order variants, and yields a bias-variance decomposition of estimation error. Our main result is a unified high-probability bound proved using a new dimension-free vector-valued Freedman inequality, valid for smooth normed spaces involving random sums of vector martingales. The result applies in both Euclidean and non-Euclidean settings, including the analysis of mirror-descent-based methods in Banach spaces. As applications, we obtain high-probability oracle complexities for unconstrained optimization with mirror descent, establishing the logarithmic dependence on the confidence level. We also derive the first $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\varepsilon^{-3})$ oracle-complexity bounds for stochastic optimization with expectation constraints, improving upon the existing $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\varepsilon^{-4})$ complexity by leveraging variance-reduced estimation for the first time in this setting.

</details>


### [75] [LPDS: Evaluating LLM Robustness Through Logic-Preserving Difficulty Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15393)
*Philipp Mondorf,Samuel J. Bell,Jesse Dodge,Dieuwke Hupkes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 LPDS 框架，通过系统化搜索并生成逻辑保持的困难问题变体来评估和提升大语言模型的鲁棒性，发现针对困难变体的训练比随机样本更能有效改进模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型（LLM）在面对逻辑不变但实体（如名称、数字）发生变化的变体时表现不稳定；且现有的评估方法多采用随机采样，无法准确评估模型的真实鲁棒性，甚至会高估模型的能力。

Method: 提出了逻辑保持难度扩展（Logic-Preserving Difficulty Scaling, LPDS）框架，包含两部分：(1) 量化问题变体的难度；(2) 在允许的变体空间中进行系统搜索，找到最大化难度并诱发错误的变体。

Result: LPDS 能有效找到使模型失效的困难变体，性能下降幅度比随机采样高出 5 倍；且随着难度系数的增加，模型的推理链错误愈发明显。

Conclusion: 通过在困难变体上进行微调，能比在简单变体上训练获得更一致的鲁棒性提升，证明了该方法对模型训练的有效性。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed to perform tasks with minimal human oversight, it is crucial that these models operate robustly. In particular, a model that can solve a given problem should not fail simply because certain entities$\unicode{x2013}$such as names, numbers, or other contextual details$\unicode{x2013}$have changed while the underlying problem logic remains the same. Prior work suggests that current LLMs still struggle with this form of robustness: they often succeed on some variations of a problem but fail on others. However, existing evaluations often lack a systematic way to identify which logic-preserving variations are most likely to induce failure.
