Beyond robustness auditing, we show that the same influence scores enable efficient targeted perturbations, promoting or demoting specific models and reducing target-model uncertainty with fewer actions than previous manipulation and active-sampling baselines. By summarizing these effects with normalized dataset-level robustness scores, our framework provides a practical and helpful tool for auditing leaderboard stability and motivating more robust evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [111] [Continual Learning of Domain-Invariant Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15775)
*Pascal Janetzky,Tobias Schlagenhauf,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个针对持续学习的领域不变表示学习框架，通过在序列训练中强化领域通用特征的提取与保持，显著提升了模型在未见领域的泛化鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的持续学习方法主要关注域内性能优化，容易导致对特定领域“捷径特征（shortcuts）”的过度拟合，从而限制了模型在部署后对未知领域的泛化能力。

Method: 提出了一种结合基于重放（replay-based）的训练与定制化序列不变性对齐（sequential invariance alignment）的CL方法，旨在学习并随时间持续保持跨领域的不变结构。

Result: 在涵盖视觉、医学、制造和生态学等领域的六个基准数据集上，该方法在未见目标域的泛化性能方面始终优于现有的持续学习基线方法。消融实验证明，简单的将现有DIRL方法与序列训练结合效果有限，证实了所提定制对齐机制的必要性。

Conclusion: 本研究首次提出了将领域不变表示学习（DIRL）应用于持续学习（CL）的框架，通过在训练中对齐领域间的不变结构来增强模型对未知领域的泛化能力，克服了传统方法对特定领域虚假特征的过度拟合。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) aims to train models sequentially over multiple domains without forgetting previously learned knowledge. However, existing CL methods optimize for in-domain performance and are therefore prone to learning spurious, domain-specific cues (``shortcut learning''), which limits generalization to unseen domains after deployment. In this paper, we address this limitation through continual learning of domain-invariant representation. We introduce a broad class of CL methods that sequentially learn representations capturing invariant structures across domains. Our methods are motivated by the observation that such invariant structures often preserve the underlying causal mechanisms, which can reduce the risk of overfitting to domain-specific cues and thus offer better out-of-domain generalization. Our proposed CL methods combine replay-based training with a tailored sequential invariance alignment to learn -- and preserve -- invariant structures over time. We evaluate our methods under a deployment-oriented protocol that measures performance on unseen target domains. Across six benchmark and real-world datasets spanning vision, medicine, manufacturing, and ecology, our methods consistently outperform existing CL baselines in terms of generalization to unseen target domains. As an ablation, we further show that naïve extensions of sequential training with existing domain-invariant representation learning (DIRL) methods provide only limited benefits. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to develop domain-invariant representation methods for CL.

</details>


### [112] [AOT-POT: Adaptive Operator Transformation for Large-Scale PDE Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15793)
*Qitan Lv,Hong Wang,Zhongkai Hao,Wen Wu,Xuenan Xu,Bowen Zhou,Feng Wu,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多PDE预训练中算子差异大、难建模的问题，本文提出AOT-POT，通过自适应变换将多样化的PDE算子重塑为统一形式，在参数量极小增加的前提下显著提升了预测精度与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多PDE预训练方法大多依赖增加模型容量，但忽略了不同PDE算子本身结构的差异性和复杂性，导致模型难以高效、统一地建模多样化的物理系统。

Method: 提出AOT-POT（自适应算子变换算子Transformer），其核心机制包括：将隐藏表示扩展为多个并行流、在子层前后进行自适应聚合与重分布、以及利用Sinkhorn投影的双随机矩阵进行流混合。

Result: 在12个PDE基准测试上达到SOTA，仅增加3%参数的情况下，平均相对L2误差降低40.9%（最高77.6%）；微调后在域内和域外PDE的表现分别提升高达92%和89%。

Conclusion: 自适应算子变换（AOT）是一种通用的预训练策略，能够通过显式地对齐复杂的PDE算子，显著提升神经算子的表现与泛化能力，不仅限于单纯的参数规模扩展。

Abstract: Pre-training neural operators on diverse partial differential equation (PDE) datasets has emerged as a promising direction for building general-purpose surrogate models in scientific machine learning. However, the inherent complexity and structural diversity of PDE solution operators make multi-PDE pre-training fundamentally challenging. Existing methods mainly address this by increasing model capacity, while leaving the target solution operators unchanged. Inspired by classical numerical analysis, we instead propose to transform complex and diverse solution operators into simpler, better-aligned forms that are easier to model jointly. Since the optimal transformation varies across PDE types, it must be adaptive and input-dependent, allowing a single neural operator to approximate an entire family of operators. We instantiate this idea as AOT-POT (adaptive operator-transformation for pre-training operator transformer), which expands hidden representations into multiple parallel streams, adaptively aggregates and redistributes them before and after each sub-layer, and mixes streams through Sinkhorn-projected doubly stochastic matrices for stable training. These mechanisms together reshape diverse solution operators into a unified form that can be effectively modeled by a single architecture. Empirically, AOT-POT achieves state-of-the-art performance on 12 PDE benchmarks with only 3\% additional parameters, reducing relative L2 error by up to 77.6\% (40.9\% on average). Fine-tuning AOT-POT further reduces L2 error by up to 92\% on in-domain PDEs and 89\% on out-of-domain PDEs (unseen types during pre-training), demonstrating that adaptive operator transformation is an effective and complementary direction for advancing PDE foundation models beyond simply scaling model capacity.

</details>


### [113] [Ti-iLSTM: A TinyDL Approach for Logic-Level Anomaly Detection in Industrial Water Treatment Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15874)
*Mandar Joshi,Farzana Zahid,Judy Bowen,Matthew M. Y. Kuo,Valeriy Vyatkin,Emil Karlsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为Ti-iLSTM的轻量化TinyDL框架，旨在解决IWTS等工业系统中PLC设备资源受限情况下，对隐蔽逻辑层异常检测困难的问题，实现了高效且高精度的异常监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业水处理系统（IWTS）面临逻辑层欺骗攻击，这类攻击能保持数据数值合理但破坏因果关系，传统的阈值检测或大型服务器端模型难以有效且高效地应对。

Method: 提出了TinyDL-based incremental LSTM (Ti-iLSTM) 框架，通过优化LSTM的内存和空间占用，实现在资源受限的PLC上进行轻量级设备端逻辑级异常检测。

Result: 在SWaT数据集上取得了F1-score=0.983和ROC-AUC=0.998的高检测性能；在WADI数据集上的验证证实了该轻量级框架的可移植性。

Conclusion: 将逻辑感知监督与TinyDL序列学习相结合，为资源受限的PLC环境提供了一种高效且准确的异常检测方案，且在不同数据集上表现出良好的普适性。

Abstract: Industrial Water Treatment Systems (IWTS) are safety critical cyber-physical infrastructures and due to increased connectivity, these systems are exposed to cyber threats that can manipulate process behaviour without creating obvious devices outliers. In particular, logic-layer deception anomalies can preserve numerically plausible measurements while breaking expected cause-and-effect relationships in the control process. These attacks are difficult to detect using threshold-based monitoring or require heavy server-oriented anomaly detection models. This paper explores the potential of Tiny Deep Learning (TinyDL) to provide lightweight on-device logic-level anomaly detection for resource constrained Programmable Logic Controllers (PLCs). We propose a novel framework, TinyDL-based incremental LSTM (Ti-iLSTM) which optimises the memory and space foot print of Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM), to detect logic-layer inconsistencies in Programmable Logic Controller (PLC) based Industrial Water Treatment Systems (IWTS). Experiments on the publicly available SWaT dataset show that the optimised model achieves high detection performance (F1-score=0.983 and ROC-AUC=0.998). A deployment-style validation on the WADI dataset confirms that the proposed light-weight framework remains applicable beyond a single dataset. The research demonstrates that combining logic-aware supervision with Tiny Deep Learning (TinyDL) sequence learning creates an efficient and accurate anomaly detection suitable for resource constrained Programmable Logic Controllers (PLCs) in industrial environments.

</details>


### [114] [Practical Validity Conditions for Byzantine-Tolerant Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15887)
*Mélanie Cambus,Darya Melnyk,Tijana Milentijević,Stefan Schmid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文引入了最小包围球 (MEB) 有效性及其松弛版本 $c$-MEB，旨在替代联邦学习中局限性较大的“凸有效性”条件。研究表明，$c$-MEB 能有效结合现有聚合算法并提供理论保障，为拜占庭鲁棒联邦学习提供了更具实践价值的几何有效性框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的“凸有效性”(convex validity) 条件虽然理论上较强，但存在维度依赖的鲁棒性限制，且与现代联邦学习中使用的许多实际聚合规则不兼容。因此需要寻找一种更通用、更符合工程实践的几何有效性条件。

Method: 1. 提出最小包围球 (MEB) 有效性条件及其乘法松弛版本 ($c$-MEB)；2. 分析了 $c$-MEB 在不同假设下的恢复能力；3. 提出最优的 MinMax-MEB 聚合规则；4. 证明了多种现有标准聚合器（如最小直径平均、中心点和几何中值）满足松弛后的 MEB 有效性；5. 将 MEB 有效性与现有的凸、松弛凸和盒有效性进行关联对比。

Result: 证明了精确的 MEB 有效性在鲁棒性上存在局限，但松弛后的 $c$-MEB 有效性在多数客户端诚实（$n>2t$）的情况下是可达的；推导出了 $c<\sqrt{2}$ 的最优界限，并验证了主流聚合算法在该条件下具有明确的几何保证。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 $c$-MEB 有效性条件是传统凸包有效性的一种更具实际意义的替代方案。它不仅在理论上统一了现有的几何有效性条件，还证明了在 $n>2t$ 的合理假设下，包括几何中值在内的多种主流聚合算法满足 $c$-MEB 有效性，从而在拜占庭鲁棒联邦学习中实现了更好的性能与兼容性平衡。

Abstract: Robust aggregation is the core operation in Byzantine-tolerant federated learning. To ensure the quality of aggregation independently of data distribution or attacks, validity conditions are needed. They provide geometric guarantees of where the output of the aggregation must lie. The widespread convex validity requires the output to lie in the convex hull of the honest vectors. Although this guarantee is strong in theory, it is poorly suited to modern federated learning systems, as it has dimension-dependent resilience and excludes many practical aggregation rules.
  We introduce the minimum enclosing ball (MEB) validity condition for robust aggregation, as well as its multiplicative relaxation, $c$-MEB validity, where $c$ is a constant. We show that exact MEB validity still suffers from limited resilience, while relaxed $c$-MEB validity is achievable if a majority of clients is honest, i.e. $n>2t$. We give an optimal MinMax-MEB rule for the relaxed condition with the bound $c<\sqrt{2}$ and prove explicit relaxed-MEB guarantees for standard aggregators including minimum-diameter averaging, medoid and geometric median. Finally, we relate MEB validity to convex, relaxed-convex and box validity studied in prior literature, thus providing a systematic map of geometric validity conditions for Byzantine-robust aggregation. Our results show that relaxed MEB validity connects validity conditions in distributed computing and Byzantine-tolerant aggregation rules, and offers a practical alternative to convex validity.

</details>


### [115] [LoCO: Low-rank Compositional Rotation Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15916)
*An Nguyen,Jaesik Choi,Anh Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoCO 通过低秩组合正交变换，实现了在保持模型几何结构的同时进行高效微调，并通过并行计算优化使其适用于高维空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的参数高效微调 (PEFT) 方法（如 LoRA）虽然实现了参数高效性，但在微调过程中难以保持预训练模型表征的几何结构。为了解决这一问题，需要一种既能保持正交性，又能高效处理高维空间的方法。

Method: 提出了一种名为 LoCO (Low-rank Compositional Orthogonal fine-tuning) 的方法。该方法通过低秩斜对称矩阵构造正交变换，并构建组合旋转链。同时，引入了一种近似方案，实现了组合旋转的完全并行计算，以适应高维特征空间。

Result: 在扩散 Transformer 微调、视觉 Transformer 和语言模型微调等多个任务中进行了验证，结果表明 LoCO 在性能上优于或等同于现有的正交和非正交 PEFT 方法，且具有较低的计算复杂度。

Conclusion: LoCO 通过利用低秩斜对称矩阵和组合旋转链，在保持模型正交性约束的同时，提供了高效的参数微调能力，解决了现有 PEFT 方法难以保持预训练表示几何结构的问题。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) has emerged as an critical technique for adapting large-scale foundation models across natural language processing and computer vision. While existing methods such as low-rank adaptations achieve parameter efficiency via low-rank weight updates, they are limited in their ability to preserve the geometric structure of pretrained representations. We introduce Low-rank Compositional Orthogonal fine-tuning (LoCO), a novel PEFT method that constructs orthogonal transformations through low-rank skew-symmetric matrices and compositional rotation chains. We propose an approximation scheme that enables fully parallel computation of compositional rotations, making the approach practical for high-dimensional feature spaces. Our method maintains low computational complexity while maintaining orthogonality with controlled approximation error. We validate LoCO across diverse domains, including diffusion transformer fine-tuning, vision transformer adaptation, and language model adaptation. Our method demonstrates superior or competitive performance compared to both existing orthogonal and non-orthogonal methods.

</details>


### [116] [Entropy-Based Characterisation of the Polarised Regime in Latent Variable Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15965)
*Peter Clapham,Lisa Bonheme,Marek Grzes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用的信息论准则来表征VAE的极化现象，揭示了被动维度在适当处理后仍包含有用信息，并推翻了“被动维度即代表完全信息坍缩”的观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有识别VAE活跃维度的准则多依赖高斯先验，普适性较差。研究旨在建立一种更通用的信息论框架来表征VAE的极化现象。

Method: 提出一种基于均值表示熵的信息论准则，无需依赖特定先验分布，并结合方差表示信号以区分活跃与混合维度。

Result: 实验验证该准则在多种VAE变体（如β-VAE、可辨识VAE等）中均能稳定识别出极化现象。

Conclusion: 被动维度并非完全无用，通过适当的归一化处理，它们仍能增强下游任务性能，表明模型崩溃并非信息的绝对丢失，而是尺度的体现。

Abstract: Variational Autoencoders (VAEs) often exhibit a polarised regime in which latent variables separate into active, passive, and mixed subsets. Existing criteria for identifying active dimensions depend on a Gaussian prior, limiting their applicability to variational models and specific priors. We propose a simple information-theoretic classification of the polarised regime based on the entropy of the mean representation. We show theoretically how this entropy couples to KL minimisation through entropy--variance bounds, and we relate the resulting criterion to Bonheme's active/passive conditions. We also clarify a key limitation: entropy of the mean alone cannot reliably distinguish active from mixed dimensions without additional signals from the variance representation. Empirically, we evaluate the entropy criterion on $β$-VAEs, identifiable VAEs, Least-Volume Autoencoders, and L2-regularised autoencoders, and find that it consistently recovers a polarised regime when such a regime is present across the model classes studied. Finally, we show that passive dimensions can yield small but consistent improvements on downstream tasks when latent codes are appropriately normalised, suggesting that collapse is often a matter of scale rather than absolute information removal.

</details>


### [117] [Accelerated Gradient Descent for Faster Convergence with Minimal Overhead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16017)
*Manuel Graca,L. Miguel Silveira,Arlindo Oliveira,Frank Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于曲率调节的一阶优化加速方法 CT-AGD，在不增加额外计算负担的情况下，平均提升了 33% 的深度学习训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决深层学习非凸优化问题中传统一阶优化方法收敛速度慢的问题。

Method: 提出了一种名为 CT-AGD 的通用加速方法。该方法利用有限差分商显式捕获局部曲率，并引入启发式策略来减轻随机小批量（mini-batch）训练中产生的噪声和偏差，从而加速一阶优化算法。

Result: 在实验中，CT-AGD 在达到与基准一阶方法相同精度水平的同时，将平均训练轮数（epochs）减少了 33%。

Conclusion: CT-AGD 证明了通过结合局部曲率信息，可以在保持与 Adam 等自适应方法相当的计算开销前提下，显著提高深度学习训练的收敛速度。

Abstract: In this paper, we present CT-AGD (Curvature-Tuned Accelerated Gradient Descent), an optimization method for non-convex optimization problems in deep learning training tasks. CT-AGD is a general boosting procedure that accelerates first-order methods by explicitly capturing the local curvature using finite-difference quotients, and the development of heuristics aimed at mitigating noise and bias introduced by stochastic mini-batch training. CT-AGD has a comparable storage and computational overhead as adaptive gradient methods such as Adam. Our extensive experiments demonstrate that CT-AGD achieves the same level of accuracy as the baseline first-order methods, yet reduces the required training epochs by 33% on average.

</details>


### [118] [Ada-Diffuser: Latent-Aware Adaptive Diffusion for Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16054)
*Fan Feng,Selena Ge,Minghao Fu,Zijian Li,Yujia Zheng,Zeyu Tang,Yingyao Hu,Biwei Huang,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对现有生成式决策模型忽略潜在动态的缺陷，提出了Ada-Diffuser。该框架通过因果扩散模型显式学习并利用潜在动态，显著提升了智能体在复杂环境下的规划与控制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于生成式模型（如扩散模型）的决策方法缺乏对环境过渡、奖励结构和智能体行为中潜在动态因素的显式建模，导致在复杂环境下的决策精度和适应性不足。

Method: 提出Ada-Diffuser，一种结合了因果扩散模型与潜变量动态推断的统一框架。该方法通过小时间窗口的观测数据识别潜在过程，并学习观测交互与底层动态的时间结构，支持规划和策略学习的模块化设计。

Result: 实验结果显示，Ada-Diffuser在模拟控制和机器人基准测试中表现出色，能准确推断潜在动态，并实现高效的自适应策略学习。

Conclusion: 本文提出的Ada-Diffuser框架通过因果扩散模型成功实现了对潜在动态的显式建模与推断，证明了在生成式决策中整合潜变量动态对于提升复杂环境下的规划与控制能力的有效性。

Abstract: Recent work has framed decision-making as a sequence modeling problem using generative models such as diffusion models. Although promising, these approaches often overlook latent factors that exhibit evolving dynamics, elements that are fundamental to environment transitions, reward structures, and high-level agent behavior. Explicitly modeling these hidden processes is essential for both precise dynamics modeling and effective decision-making. In this paper, we propose a unified framework that explicitly incorporates latent dynamic inference into generative decision-making from minimal yet sufficient observations. We theoretically show that under mild conditions, the latent process can be identified from small temporal blocks of observations. Building on this insight, we introduce Ada-Diffuser, a causal diffusion model that learns the temporal structure of observed interactions and the underlying latent dynamics simultaneously, and furthermore, leverages them for planning and control. With a modular design, Ada-Diffuser supports both planning and policy learning tasks, enabling adaptation to latent variations in dynamics, rewards, and latent actions. Experiments on simulated control and robotic benchmarks demonstrate its effectiveness in accurate latent inference and adaptive policy learning.

</details>


### [119] [ITGPT: Generative Pretraining on Irregular Timeseries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16069)
*Antoine Honoré,Ming Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ITGPT 是一种专门处理不规则采样多模态时间序列的 Transformer 架构，通过结合 SSL 和 GPT 预训练，无需复杂的数据预处理即可在医疗和工业领域实现 SOTA 性能，并极大提高了在少样本场景下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间序列回归模型难以处理具有不规则采样和缺失值的多模态数据（常见于医疗和预测性维护领域），且现有的大语言模型架构缺乏处理此类特定领域数据的灵活性。

Method: 提出了 ITGPT 架构，这是一种基于注意力机制的模型，旨在直接处理多模态、不规则采样的时间序列，支持自监督学习（SSL）和类 GPT 生成式训练目标。

Result: 在 TIHM（医疗）和 CompX（预测性维护）数据集上的实验表明，ITGPT 达到了 SOTA 性能，并证明在标注数据有限时，通过 SSL 和 GPT 预训练能够优于纯监督学习方法。

Conclusion: ITGPT 证明了在处理不规则采样多模态时间序列时，无需重采样、数据融合或显式填充即可实现卓越性能；其利用无监督学习（SSL）和生成式预训练（GPT）的能力，显著缓解了标注数据稀缺的问题，为处理大规模非结构化时间序列数据提供了高效的范式。

Abstract: Timeseries regression models often struggle to leverage large volumes of labeled multimodal data, particularly when the data are irregularly sampled or contain missing values. This is common in domains like healthcare and predictive maintenance, where data are collected from unreliable sources, and labeling requires expert knowledge or costly equipments. Transformer-based large language models have proven effective on structured data such as text through self-supervised learning (SSL) and generative pretraining (GPT) frameworks. However, such models lack the flexibility to efficiently process irregularly sampled multimodal timeseries data. In this paper, we introduce ITGPT, an attention-based architecture designed for handling multimodal, irregularly sampled timeseries by allowing training with both SSL losses and GPT-like objectives. We evaluate its performance on a healthcare task with the TIHM dataset, and a predictive maintenance task with the CompX dataset. Our results demonstrate that ITGPT achieves state-of-the-art performance without requiring resampling, feature fusion or explicit data imputation. Furthermore, when labels are scarce, ITGPT effectively leverages unlabeled data through SSL and GPT training, outperforming the purely supervised approach. This represents an important step towards efficiently using large and unstructured timeseries datasets for practical inference tasks.

</details>


### [120] [MIND: Decoupling Model-Induced Label Noise via Latent Manifold Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16081)
*Dayong Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对预训练模型产生的非随机、结构化标注噪声，本文提出了MIND框架，通过潜在流形解耦技术在无需真实标签的情况下精准识别并纠正系统性错误，在复杂数据集和视觉模型蒸馏中展现了优异的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前由预训练专家模型自动标注产生的噪声并非随机噪声，而是带有归纳偏置的系统性噪声，现有的全局转移矩阵方法无法处理这种与特征流形紧密耦合的结构化错误。

Method: 提出Model-Induced Noise Decoupling (MIND)框架，利用潜在流形解耦（Latent Manifold Disentanglement）将高维噪声分解为可处理的子空间组件；核心部分为潜在解耦估计器（LDE），通过将样本动态投影到具有一致误差模式的结构化簇中来识别噪声。

Result: 在CIFAR-100（受控噪声）及S3DIS、ScanNet（大规模真实3D数据集）上表现优于现有最优方法（SOTA），并成功修正了Vision-Language模型（如OpenSeg）的零样本幻觉。

Conclusion: MIND框架通过潜在流形解耦技术，有效解决了由预训练模型导致的系统性标签噪声问题，为基础模型的鲁棒蒸馏提供了一种可行且高效的方案。

Abstract: The paradigm of learning from automatic annotations driven by pre-trained experts and Foundation Models dominates data-hungry applications. However, it introduces a critical challenge: model-induced label noise. Unlike stochastic noise in classical robust learning, this noise stems from annotator inductive biases, manifesting as systematic errors tightly coupled with local feature manifolds. Existing methods relying on global transition matrices underfit these structural patterns, while learning instance-specific matrices remains mathematically intractable. We propose Model-Induced Noise Decoupling (MIND), a theoretically grounded framework addressing this dilemma. We demonstrate that the high-dimensional noise manifold can be decoupled into tractable, subspace-dependent components via Latent Manifold Disentanglement. Specifically, our Latent Decoupling Estimator (LDE) dynamically projects samples into latent structural clusters with consistent error modes, facilitating noise identifiability without ground-truth anchor points. To rigorously evaluate robustness, we adopt a hierarchical protocol: moving from controlled noise on CIFAR-100 to a structural stress test on large-scale real-world 3D datasets (S3DIS, ScanNet), where error patterns explicitly couple with geometric manifolds. Empirically, MIND significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods on these complex benchmarks and effectively corrects zero-shot hallucinations from Vision-Language Models (e.g., OpenSeg), highlighting its potential as a robust distillation framework for Foundation Models.

</details>


### [121] [Multi-level Self-supervised Pretraining on Compositional Hierarchical Graph for Molecular Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16088)
*Xiayu Liu,Zhengyi Lu,Hou-biao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MolCHG 是一种新的分子图预训练框架，通过构建包含原子、键、片段的多层级图结构，并设计针对性的对比学习与预测任务，显著提升了分子性质预测的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督学习方法多局限于单一结构粒度，且未将化学键信息视为独立的语义层，限制了模型对分子复杂结构和化学特性的捕捉能力。

Method: 提出组件化层次图（Compositional Hierarchical Graph），将分子组织为含原子、键、片段（functional groups）和全图的四种节点；采用三个预训练任务：原子-键跨视图对比学习、片段级官能团预测以及图级结构预测。

Result: 在 MoleculeNet 的 9 个基准数据集上进行测试，MolCHG 在 7 个数据集上取得了最优（SOTA）性能，表现出极强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: MolCHG 提出的多层级结构与预训练目标能够有效捕获分子深层语义，在分子性质预测任务中表现优异，论证了将键信息提升为独立语义层的重要性。

Abstract: Self-supervised pretraining on molecular graphs has emerged as a promising approach for molecular property prediction, yet most existing methods operate at a single structural granularity and treat bond information as auxiliary edge attributes rather than as an independent semantic layer. In this work, we propose MolCHG, a multi-level self-supervised pretraining framework built upon a novel Compositional Hierarchical Graph that organizes molecular structure into four types of nodes across three semantic levels. By introducing a bond graph that operates in parallel with the atom graph, our architecture elevates bond-level information to independently evolving node representations, enabling fragment nodes to aggregate atom-level and bond-level semantics on an equal footing. We design three level-specific pretraining objectives: an atom-bond cross-view contrastive task that aligns the atom-view and bond-view representations within each fragment, a fragment-level functional group prediction task to inject domain-relevant chemical knowledge, and graph-level structure prediction tasks to encode global molecular topology. Experiments on nine MoleculeNet benchmarks demonstrate that MolCHG achieves the best performance on seven datasets across both classification and regression tasks, remaining competitive with the strongest baselines on the rest. Ablation studies further confirm that the multi-level supervision signals are complementary and that each component contributes to the overall performance.

</details>


### [122] [Centralized vs Decentralized Federated Learning: A trade-off performance analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16089)
*Chaimaa Medjadji,Guilain Leduc,Sylvain Kubler,Yves Le Traon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过Fedstellar模拟器对比了集中式、去中心化和半去中心化联合学习架构的性能，填补了架构间权衡分析的研究空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对CFL、DFL和SDFL三种主要联合学习架构的系统性实验对比，未能充分揭示其在不同性能指标下的优势、局限性及折衷方案。

Method: 利用Fedstellar模拟器，在MNIST数据集上使用MLP分类器，对集中式(CFL)、去中心化(DFL)和半去中心化(SDFL)三种联合学习架构进行实验对比与分析。

Result: 实验分析了不同架构在性能指标上的表现差异，明确了各架构在处理通信限制、隐私和分布式环境下的具体表现与权衡。

Conclusion: 通过对三种FL架构的实证对比，弥补了现有研究在性能评估和权衡分析方面的空白，为不同应用场景下的架构选择提供了参考依据。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for collaborative model training across distributed edge devices while preserving data privacy especially with the huge increase amount of data due to the adoption of technologies which contributes to the growing number of IoT devices. Storing this amount of data centrally is challenging due to issues like limited communication, privacy, and regulations. FL can be Centralized (CFL), Decentralized (DFL), and Semi-decentralized (SDFL). Choosing the right FL architecture depends on the application's needs. However, very few research studies have experimentally compared these three types of architectures to not only understand the respective strengths and limitations, but also trade-offs between different performance indicators. This paper overcome this lack of analysis, conducting experimental analyses using the Fedstellar simulator, MNIST dataset, and MLP classifier.

</details>


### [123] [Attention Dispersion in Dynamic Graph Transformers: Diagnosis and a Transferable Fix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16112)
*Jinhao Zhang,Kangfei Zhao,Qiuhao Zeng,Long-Kai Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对动态图 Transformer 在时间分布偏移下注意力分散导致性能下降的问题，本文提出了差分注意力机制（Differential Attention）并构建了 DiffDyG 模型，通过增强对关键节点的关注，显著提升了模型在时间动态环境下的鲁棒性与预测准确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管 Transformer 在连续时间动态图（CTDG）学习中占据主导地位，但在面对时间分布偏移（temporal distribution shift）时表现不佳。研究核心动机在于探索并解决这种性能降级背后的注意力机制失效问题。

Method: 该研究首先诊断出动态图 Transformer 在时间偏移下存在“注意力分散”（attention dispersion）问题。随后，提出了差分注意力机制，旨在抑制模型对非特征性节点的关注，放大关键节点的预测信号，并据此构建了 DiffDyG 模型。

Result: 实验表明，差分注意力显著提高了三种主流 CTDG 模型的性能，尤其是在高偏移数据集上。DiffDyG 在 9 个基准测试和多种负采样协议下均达到了最先进水平，验证了注意力熵的降低和关键节点关注权重的提升。

Conclusion: 通过引入差分注意力机制（Differential Attention），DiffDyG 模型有效抑制了注意力分散现象，增强了模型在时间分布偏移数据集上的鲁棒性，并在多个基准测试中达到了 SOTA 性能。

Abstract: Transformer-based architectures have become the dominant paradigm for Continuous-Time Dynamic Graph (CTDG) learning, yet their performance remains limited on temporally shifted datasets. In this work, we identify attention dispersion as a shared failure mode of dynamic graph Transformers under temporal distribution shift. Through controlled ablation contrasting structurally and temporally distinguished historical neighbors against random ones, we show that prediction depends on a class of critical nodes that carry consistently more predictive signal than arbitrary neighbors. However, existing Transformers fail to focus on these nodes even when they are present in the input, as temporal shift weakens attention contrast and produces overly dispersed attention distributions. This diagnosis suggests a simple and transferable fix: replace standard attention with differential attention, which suppresses common-mode attention and amplifies distinctive token-level signals. When added to three representative CTDG Transformer baselines, differential attention consistently improves performance, with gains concentrated on high-shift datasets. Attention-level measurements further confirm the mechanism, showing reduced attention entropy and increased attention mass on critical nodes. Building on these findings, we introduce DiffDyG, a reference implementation combining differential attention with standard input encodings. Across 9 benchmarks and three negative sampling protocols, DiffDyG achieves SOTA performance, with especially large gains on the most shifted datasets.

</details>


### [124] [Entropy Across the Bridge: Conditional-Marginal Discretization for Flow and Schrödinger Samplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16126)
*Bruno Trentini,Dejan Stancevic,Michael M. Bronstein,Alexander Tong,Luca Ambrogioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于熵率的离散化方法来优化流模型和扩散桥在低计算预算下的生成质量，通过“边界密集型”采样策略，在极少步数推理中实现了比传统线性或余弦调度更好的FID和MMD性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定的小推理预算下，流模型和薛定谔桥的采样质量高度依赖于离散化网格的选择，但传统的经验推断网格（如线性或余弦）往往缺乏理论指导，无法适应特定模型的路径几何。

Method: 提出了一种基于条件-边缘熵率（conditional-marginal entropy-rate）的推断时离散化目标，并据此构建了一个无需额外训练的“熵推断调度器”（entropic inference-time scheduler），该调度器偏好在边界采样（boundary-heavy）以捕捉桥接几何结构。

Result: 在多个测试集上表现优异，包括二维模型（MMD显著提升）、CIFAR-10（5步FID最优）以及 AlphaFlow 蛋白质生成任务，验证了其在低NFE环境下的实用性。

Conclusion: 基于熵率的离散化调度是一种在低成本预算下优化流模型采样质量的有效方法，特别是在极少步数（Low-NFE）的生成场景中具有显著优势。

Abstract: For a fixed flow-based generative model under a small inference budget, sample quality can depend strongly on where the sampler spends its few function evaluations. Flow matching and Schrödinger bridges define probability paths, yet their inference grids are usually heuristic or inherited from one-endpoint diffusion. We derive a conditional-marginal entropy-rate objective for bridge-aware discretization, separating endpoint-conditioned bridge geometry from marginal flow evolution, and use it to build a training-free entropic inference-time scheduler from first principles. For Gaussian Brownian bridges this rate is closed-form and U-shaped, motivating boundary-heavy nonuniform grids. On trained two-dimensional bridge/flow models, the estimated profile recovers the predicted shape and improves 10-step ODE-Heun MMD over linear by 18.1%, with a paired 22.7% SDE-Heun improvement in the same low-NFE sweep. On EDM/CIFAR-10, the entropic time-discretization gives the best tested five-step FID (186.3 \pm 4.0 versus 200.5 \pm 2.9 for linear and 238.0 \pm 5.3 for cosine). On AlphaFlow protein generation, entropic conditional-marginal (cond-marg) scheduling shows advantage in low-NFE regimes on both CAMEO22 and ATLAS benchmarks. These results support entropy-rate scheduling as a practical low-budget allocation signal for high-dimensional bridge and flow samplers.

</details>


### [125] [Navigating Potholes with Geometry-Aware Sharpness Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16134)
*Simon Dufort-Labbé,Mehrab Hamidi,Razvan Pascanu,Ioannis Mitliagkas,Damien Scieur,Aristide Baratin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLQR+SAM通过引入LLQR学习到的慢速几何预条件器来引导SAM的快速锐度探测，实现了一种更精细、有效的曲率感知的优化策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SAM算法虽然能寻找平坦极小值，但在参数空间中对待所有方向一视同仁，忽略了损失平面的内在几何结构。为了针对性地探测局部“坑洼”并保留宽平盆地，作者探索了融合学习到的几何信息与锐度感知优化的可能性。

Method: 提出LLQR+SAM方法，将基于二阶优化LLQR框架得到的预条件器与SAM结合。该方法采用双时间尺度更新策略：预条件器（用于捕捉平滑的全局几何信息）缓慢更新，SAM扰动（用于探测局部曲率）快速执行。

Result: 实验结果表明，LLQR+SAM在标准视觉和序列建模任务中均优于单一的SAM或LLQR算法，证明了预条件先验能够有效放大平坦区域并修正局部尖锐点。

Conclusion: LLQR+SAM通过结合慢速学习的几何先验（LLQR）和快速的锐度修正（SAM），验证了两种机制在优化中的互补性，能有效提升模型在视觉和序列建模任务上的泛化性能。

Abstract: Sharpness-aware minimization (SAM) encourages flat minima by perturbing parameters along directions of high loss curvature, but treats all parameter directions uniformly, ignoring the underlying loss geometry. We introduce LLQR+SAM, which combines SAM with a learned preconditioner obtained from the recently proposed LLQR framework, a second-order method that recasts steepest descent as a layerwise linear-quadratic regulator problem. The preconditioner is updated sparsely and maintained as a slow exponential moving average, so it captures a smoothed, low-resolution picture of the loss landscape geometry. The SAM perturbation then operates on top of this learned geometry, probing curvature at a faster timescale. We show that this two-timescale structure is not merely a computational convenience: theoretically, the preconditioner amplifies the SAM escape signal in directions that are flat under the average geometry but locally sharp (potholes). Wide, flat basins, by contrast, remain stable. Empirically, LLQR+SAM gives consistent gains over both SAM and LLQR alone across standard vision and sequence modeling benchmarks, supporting the view that slow learned geometry and fast sharpness correction are genuinely complementary.

</details>


### [126] [Entropic Auto-Encoding via Implicit Free-Energy Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16164)
*Hazhir Aliahmadi,Irina Babayan,Greg van Anders*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 VAE 后验坍塌问题，本文提出熵自编码器 (EAE)，通过将先验隐式化并引入熵驱动的编码器集合策略，有效提升了潜在空间的表示能力和模型生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决变分自编码器 (VAEs) 中固有的“后验坍塌”（Posterior Collapse）问题，即模型倾向于忽略潜在变量（Latent variables），导致模型性能退化。

Method: 提出熵自编码器 (EAE) 框架：移除显式先验约束，仅保留重构损失；利用编码器集合（Ensemble）通过最小化自由能隐式生成先验，并结合解码器优化引导搜索路径向信息丰富的潜在表示收敛。

Result: EAE 能够学习到非高斯、多模态的潜在分布，实现了更多样化且符合数据的生成；在反应扩散过程、MNIST 分类及 CelebA 人脸结构分层表示任务中均展现了优异性能。

Conclusion: EAE 通过将先验分布的隐式化和基于熵的优化，成功解决了 VAE 中的后验坍塌问题，证明了该方法在捕获复杂、非高斯及多模态数据结构方面的有效性。

Abstract: Despite their ubiquity, variational autoencoders (VAEs) inherently suffer from posterior collapse, a failure mode in which latent variables are effectively ignored. This failure arises because explicit prior imposition drives optimization toward loss landscape regions corresponding to uninformative latent representations. Here, we introduce Entropic Autoencoders (EAEs), a framework in which reconstruction loss is the only explicit objective, and entropy generates the latent variables' prior implicitly through a free energy-minimizing ensemble of encoders. This ensemble biases learning toward high-volume regions of near-optimal solutions, while decoder updates direct the search trajectories toward informative latent representations. We demonstrate that EAEs mitigate posterior collapse by learning non-Gaussian, multimodal latent distributions that yield diverse, data-consistent generations and preserve different forms of underlying structure in the data. As a proof-of-concept, we show that an EAE captures a superposition of the known low-dimensional dynamics of a reaction-diffusion process. Then, we show that an EAE identifies implicit categorical distinctions in MNIST latent representations, and displays a hierarchical understanding of facial structure on the CelebA dataset, from an "all-human" face to individual-dependent features.

</details>


### [127] [BAPR: Bayesian amnesic piecewise-robust reinforcement learning for non-stationary continuous control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16170)
*Yifan Zhang,Liang Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对分段平稳环境下的强化学习困境，BAPR通过结合贝叶斯变化检测与鲁棒集成策略，提供了一种具备形式化验证安全保证的自适应机制，实现了突变环境下的性能优化与稳定性博弈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实控制系统常处于分段平稳状态，现有RL方法存在性能与鲁棒性的两难选择：保守策略导致性能浪费，而适应性策略在发生环境突变时极易失效。提出BAPR旨在实现一种既能高效利用平稳期性能，又能在环境突变时保持鲁棒的安全自适应方案。

Method: 1. BAPR框架：结合贝叶斯在线变化检测（BOCD）与鲁棒集成RL。
2. 算子设计：引入基于冻结置信度分布的模式条件贝尔曼算子加权组合。
3. 理论证明：利用Lean 4进行形式化验证，分析收缩性质及失效边界，推导组件级别的误差预算。
4. 模型实现：结合上下文条件模块（RMDM损失），实现部署时的无标签模式感知。

Result: 1. 证明了BAPR算子在满足特定条件下是γ-收缩映射。
2. 通过Lean 4形式化验证了所有理论结果与误差预算（1145行代码，22个定理，无sorry）。
3. 实现了检测延迟为O(log(1/δ))的自适应保守机制。
4. 部署时无须模式标签即可实现环境突变后的快速恢复与策略调节。

Conclusion: BAPR通过将贝叶斯在线变化检测（BOCD）与鲁棒集成强化学习相结合，有效平衡了性能与风险。通过Lean 4形式化验证，证明在冻结参数设计和特定的置信度加权机制下，算子具有收缩映射属性且具备可控的误差边界，实现了在突发环境变化下的快速自适应与安全恢复。

Abstract: Real-world control systems frequently operate under \emph{piecewise stationary} conditions, where dynamics remain stable for extended periods before undergoing abrupt regime changes. Standard robust RL methods face a fundamental dilemma: a globally conservative policy wastes performance during stable periods, while a locally adaptive policy risks catastrophic failure when the regime changes undetected.
  We propose \textbf{BAPR} (Bayesian Amnesic Piecewise-Robust SAC), which unifies Bayesian Online Change Detection (BOCD) with robust ensemble RL. The BAPR operator -- a convex combination of mode-conditional Bellman operators weighted by a frozen belief distribution -- is a $γ$-contraction. A complementary counterexample, machine-verified in Lean~4, establishes a \emph{sharp boundary}: when beliefs depend on the Q-function, the contraction factor becomes $γ+ λΔ$ (where $Δ$ is the mode reward gap), and contraction fails exactly when $γ+ λΔ\geq 1$. We derive a \emph{component-wise} formal error budget for the abstract operator -- every component machine-verified -- bounding post-switch recovery; the budget applies to the abstract mode-mixture operator and inherits to the implemented shared-critic algorithm only through the frozen-parameter design intuition. All results are formally verified with no \texttt{sorry} (1,145 lines across 3 Lean~4 files, 22 machine-verified theorems). BOCD drives an adaptive conservatism mechanism: the policy becomes maximally conservative after detected change-points and smoothly relaxes as confidence grows, with detection delay $O(\log(1/δ))$. A context-conditioning module trained via RMDM loss provides mode-aware representations from simulator-provided mode IDs at training time and requires no mode labels at deployment.

</details>


### [128] [Imitation learning for clinical decision support in pediatric ECMO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16175)
*Fateme Golivand,Michael Skinner,Saurabh Mathur,Ameet Soni,Phillip Reeder,Kristian Kersting,Lakshmi Raman,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文探讨了利用 TabPFN 对儿科 ECMO 临床决策进行建模。在动作未直接观测的挑战下，TabPFN 表现出比传统机器学习模型更优的决策预测能力，为儿科重症监护环境下的辅助决策提供了新的基准方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿科体外膜肺氧合（ECMO）治疗过程复杂且数据稀缺，迫切需要一种能够从观测数据中学习临床决策模型（特别是动作未直接观测的情况下）的有效方法，以提供精准的决策支持。

Method: 将临床决策过程建模为模仿学习问题（从轨迹中学习动作模型），并对比了 TabPFN（基于 Transformer 的表格数据学习模型）与 XGBoost 和 MLP 等传统基准在真实儿科 ECMO 数据集上的性能。

Result: TabPFN 在各项对比指标中始终优于传统机器学习基准模型（如 XGBoost 和 MLP）。

Conclusion: TabPFN 在建模儿科 ECMO 临床决策方面表现优于 XGBoost 和 MLP 等基准模型，证明其作为临床行为基准在儿科重症监护决策支持中具有很强的应用潜力。

Abstract: Pediatric critical care is a dynamic, high-stakes process involving constant monitoring and adjustments in life-saving treatments. Modeling these interventions is crucial for effective decision support. To address the challenges of high complexity and data scarcity in pediatric Extracorporeal Membrane Oxygenation (ECMO), we frame clinical decision-making as learning to act from trajectories, i.e., imitation learning that learns action models from observational data, with a key feature that actions are not directly observed. We consider TabPFN, a recent transformer-based approach for tabular data, and traditional baselines including XGBoost and Multi-Layer Perceptrons(MLPs) on real-world pediatric ECMO data to learn the action models. We find that the TabPFN-based approach consistently outperforms these classical baselines, supporting its use as a strong clinician-behavior baseline for pediatric ECMO decision support.

</details>


### [129] [Hypothesis-driven construction of mesoscopic dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16211)
*Zhuoyuan Li,Aiqing Zhu,Qianxiao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于广义昂萨格原理的学习框架，通过在数学约束空间内学习介观动力学，解决了复杂多尺度系统的建模难题，并为物理模型提供了鲁棒性和可解释性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的科学建模依赖于针对特定问题的固定有效方程，在处理复杂多尺度系统时面临严重的计算和分析挑战。

Method: 基于广义昂萨格原理（Generalized Onsager principle），构建了一个统一的算子假设空间，涵盖耗散动力学和保守动力学，并通过特定的数学约束（如全局适定性、渐近稳定性、唯一可辨识性和离散能量耗散）预先保证动力学性质。

Result: 在连续PDE数据和缺乏精确介观模型的微观链模型数据上验证了该框架的有效性，结果显示该方法能学习到准确、鲁棒且具有物理可解释性的动力学模型。

Conclusion: 该方法通过在数学约束下的假设空间内学习介观动力学，成功地将物理定律转化为学习框架的先验，既保证了模型的严谨性和可解释性，又具备了解决复杂多尺度系统的强大能力。

Abstract: Traditional scientific modeling typically begins with fixed, instance-wise effective equations and then carries out equation-specific analysis and computation, a procedure that becomes exceptionally challenging in complex applications such as multiscale systems. We propose an alternative paradigm by learning mesoscopic dynamics within a mathematically constrained hypothesis class. Building upon a generalized Onsager principle, we introduce a unified framework encompassing both dissipative and conservative mesoscopic dynamics. We establish uniform and a priori theoretical guarantees, including global well-posedness, asymptotic stability, unique factorization identifiability, and discrete energy dissipation, applicable to all spatio-temporal evolution equations within this hypothesis class prior to all learning stages. Data from each problem instance is then used to guide the identification of members within our hypothesis class, giving rise to accurate, robust and interpretable dynamical models. We empirically validate this framework on both data from continuum PDE models as a check, and on data arising from microscopic chain models for which exact meso-scale models are unknown. The proposed approach not only acts as an effective dynamics learner, but also offers vital interpretable diagnostics of the underlying physics.

</details>


### [130] [Dynamics-Level Watermarking of Flow Matching Models with Random Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16239)
*Shuchan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在生成模型的速度场（动力学层面）嵌入水印的方法，通过添加不影响生成分布的密钥相关扰动，实现了高效、隐蔽且安全的水印嵌入与检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的水印技术通常依赖于修改模型权重或输出像素，可能会影响模型性能或容易被轻易移除。为了解决这些问题，提出了一种直接嵌入在生成模型连续动力学层面的新型水印方法。

Method: 通过在流匹配（flow matching）模型的连续动力学（速度场）中引入与密钥相关的摄动。将水印视为连续信道上的随机编码，在训练阶段施加扰动，并在检测阶段通过黑盒查询进行信息恢复。该摄动设计旨在不改变原始数据分布。

Result: 在MNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上的实验证明，该方法实现了可靠的消息恢复，保持了高质量的生成效果，且未持有密钥的第三方无法解码，解码准确率仅处于偶然水平。

Conclusion: 本文提出的动力学水印方法成功实现了在生成模型中的水印嵌入，既保证了生成质量，又提供了高可靠性的水印检测，且在无密钥情况下具有极高的安全性。

Abstract: We introduce a dynamics-level approach to watermarking generative models. Rather than embedding signals into model weights or outputs, we embed the watermark directly into the learned continuous dynamics -- the velocity field of a flow matching model. We formulate this as random coding over a continuous channel: a key-dependent perturbation is added during training, and the message is recovered at detection time from black-box queries. The perturbation is designed to leave the generated distribution unchanged. Experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 across different architectures confirm reliable message recovery, preserved generation quality, and chance-level decoding accuracy without the secret key.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [MaxSketch: Robust Distinct Counting in Streams via Random Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15571)
*Nikos Tsikouras,Constantine Caramanis,Christos Tzamos*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对含噪高维数据流的去重难题，本文提出了MaxSketch，利用随机高斯投影有效利用数据的几何结构，将去重所需的内存复杂度从 $\widetilde{\Theta}(\sqrt{n})$ 降低至 $\widetilde{O}(\log n / \varepsilon^2)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统去重算法（如HyperLogLog）仅适用于精确匹配场景，无法处理现代表示学习中普遍存在的含噪、高维、仅近似相似的数据流环境；现有的鲁棒去重算法在广义度量空间下内存上限较高（$\widetilde{\Theta}(\sqrt{n})$）。

Method: 提出MaxSketch：一种基于随机高斯投影的max-linear sketch，通过对高维数据流进行投影，实现在近似相似的数据流中估计不同独立对象的数量。

Result: 理论证明在特定几何假设下，MaxSketch 仅需 $\widetilde{O}(\log n / \varepsilon^2)$ 的投影数量和内存即可实现 $(1+\varepsilon)$ 误差范围内的估计；实验验证了其在图像流中的有效性及泛化能力。

Conclusion: 在具有潜在几何结构的表示学习场景中，利用随机高斯投影的MaxSketch能够以准线性空间复杂度实现精确的去重统计，证明了结合几何结构可以显著降低经典流计算的复杂度。

Abstract: Estimating the number of distinct elements in a data stream is well understood when repeated elements are identical. In modern settings, however, observations are high-dimensional and noisy, so repeated instances of the same object are only approximately similar -- for example, different images of the same individual may vary significantly at the pixel level. Classical sketches such as HyperLogLog rely on consistent hash values for identical elements and break down in this regime. Recent work on robust distinct counting in general metric spaces achieves $\widetildeΘ(\sqrt{n})$ memory, which is tight in the worst case. We show that substantially improved memory guarantees are possible under geometric structure common in learned representations. We introduce MaxSketch, a simple max-linear sketch built from random Gaussian projections, and prove that it succeeds in estimating the number of distinct latent objects. Concretely, we show that under this assumption $m = \widetilde{O} (\log n / \varepsilon^2)$ random projections (and hence $\widetilde{O} (\log n/\varepsilon^2)$ memory) suffice to recover the true distinct count within a $(1+\varepsilon)$ factor. Experiments on image streams confirm that MaxSketch accurately estimates distinct counts and generalizes beyond the training regime. Our results bridge classical streaming algorithms and modern representation learning, showing how geometric structure can fundamentally reduce the complexity of distinct counting.

</details>


### [132] [Pessimistic Risk-Aware Policy Learning in Contextual Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15620)
*Yilong Wan,Yuqiang Li,Xianyi Wu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的离线策略学习框架，用于优化Lipschitz连续风险准则（如CVaR、方差等），实现了与风险中性优化相同的统计效率（$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/\sqrt{n})$），解决了高风险领域策略优化的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离线上下文多臂老虎机方法大多集中在期望奖励准则上，或者仅限于风险评估而非优化，缺乏在无法进行在线交互且需严格控制风险的高风险领域中使用的离线策略寻优机制。

Method: 提出了一种用于优化Lipschitz连续风险泛函的统一分布式框架；开发了基于重要性采样分布式估计器的新型经验浓度不等式。

Result: 推导出了依赖于数据且收敛速度为 $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/\sqrt{n})$ 的次优性界限，该界限无需强加限制性的均匀重叠假设，且在极小极大意义下是最优的。

Conclusion: 在Lipschitz连续风险泛函下进行离线策略优化，其统计最优性与风险中性策略优化相同，不会产生额外的统计代价。

Abstract: We study risk-aware offline policy learning, aiming to learn a decision rule from logged data that is optimal under general risk criteria. This problem is crucial in high-stakes domains where online interaction is infeasible and adverse outcomes must be carefully controlled. However, existing literature on offline contextual bandits either centers on expected-reward criteria or restricts risk considerations to policy evaluation instead of optimization. In this work, we propose a unified distributional framework for optimizing Lipschitz-continuous risk functionals, a broad class of risk measures encompassing mean-variance, entropic risk, and conditional value-at-risk, among others. By developing novel empirical concentration inequalities for importance sampling-based distributional estimators, our analysis derives data-dependent suboptimality bounds with an $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/\sqrt{n})$ rate, without relying on restrictive uniform overlap assumptions. This rate is minimax optimal and matches that of risk-neutral offline policy optimization, indicating that optimizing general Lipschitz risk criteria incurs no additional statistical cost relative to the expected-reward.

</details>


### [133] [$α$-TCAV: A Unified Framework for Testing with Concept Activation Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15688)
*Ekkehard Schnoor,Jawher Said,Malik Tiomoko,Wojciech Samek,Alexander Jung*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文揭示了 TCAV 类方法在统计学上的不稳定性，通过提出 $\alpha$-TCAV 广义框架解决了方差收敛问题，并提供了改进现有人工解释实践的明确理论指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于概念的解释方法（如 TCAV）存在统计不稳定性，标准评估方法在该领域缺乏理论依据且在特定条件下表现出不可控的方差。

Method: 1. 对 PatternCAV、FastCAV 及岭回归 CAV 的分布进行概率分析；2. 识别并证明标准 TCAV 分数中指标函数导致的方差问题；3. 提出 $\alpha$-TCAV 广义框架，使用参数化平滑函数替代不连续指标函数；4. 提供基于 Bayes 最优性的参数调优建议。

Result: $\alpha$-TCAV 能够以较低计算成本实现与 Multi-TCAV 等效甚至更优的效果；证明了现有主流实验设定缺乏理论依据，并建议实践中应将采样预算集中分配给单个 CAV 以提高统计精度。

Conclusion: $\alpha$-TCAV 通过引入平滑函数解决了 TCAV 类方法在关键区域方差不收敛的问题，并提供了一套具有理论支撑的评估与调参框架，显著提升了概念解释的可靠性与计算效率。

Abstract: Concept Activation Vectors (CAVs) are a fundamental tool for concept-based explainability in deep learning, yet their practical utility is limited by statistical instability. We analyze the stochastic nature of CAVs and the Testing with CAVs (TCAV) method, deriving the distributions of major CAV classes including PatternCAV, FastCAV, and ridge regression-based CAVs. We then identify a fundamental flaw in the standard TCAV score: its reliance on a discontinuous indicator function induces non-decaying variance in critical regimes. To address this, we introduce $α$-TCAV, a generalized framework that replaces the indicator with a parameterized smooth function, yielding a unified probabilistic formulation that subsumes both TCAV and Multi-TCAV. We characterize the induced distributions of sensitivity scores and different TCAV variants, showing that established state-of-the-art choices lack theoretical justification. We provide principled guidance on tuning the parameter in $α$-TCAV -- either to imitate Multi-TCAV at substantially lower computational cost, or to obtain a calibrated Bayes-optimal probabilistic measure of a concept's influence. Finally, our analysis yields practical recommendations that challenge established routines: most notably, allocating the full sampling budget to a single CAV rather than splitting it across several.

</details>


### [134] [Unsupervised Domain Shift Detection with Interpretable Subspace Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15920)
*Sebastian Springer,Alessandro Laio*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文设计了一种用于检测和解释高维数据集中领域偏移的算法，并通过从不平衡数据中提取无偏差子集来补偿这些差异，有效提升了数据的质量和模型的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据集中常存在微小的领域偏移（domain shifts），导致模型性能下降或产生偏差，且通常难以解释其成因，迫切需要一种能够定位并减轻这些偏移的方法。

Method: 提出了一种基于高维特征空间局部密度异常检测的算法，通过识别异常最显著的特征子空间实现偏差的可解释性；并设计了一种通过提取无分布差异子集来补偿领域偏移的协议。

Result: 在受控的20维基准测试中成功验证了该方法；在心脏电图（ECG）数据集应用中，成功定位了设备引发的偏移，并筛选出平衡的样本子集，同时识别出与数据采集差异相关的特征。

Conclusion: 密度偏移检测与子空间归因提供了一个实用的框架，能够在下游建模前识别并修正数据集中潜在的群体偏差，从而提高模型的稳健性。

Abstract: We developed a tool for detecting domain shifts, namely subtle differences in the probability distributions of datasets. We identify these shifts using an algorithm designed to detect localised density anomalies in high-dimensional feature spaces. If an anomaly is present, we then identify the feature subspace in which the anomaly is most pronounced. This allows us to trace the domain shift to a small set of features, making the shift interpretable. Moreover, we provide a protocol for compensating domain shifts by extracting, from two unlabelled datasets, subsets of samples with no detectable residual distributional difference. We validate the framework on controlled 20-dimensional benchmarks with known ground truth, recovering both broad and localized shifts together with their supporting feature subspaces. We then apply it to healthy electrocardiogram (ECG) recordings represented by 782 features. In age- and sex-matched cohort comparisons differing in measurement-device composition, the method detects device-induced shifts, extracts representative subsets enriched in the imbalanced device components, and identifies ECG features associated with the acquisition contrast. These results suggest that density-shift detection and subspace attribution provide a practical framework for uncovering hidden cohort biases before downstream modelling.

</details>


### [135] [Testing properties of trees in graphical models with covariance queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15996)
*Sofiya Burova,Francisco Calvillo,Gábor Lugosi,Piotr Zwiernik*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了高维树状图模型的属性测试问题，提出了一系列随机化算法，实现在亚二次查询复杂度内高效测试树的全局结构属性（如直径、最大度、叶子数等）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维图形模型中重构整棵树计算成本高昂，因此探讨是否能以较低的查询复杂性测试树的特定全局结构属性。

Method: 采用协方差查询模型（covariance query model），为树状图的多个基础属性（叶子数量、最大度数、典型距离、直径）设计了专门的随机化测试算法。

Result: 针对树的叶子数量、最大度数、典型距离和直径等属性，给出了明确的查询复杂度上界（依赖于阈值和容差参数），证明了这些属性可以在亚二次查询次数下被有效测试。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在高维树状图模型中，全局结构属性可以比完全重构树更高效地进行测试，通过随机化算法实现了亚二次（sub-quadratic）查询复杂度。

Abstract: We consider the problem of testing properties of graphs underlying high-dimensional graphical models. We adopt the model of covariance queries introduced by Lugosi, Truszkowski, Velona, and Zwiernik (2021). We study the case when the underlying graph is a tree. The main results of the paper show that, while reconstructing the entire tree may be costly, certain global structural properties can be tested efficiently. In particular, we design randomized tests for global structural properties that use a sub-quadratic number of queries. We develop testing procedures for several fundamental properties, including the number of leaves, the maximum degree, the typical distance, and the diameter of the tree. For each property, we obtain explicit query complexity bounds that depend on the target threshold and tolerance parameters.

</details>


### [136] [Skew-adaptive conformal prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16145)
*Paulo C. Marques F.,Helton Graziadei*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种偏度自适应的分裂共形预测方法，通过学习预测误差的局部偏度，在保持理论有效性的前提下，显著提高了回归分析中预测区间的紧致度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的共形预测方法（如基于缩放评分的预测）通常假设误差分布是对称的，而忽略了预测区间在特征空间中可能存在的局部偏度问题，这限制了预测区间的效率。

Method: 提出了一种偏度自适应的分裂共形预测扩展方法。主要步骤包括：1. 采用以点预测为中心的不对称区间族；2. 利用规范（gauge）方法推导出诱导性的符合度（conformity）评分；3. 对符号化缩放残差进行反双曲正弦变换，并以此作为辅助预测模型的训练目标，学习预测误差在特征空间上的偏移；4. 开发了一种基于校准样本的估计量，用于比较新方法与传统方法的预期相对区间宽度。

Result: 实验表明，相比传统缩放评分构建方法和共形分位数回归，该方法能显著提高预测区间的效率；此外，所提出的估计量能够准确预测测试集上的平均宽度比率。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持有限样本下边际有效性的同时，能够生成有效适应局部尺度和局部偏度的预测区间，在各种数据集上展现出比传统缩放评分和共形分位数回归更高的预测效率。

Abstract: We develop a skew-adaptive extension of split conformal prediction for regression. The method starts from an asymmetric interval family centered at a point prediction and uses the gauge approach to deduce the conformity score induced by this family. The inverse hyperbolic sine transform of signed scaled residuals provides the training target for an additional predictive model, whose role is to learn how predictive uncertainty should tilt across the feature space. The resulting procedure preserves the finite-sample marginal validity of split conformal prediction under exchangeability, while producing intervals that adapt to both local scale and local skewness. We also develop a calibration-sample-based estimator for comparing the expected relative future width of the skew-adaptive and classical scaled-score intervals. Experiments on a variety of datasets indicate gains in prediction interval efficiency over the scaled-score construction and conformalized quantile regression, and show that the proposed estimator closely matches the corresponding average width ratio observed on the test sample.

</details>


### [137] [On Kernel Eigen-alignments of KRR: Reconstruction and Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15240)
*Yang Liu,Ernest Fokoue,Richard Lange,Daniel Krutz*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析核矩阵的特征对齐，揭示了核方法泛化性能的本质，提出核方法的泛化能力不仅依赖于拟合程度，更取决于核空间与目标函数的特征谱对齐质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论对于核方法泛化性能的直观解释不足，且缺乏对有限样本下特征分解稳定性与泛化性能之间定量联系的透彻研究。

Method: 基于矩阵特征值和特征向量的估计稳定性分析，探讨核岭回归（KRR）在有限样本设置下的泛化误差；建立泛化误差与核矩阵扰动之间的直接联系。

Result: 成功建立了一个基于特征值/特征向量估计的泛化边界；证明了高秩核矩阵虽能实现近零重构误差，但该误差并不直接决定泛化能力。

Conclusion: 核方法的泛化效能取决于核矩阵与学习目标之间的特征对齐（eigenalignment），强泛化能力需要更高水平的特征向量对齐度、更大的特征值幅值或更大的特征值间隙。单纯的训练误差（重构误差）在核方法中对泛化能力的预测作用有限。

Abstract: This paper investigates the critical role of eigenalignments between the kernel matrix and learning targets in achieving robust generalization in learning problems. We establish a direct connection between generalization performance in kernel methods and the estimation of eigenvectors and eigenvalues of matrices, offering a more intuitive understanding compared to prior work with minimal assumptions. We also show that, since the prediction task in KRR is essentially the weighted sum of eigenvectors/singular vectors, by analyzing how much error can be caused by perturbations to the kernel matrix, we can then derive a bound on this generalization error using the estimation stability of matrix eigenvalues and eigenvectors. Compared with previous work, our analysis concentrates on finite-sample settings and on the generalization error arising from having a suboptimal finite training set. Our findings reveal that in kernel methods, as long as the kernel is of high rank, the near-zero reconstruction error can be trivially obtained, implying that the reconstruction error will have limited predictive power for generalization. Finally, we establish a generalization bound from an eigenvalues/eigenvectors estimation perspective, showing that strong generalization requires increasing eigenvector alignment, eigenvalue magnitude, or gaps between consecutive eigenvalues.

</details>


### [138] [Harnessing Unimodality in Semiparametric Contextual Pricing via Oracle Price Map Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15411)
*Yingying Fan,Yuxuan Han,Jinchi Lv,Xiaocong Xu,Zhengyuan Zhou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ORBIT的算法，用于解决半参数上下文动态定价问题。通过将复杂的上下文依赖降维为一维标量预言图，该方法在保证学习效率的同时实现了极小化极大最优的后悔界，并可平滑扩展至多种效用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在上下文动态定价中，通常假设估值模型为参数化形式，但实际中函数形式往往未知。本文旨在解决更通用的半参数标量指标模型（$v_t=μ_\ast(\mathsf c_t)+ξ_t$）下的定价挑战，避开对误差分布的严格假设，并克服维数灾难。

Method: 提出了一种模块化的从粗到细（coarse-to-fine）的策略ORBIT。该方法利用标量先导（scalar pilot）信息，在活动区间内通过局部多项式逼近和带约束的凸优化策略，对预言价格图（oracle map）进行学习；对于线性效用模型，结合自适应椭圆探索（adaptive elliptical exploration）来在线构建标量先导。

Result: 该策略实现了 $\widetilde{O}\big(T^{\frac{2β-1}{4β-3}}+\sqrt{dT}\big)$ 的后悔界。在固定维度 $d$ 的条件下，该结果在时间视界（horizon）依赖度上与下界匹配，证明了其在非参数学习项上的最优性。此外，该框架可推广至稀疏高维线性效用和非参数Hölder效用模型。

Conclusion: 本文提出的ORBIT算法在半参数标量指标估值模型下实现了理论最优的后悔界。通过标量先导策略将高维决策转化为一维非参数估计，证明了在给定维度下，该策略的学习效率达到了极小化极大(minimax)最优。

Abstract: We study contextual dynamic pricing in a semiparametric scalar-index valuation model where the latent value is $v_t=μ_\ast(\mathsf c_t)+ξ_t$, with an unknown utility map $μ_\ast$ and an unknown additive noise distribution. The key decision object is the one-dimensional oracle price map $u\mapsto p^\ast(u)$ induced by the scalar index $u=μ_\ast(\mathsf c)$ and the noise tail. Under the $β$-Hölder smoothness of the tail function for $β\geq 2$ and a revenue-geometry condition that gives a unique, stable, interior maximizer, this oracle map is itself $(β-1)$-smooth. We exploit such structure through $\mathsf{ORBIT}$, a modular coarse-to-fine policy that takes a scalar pilot index as input, localizes a benchmark price in each active bin, and learns a local polynomial approximation of the oracle map inside a trust region via bandit convex optimization. For the baseline linear utility model $μ_\ast(\mathsf c)=\mathsf c^\topθ_\ast$, an adaptive elliptical exploration scheme constructs the required scalar pilot online without distributional assumptions on the contexts. The resulting policy achieves regret $\widetilde{O}\big(T^{\frac{2β-1}{4β-3}}+\sqrt{dT}\big)$. For fixed $d$, we establish a matching lower bound in the horizon dependence, unveiling that the nonparametric oracle-map learning term is minimax sharp. The same scalar-pilot interface also yields extensions to sparse high-dimensional linear utility and nonparametric Hölder utility.

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [Heuristic-Based Merging of HPC Traces to Extend Hardware Counter Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15832)
*Júlia Orteu Aubach,Fabio Banchelli,Marc Clascà Ramírez,Marta Garcia-Gasulla*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于启发式算法的执行轨迹合并方法，通过对齐多次运行的监控数据，解决了HPC性能分析中硬件计数器数量受限的问题，实现了在不使用复用技术的前提下获取更丰富的性能特征集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件计数器（hardware counters）的同时采集数量受到物理限制，导致性能建模时难以获取全面的硬件特征，从而制约了机器学习模型在HPC负载性能预测中的表现。

Method: 提出了一种基于启发式的轨迹合并方法（heuristic-based methodology），通过分析MPI结构、执行时间和通信模式，将不同运行批次（每个批次开启不同硬件计数器）产生的执行轨迹（execution traces）进行对齐与融合。

Result: 在MareNostrum5超级计算机上通过多种内核和实际应用进行了验证，表明合并后的计数器数据可以有效且直接地用于训练机器学习模型，且无需进行预先的特征选择。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效地在不依赖硬件复用（multiplexing）的情况下构建包含丰富硬件特征的统一数据集，为HPC性能预测提供了更稳健的特征空间，且对于多数应用仍能保持可接受的预测准确性。

Abstract: This work extends a framework for predicting the performance of High-Performance Computing (HPC) workloads using Machine Learning (ML). A common limitation in performance modeling is the restricted number of hardware counters that can be collected simultaneously. To address this, we propose a heuristic-based methodology to merge execution traces from multiple runs, each instrumented with a different set of hardware counters. Our approach matches computation bursts across executions by analyzing MPI structure, timing, and communication patterns. This process enables the construction of a unified dataset that includes a wider set of hardware features without relying on multiplexing. The output is a new synthetic trace with all merged counters, which can be used both for HPC performance prediction and for conventional performance analysis. The methodology has been validated on MareNostrum5 machine with a range of kernels and real applications. Results show that the merged counters maintain acceptable accuracy depending on the application, and can be directly used to train ML models on a richer feature space without prior counter selection.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [140] [DeepSlide: From Artifacts to Presentation Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15202)
*Ming Yang,Zhiwei Zhang,Jiahang Li,Haoseng Liu,Yuzheng Cai,Weiguo Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeepSlide是一个专注于全程演示准备（从规划到排练）的多智能体系统，不仅能生成高质量幻灯片，更通过引入时间规划和交付优化机制，解决了传统AI工具忽视演讲过程的痛点，显著提升了学术演示的交付质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI PPT生成工具多关注“幻灯片成品”的视觉层面，而忽略了学术演示中至关重要的交付过程，如节奏控制、叙事逻辑和演讲准备。

Method: 提出了名为DeepSlide的“人在回路”多智能体系统，核心组件包括：(1) 可控逻辑链规划器（支持时间预算分配）；(2) 轻量级内容树检索器（用于基于证据的生成）；(3) 马尔可夫式序列渲染（实现样式继承）；(4) 沙盒执行与最小化修复机制（确保渲染稳定性）。此外，还构建了一个区分静态产物质量与动态交付表现的双分分指标体系。

Result: DeepSlide在20个领域及多类观众设置下进行了测试，结果显示其在产物质量上与基准模型持平，但在叙事流程、节奏精准度、幻灯片与讲稿的协同性以及注意力引导等方面表现出显著优势。

Conclusion: DeepSlide证明了通过人机协作和多智能体系统，可以有效优化学术演示的交付过程，不仅提升了幻灯片的视觉质量，还显著改善了叙事逻辑、节奏控制和演讲准备的协同效应。

Abstract: Presentations are a primary medium for scholarly communication, yet most AI slide generators optimize the artifact (a visually plausible deck) while under-optimizing the delivery process (pacing, narrative, and presentation preparation). We present DeepSlide, a human-in-the-loop multi-agent system that supports preparing the full presentation process, from requirement elicitation and time-budgeted narrative planning, to evidence-grounded slide--script generation, attention augmentation, and rehearsal support. DeepSlide integrates (i) a controllable logical-chain planner with per-node time budgets, (ii) a lightweight content-tree retriever for grounding, (iii) Markov-style sequential rendering with style inheritance, and (iv) sandboxed execution with minimal repair to ensure renderability. We further introduce a dual-scoreboard benchmark that cleanly separates static artifact quality from dynamic delivery excellence. Across 20 domains and diverse audience profiles, DeepSlide matches strong baselines on artifact quality while consistently achieving larger gains on delivery metrics, improving narrative flow, pacing precision, and slide--script synergy with clearer attention guidance.

</details>


### [141] [Does Theory of Mind Improvement Really Benefit Human-AI Interactions? Empirical Findings from Interactive Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15205)
*Nanxu Gong,Zixin Chen,Haotian Li,Zishu Zhao,Jianxun Lian,Huamin Qu,Yanjie Fu,Xing Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出目前大模型心理理论（ToM）评估范式存在局限性，提出了针对动态人机交互的评估新框架，并发现现有ToM增强技术在实际交互场景中的有效性与静态评估结果表现不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ToM评估多采用第三人称静态故事阅读或多项选择题，忽略了人机交互中第一人称、动态且开放式的特性，导致技术改进在实际应用中效果存疑。

Method: 1. 提出了一种包含视角转换和度量转换的交互式ToM评估新范式。2. 对比了四种典型ToM增强技术，通过四个真实世界数据集及用户研究（涵盖目标导向和体验导向任务）进行系统性评估。

Result: 研究发现：在静态基准测试中表现良好的ToM增强技术，在动态交互场景中并不总是能提供同等程度的性能提升。

Conclusion: 现有的静态基准测试无法完全代表大模型在动态人机交互（HAI）中的心理理论（ToM）表现，交互式评估对于开发社会化大模型至关重要。

Abstract: Improving the Theory of Mind (ToM) capability of Large Language Models (LLMs) is crucial for effective social interactions between these AI models and humans. However, the existing benchmarks often measure ToM capability improvement through story-reading, multiple-choice questions from a third-person perspective, while ignoring the first-person, dynamic, and open-ended nature of human-AI (HAI) interactions. To directly examine how ToM improvement techniques benefit HAI interactions, we first proposed the new paradigm of interactive ToM evaluation with both perspective and metric shifts. Next, following the paradigm, we conducted a systematic study of four representative ToM enhancement techniques using both four real-world datasets and a user study, covering both goal-oriented tasks (e.g., coding, math) and experience-oriented tasks (e.g., counseling). Our findings reveal that improvements on static benchmarks do not always translate to better performance in dynamic HAI interactions. This paper offers critical insights into ToM evaluation, showing the necessity of interaction-based assessments in developing next-generation, socially aware LLMs for HAI symbiosis.

</details>


### [142] [SkillSmith: Compiling Agent Skills into Boundary-Guided Runtime Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15215)
*Duling Xu,Zheng Chen,Zaifeng Pan,Jiawei Guan,Dong Dong,Jialin Li,Bangzheng Pu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillSmith 是一种优化 LLM 智能体技能调用的编译器框架，通过离线编译提取技能执行边界，有效减少了运行时的冗余推理和上下文开销，大幅提升了执行效率并降低了成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 智能体技能调用范式存在冗余问题：注入不相关的上下文，以及由于反复进行技能特定的推理和规划导致的计算资源浪费。

Method: 提出了一种“边界优先（boundary-first）”的编译器-运行时框架，将原始技能离线编译为精简的可执行接口。通过提取细粒度的操作边界，实现运行时仅调用相关组件，从而减少上下文冗余和推理开销。

Result: 在 SkillsBench 基准测试中，与原始技能相比，SkillSmith 将解决阶段的 Token 使用量减少了 57.44%，推理迭代次数减少了 42.99%，解决时间缩短了 50.57%（速度提升 2.02 倍），且支持强模型编译产物由弱模型复用，提升了任务准确率。

Conclusion: SkillSmith 通过编译优化技能，显著提升了 LLM 智能体的推理效率和成本效益，并实现了模型间的技能复用，证明了“边界优先”范式在处理复杂技能任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Recently, skills have been widely adopted in large language model (LLM)-based agent systems across various domains. In existing frameworks, skills are typically injected into the agent reasoning loop as contextual guidance once matched to a runtime task, enabling specialized task-solving capabilities. We find that this execution paradigm introduces two major sources of redundancy: irrelevant context injection and repeated skill-specific reasoning and planning. To this end, we propose SkillSmith, a boundary-first compiler-runtime framework that compiles skill packages offline into minimal executable interfaces. By extracting fine-grained operational boundaries from skills, SkillSmith enables agents to dynamically access and execute only the relevant components at runtime, thereby minimizing unnecessary context injection and redundant reasoning overhead. In the evaluation on SkillsBench benchmark, SkillSmith reduces solve-stage token usage by 57.44%, thinking iterations by 42.99%, solve time by 50.57% (2.02x faster), and token-proportional monetary cost by 57.44% compared with using raw-skills. Moreover, compiled artifacts produced by a stronger model can be reused by a smaller or more efficient runtime model, improving task accuracy in cases where raw skill interpretation fails. The source code and data are available at https://github.com/AetherHeart-AI/Aeloon.

</details>


### [143] [Fair outputs, Biased Internals: Causal Potency and Asymmetry of Latent Bias in LLMs for High-Stakes Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15217)
*Jagdish Tripathy,Marcus Buckmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 指令微调模型虽然在外在决策上显示公平，但内部仍残留并放大了种族偏差。研究发现这些潜藏偏差具有强大的因果影响且具有不对称性，简单的输出行为审计无法发现这些可被轻易触发的隐蔽歧视风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在揭示指令微调语言模型（IT-LLM）在高风险决策中虽然在输出端表现出公平，但其内部是否仍保留并能被触发利用种族偏差，以及这种偏差在不同人口统计学群体间是否存在非对称性的因果效力。

Method: 使用开放权重模型模拟抵押贷款承销任务，通过匹配申请（仅种族相关姓名不同）进行实验；应用激活引导（Activation Steering）和跨层干预（Cross-layer Interventions）技术来探究隐藏表征对决策的影响；并测试了对抗性提示工程及参数高效微调（PEFT）对潜藏偏差的触发作用。

Result: 模型在输出端表现公平，但在内部表征中保留并放大了种族偏差；隐藏的偏差具有高度因果相关性，将其注入特定层可导致决策近乎完全反转；偏差具有不对称性，且易受对抗性攻击和微调干扰。

Conclusion: 仅依靠由于输出层行为审计（Behavioral Audits）不足以评估AI的公平性。研究强调了在处理高风险决策任务时，必须结合“输出评估”与“表征分析”的双层测试框架，以防范被隐藏或可引导的内部偏差。

Abstract: Instruction-tuned language models exhibit behavioural fairness in high-stakes decisions while retaining biased associations in their internal representations. However, whether these suppressed representations can affect model outputs - and whether such causal potency is symmetric across demographic groups - remains unknown. We investigate the use of open-weight models for mortgage underwriting using matched applications that differ only in racially-associated names and reveal a critical disconnect: models show no output-level bias, yet retain and amplify demographic representations across model layers. Through activation steering and novel cross-layer interventions, we demonstrate that this suppressed information is decision-relevant: when reinjected at critical layers, it produces near-complete decision reversals. Critically, this latent bias is asymmetric - steering interventions affect decisions in one demographic direction, while producing minimal effects in reverse - and susceptible to adversarial prompt engineering and parameter-efficient fine-tuning. These findings demonstrate that behavioural audits focused on outputs are insufficient: fair outputs can mask exploitable internal biases. They also motivate dual-layer testing frameworks combining output evaluation with representational analysis for AI governance in high-stakes decisions.

</details>


### [144] [CAX-Agent: A Lightweight Agent Harness for Reliable APDL Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15218)
*Chenying Lin,Yichen Hai,Yi He,Ran Wang,Haiyan Qiang,Liang Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 CAX-Agent，一种专门用于 MAPDL 仿真的轻量级智能体编排框架，通过分层架构和模型驱动的故障恢复策略，解决了 LLM 自动化仿真中的可靠性难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型（LLM）驱动 MAPDL 有限元仿真任务中，缺乏结构化执行控制、工具封装和故障恢复机制，导致输出不一致和任务失败，严重制约了实际工程应用。

Method: 设计并实现了一个三层架构的 agent harness（CAX-Agent），包括 LLM 服务层、Agent 编排层和求解器后端。提出并评估了三种故障恢复策略：无恢复（no_recovery）、纯规则修复（rule_only）和模型驱动修复（model_only），在 50 个结构基准测试上进行了 450 次重复实验。

Result: 模型驱动恢复（model_only）策略表现最佳，任务完成率达到 0.9267，任务得分为 3.59/4，且实现了 0.84 的无需人工干预率，在各项指标上具有统计学显著优势。

Conclusion: CAX-Agent 的分层架构和基于模型驱动的恢复策略显著提高了 MAPDL 有限元仿真的可靠性与自动化水平。实验证明，模型驱动恢复（model_only）在任务完成率和执行质量上均优于规则驱动恢复和无恢复方案，是实现稳定仿真自动化的关键技术路径。

Abstract: Large language models deployed for MAPDL finite-element simulation face practical reliability challenges: without structured execution control, tool encapsulation, and fault recovery, outputs may be inconsistent and task failures are common. The Agent Harness paradigm addresses this by inserting domain-specific orchestration middleware that manages tool lifecycles, workflow state, and recovery escalation. This paper presents the architecture of CAX-Agent, a lightweight agent harness purpose-built for MAPDL automation, and empirically evaluates one of its core components -- the recovery policy.CAX-Agent organizes execution into three layers -- LLM service, agent harness, and solver backend -- with a recovery ladder that escalates from deterministic rule patching through model-driven regeneration to context enrichment and human intervention. We evaluate three recovery strategies (no_recovery, rule_only, and model_only) on 50 standard structural benchmarks with three repeated runs per strategy (450 case-runs total). Two independent human raters score task completion under blind conditions; inter-rater agreement is strong (quadratic weighted Cohen's kappa = 0.84, 96 percent of score pairs within one point). Model_only achieves the best completion rate (0.9267), task score (3.59/4), total score (9.16/10), and zero-intervention rate (0.84), outperforming rule_only (0.7733, 3.17/4, 7.03/10, 0.00) and no_recovery (0.6933, 2.74/4, 5.60/10, 0.00) with large effect sizes (Cliff's delta = 0.81-0.87). The benchmark uses deliberately simple geometries to isolate recovery-policy effects; we discuss the scope of these findings and directions for broader validation.

</details>


### [145] [NOVA: Fundamental Limits of Knowledge Discovery Through AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15219)
*Salman Avestimehr,Ken Duffy,Muriel Médard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了NOVA框架来分析AI迭代自改进发现知识的过程，证明了随着发现深入，成本呈幂律增长，并揭示了模型验证误差产生的“污染陷阱”以及人类引导对突破探索瓶颈的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI系统通过迭代自改进发现新知识的可能性，评估其长期性能上限、失败风险及由此产生的计算成本。

Method: 提出NOVA框架，将迭代过程建模为知识空间上的自适应采样过程；通过数学建模分析失效模式（污染、遗忘等）；引入Good-Turing估计的局限性分析；基于Zipf定律推导发现成本的缩放律（Scaling Law）。

Result: 证明了当发现D个新知识时，累积生成成本满足 $R_{\mathrm{cum}}(D)=Θ(c_{\mathrm{gen}}D^α)$ 的缩放律；揭示了验证误差导致知识库污染的必然性；明确了人类引导在突破自主探索瓶颈中的关键作用。

Conclusion: AI系统通过迭代自改进发现新知识存在明显的边际效用递减。长期探索面临“污染陷阱”（模型验证不完美导致无效信息积累），且人类专家的介入在克服自主探索瓶颈时最具价值。

Abstract: Can AI systems discover genuinely new knowledge through iterative self improvement, and if so, at what cost? We introduce the NOVA framework, which models the common ``generate, verify, accumulate, retrain'' loop as an adaptive sampling process over a knowledge space. We identify sufficient conditions under which accumulated genuine knowledge eventually covers a finite domain, and show how their violations produce distinct failure modes: contamination, forgetting, exploration failure, and acceptance failure. We then analyze imperfect verification and identify a contamination trap: as easy-to-find knowledge is exhausted, the model mass assigned to new valid artifacts shrinks, so even small false-positive rates can cause invalid artifacts to enter the knowledge base faster than genuine discoveries. We clarify that Good--Turing estimation is a local batch-diversity diagnostic, not an estimator of the historically undiscovered valid mass that governs long-term discovery. Under a separate tail-equivalence assumption relating the model's effective discovery distribution to a Zipf law with exponent $α>1$, we prove that the cumulative generation cost required to obtain $D$ distinct genuine discoveries satisfies $R_{\mathrm{cum}}(D)=Θ(c_{\mathrm{gen}}D^α)$, where $c_{\mathrm{gen}}$ is the per-candidate generation cost. This scaling law quantifies asymptotic diminishing returns as the discovery frontier advances. Finally, we formalize human amplification through guidance, generation, and verification, explaining why expert input is most valuable near autonomous exploration barriers.

</details>


### [146] [Zero-Shot Goal Recognition with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15333)
*Kin Max Piamolini Gusmão,Nathan Gavenski,Nir Oren,Felipe Meneguzzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究通过将目标识别作为基准，发现 LLM 在执行规划推理时，其表现显著受限于处理新证据的能力，而非仅仅是缺乏相关领域知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在规划任务上的成功往往依赖于外部世界知识而非真正的符号推理。目标识别任务（即评估观察到的行为序列是否与特定目标一致）比生成动作序列更符合 LLM 的特长，旨在通过该任务剖析模型的推理本质。

Method: 对前沿大语言模型进行系统性的零样本（zero-shot）自动目标识别评估，测试其在经典 PDDL（规划领域定义语言）基准上的表现。

Result: LLM 在目标识别上的表现参差不齐：部分模型能随证据增多而提高准确率，甚至达到基准里程碑水平；另一些模型则完全依赖先验知识，无法利用新的观测证据。定性分析证实，这种分歧取决于模型的证据整合能力。

Conclusion: 目标识别（Goal Recognition）是评估大语言模型（LLM）基础规划能力的规范化基准；模型的表现差异源于其证据整合机制的根本区别，而非简单的知识先验限制。

Abstract: Large language models have recently reached near-parity with classical planners on well-known planning domains, yet this competence relies on world-knowledge exploitation rather than genuine symbolic reasoning. Goal recognition is a complementary abductive task structurally better suited to LLM strengths: it consists of evaluating consistency with world knowledge rather than generating novel action sequences. This paper provides the first systematic zero-shot evaluation of frontier LLMs as goal recognisers on key classical PDDL benchmarks. Our results show that LLM competence on goal recognition is uneven: some models scale with evidence and approach landmark-based accuracy at full observations, while others remain anchored to world-knowledge priors regardless of how much evidence accumulates. Qualitative analysis of model reasoning traces reveals that this divergence reflects a fundamental difference in evidence integration rather than domain familiarity. These findings position goal recognition as a principled benchmark for the foundational planning knowledge of LLMs.

</details>


### [147] [Ensemble Monitoring for AI Control: Diverse Signals Outweigh More Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15377)
*Eugene Koran,Yejun Yun,Samantha Tetef,Benjamin Arnav,Pablo Bernabeu-Pérez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究证明，通过对多个微调及提示词驱动的GPT-4.1-Mini监测器进行集成，利用模型多样性而非仅靠扩大规模，可显著提升对智能体错误行为的识别能力，这为AI安全监测提供了一种低成本且高效的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体规模化部署，传统人工监督难以满足安全需求，且构建可靠的单体监测器难度较大，需要一种更稳健的监测方案来识别智能体的失效或不当行为。

Method: 构建了12个采用提示词驱动（prompting）与微调（fine-tuning）策略的GPT-4.1-Mini监测器，并在能够通过标准测试但易受对抗性攻击的编码任务中进行评估。通过对不同监测器组合进行集成，对比个体、同质集成与多样性集成的性能差异。

Result: 多样性集成表现优于传统方法，最佳的三监测器集成在性能增益上实现了同质集成（使用3个相同监测器）的2.4倍。此外，微调技术能赋予监测器提示词无法达到的独特检出能力，且在分布外（OOD）数据集上保持领先。

Conclusion: 多样性（而非单纯的规模扩大）是提升监测性能的关键。结合强性能个体与低相关性的监测器集成策略，是实现高效AI安全管控的实用路径，其中微调后的监测器具有不可替代的能力优势。

Abstract: As AI systems are increasingly deployed in autonomous agentic settings at scale, it is important to ensure the actions they take are safe and aligned with user intent. Monitoring agent actions is a key safety mechanism, yet reliable monitors remain difficult to build and the scale of these systems makes human oversight impractical. We show that combining signals from diverse monitors into an ensemble improves detection of misaligned actions. We build 12 GPT-4.1-Mini monitors using both prompting and fine-tuning strategies. We evaluate them on coding tasks where candidate solutions pass standard tests but fail on adversarial inputs. In this setting, diverse ensembles outperform both individual monitors and homogeneous ensembles. Our best 3-monitor ensemble achieves 2.4x greater detection performance gain compared to an ensemble composed of three identical monitors, with the same ensemble performing strongly on an independent dataset. We contend that these results show that diversity - not scale - drives gains. The best ensembles combine strong individual performance with low correlation between monitors. Furthermore, fine-tuned monitors appear in every top-performing ensemble and maintain this advantage on out-of-distribution attack types, suggesting that fine-tuning enables detection capabilities that prompting alone does not elicit. These results support ensemble monitoring as a practical AI control strategy for safety gains at reasonable inference costs.

</details>


### [148] [From LLM-Generated Conjectures to Lean Formalizations: Automated Polynomial Inequality Proving via Sum-of-Squares Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15445)
*Ruobing Zuo,Hanrui Zhao,Gaolei He,Zhengfeng Yang,Jianlin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NSPI 是一种神经符号框架，通过“LLM 提出近似 SOS 分解+符号方法精化验证+Lean 机器核验证”的流水线，高效解决大规模多项式不等式的证明难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的纯符号法在处理多变量、高次多项式时计算复杂度爆炸，扩展性差；而单纯依赖 LLM 的方法在需要严谨证明的数学任务中缺乏可靠性。需结合两者的优势以解决大规模多项式不等式的自动证明问题。

Method: 提出了 NSPI 神经符号框架：首先利用 LLM 提出近似的 SOS（平方和）分解建议，随后通过符号计算将其精化为精确的 SOS 表示，最后利用 Lean 证明助手生成可经机器验证的证明。

Result: 在包含多达 10 个变量的具有挑战性的基准测试中，NSPI 展示了良好的有效性和扩展性，实现了从启发式搜索到机器检验证明的端到端自动化。

Conclusion: NSPI 通过结合大语言模型的启发式搜索能力与符号计算的确定性，成功构建了一个可扩展的、经过机器验证的多项式不等式证明流水线，在参数规模较大的问题上表现优异。

Abstract: Automated proving of polynomial inequalities is a fundamental challenge in automated mathematical reasoning, where rich algebraic structure and a rapidly growing certificate search space hinder scalability. Purely symbolic approaches provide strong guarantees but often scale poorly as the number of variables or the degree increases, due to expensive algebraic manipulations and rapidly growing intermediate expressions. In parallel, LLM-guided methods have made notable progress, particularly on competition-style inequalities with a small number of variables. To address the remaining scalability challenges, we propose NSPI, a neuro-symbolic framework that combines the complementary strengths of LLMs and symbolic computation for polynomial-inequality proving.
