Concretely, an LLM proposes a conjecture in the form of an approximate polynomial Sum-Of-Squares (SOS) decomposition; we refine it via symbolic computation to obtain an exact polynomial SOS representation, which directly proves the target inequality, and we further certify the proof in Lean, yielding an end-to-end pipeline from heuristic discovery to machine-checked proof. Experiments on challenging benchmarks involving polynomials with up to 10 variables demonstrate the effectiveness and scalability of the proposed method.

</details>


### [149] [CAPS: Cascaded Adaptive Pairwise Selection for Efficient Parallel Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15513)
*Fangzhou Lin,Shuo Xing,Peiran Li,Siyuan Yang,Qianwen Ge,Kazunori Yamada,Ziming Zhang,Haichong Zhang,Zhengzhong Tu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CAPS通过级联自适应策略，根据候选解的价值动态分配验证资源，在显著削减推理计算成本（约75%的冗余预算）的同时，提升了模型在大规模数学和代码任务中的推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的成对自验证（Pairwise self-verification）方法在推理阶段计算成本极高，因为它们对每对候选解都进行全量阅读，且未能区分判断的必要性，导致计算资源浪费。因此需要一种更高效、更具选择性的验证框架。

Method: 提出了一种名为CAPS（Cascaded Adaptive Pairwise Selection）的推理框架。其核心机制包括两个维度：1. 证据轴（Evidence axis）：根据需要动态调整验证器查看候选解的比例；2. 分布轴（Distribution axis）：自适应分配不同候选解之间的比较频次。并将此过程实例化为包含四个阶段的级联模型，辅以可选的“抢救”子程序。

Result: 在Qwen3和GPT-OSS系列模型及5个数学代码基准测试中，CAPS在20个测试集中的14个表现优于最先进的成对验证器，同时在代码任务上仅使用了25.4%的验证Token预算，并在所有20个测试集中均优于点状自验证（Pointwise self-verification）。

Conclusion: CAPS架构通过非均匀的计算资源分配策略，显著提升了推理阶段的成本效益。实验证明，该方法在保持或超过主流推理性能的同时，大幅降低了验证阶段所需的Token消耗，为大规模语言模型的高效推理提供了可行的优化路径。

Abstract: Parallel reasoning, where a generator samples many candidate solutions and an aggregator selects the best, is one of the most effective forms of test-time scaling in large language models, and pairwise self-verification has become its strongest aggregation primitive. Yet pairwise verification carries a heavy cost: each judgment reads two complete solutions in full, and existing methods perform tens of such judgments per problem regardless of whether the comparison is informative. We introduce CAPS (Cascaded Adaptive Pairwise Selection), an inference-only framework that allocates verifier compute non-uniformly along two orthogonal axes: an evidence axis that adapts how much of each candidate the judge sees, and a distribution axis that adapts how comparisons are spread across the pool. CAPS instantiates these into a four-stage cascade with an optional rescue subroutine, and admits a closed-form verifier-token cost in which the per-candidate marginal cost is roughly halved relative to uniform full-evidence schedules. On four self-verifying models (Qwen3-14B, GPT-OSS-20B, Qwen3-4B-Instruct/Thinking) and five reasoning benchmarks spanning code (LiveCodeBench-v5/v6, CodeContests) and math (AIME 2025, HMMT 2025), CAPS outperforms the leading pairwise verifier on 14 of 20 suites while using 25.4% of its verifier-token budget on code, and outperforms pointwise self-verification on all 20. The trade-off suites admit an interpretable diagnostic in terms of the verifier's accuracy at partial versus full evidence, providing a concrete pre-deployment check for cascade suitability.

</details>


### [150] [Position: Artificial Intelligence Needs Meta Intelligence -- the Case for Metacognitive AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15567)
*Sergei Chuprov,Richard D. Lange,Leon Reznik,Paulo Shakarian,Raman Zatsarenko,Dmitrii Korobeinikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将元认知（自我监控与资源动态分配）作为提升AI准确性、安全性和效率的核心设计原则，验证了其在联邦学习中的有效性，并提供了一套实现该原则的软件框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI系统缺乏基于复杂度和误差成本的自我评估机制，导致在资源分配和决策准确性上存在局限。本文旨在通过借鉴人类的元认知能力，使AI系统能够根据具体任务动态调整策略，从而在复杂动态环境中表现更好。

Method: 提出将心理学与认知科学中的元认知策略系统性地嵌入AI设计；引入基于资源合理性（Resource-rational）的动态资源分配机制；设计了一套用于实验和实现元认知AI的软件框架，并在联邦学习（FL）场景中进行了实证评估。

Result: 在联邦学习案例研究中，证明了元认知策略能显著提升学习效率、模型准确性及安全性。开发了一套开源软件框架，为社区研究和部署元认知AI提供了可行途径。

Conclusion: 元认知（Metacognition）作为一种通用的设计原则，能够有效提升人工智能系统的准确性、安全性和计算效率。通过引入系统自我监控与资源动态分配机制，未来AI系统将具备更强的自适应能力和鲁棒性。

Abstract: This position paper argues for metacognition as a general design principle for creating more accurate, secure, and efficient AI. The metacognitive solution involves systems monitoring their own states and judiciously allocating resources depending on each problem instance's difficulty or cost of mistakes. Drawing inspiration both from past work on resource-rational AI and from well-documented metacognitive strategies in psychology and cognitive science, we identify specific challenges in embedding these strategies into AI design and highlight open theoretical and implementation problems. We showcase these principles through a tangible example of improved learning efficiency, effectiveness, and security in a Federated Learning (FL) case study. We show how these principles can be translated into practice with a novel software framework developed specifically to allow the community to design, deploy, and experiment with metacognition-enabled AI applications.

</details>


### [151] [STAR: A Stage-attributed Triage and Repair framework for RCA Agents in Microservices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15581)
*Junle Wang,Xingchuang Liao,Wenjun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出STAR框架，通过将长链式根因分析（RCA）过程解耦为四个可审计阶段，利用反事实评估实现错误定位与局部修复，解决了LLM智能体在故障诊断中推理误差堆叠的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的根因分析（RCA）智能体在处理微服务架构中的故障时，推理过程中的误差会不断传播导致最终诊断失败，缺乏对中间环节错误进行精确定位和修复的能力。

Method: 提出“STAR”框架，将RCA工作流分解为四个结构化阶段（证据包、假设集、分析结构、决策报告），并结合LangGraph实现阶段审计、预算感知的“快/慢路径路由”、基于反事实候选评估的阶段定位以及阶段特定的修补与重放机制。

Result: 在公开基准测试和真实生产数据集上的实验表明，STAR显著提升了根因定位和故障类型分类的准确率；能够高精度定位故障阶段，且通常在1-2轮重放内完成修复。

Conclusion: 通过显式地对根因分析（RCA）推理链路进行阶段化建模和错误本地化，可以有效提升大模型智能体在故障诊断任务中的可靠性、可调试性和自我修复能力。

Abstract: LLM-based root cause analysis (RCA) agents have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for incident diagnosis in microservice AIOps. However, their reliability remains fragile: an error in early evidence collection, hypothesis formulation, or causal analysis can propagate through the reasoning trace and eventually corrupt the final diagnosis. In this paper, we present \textbf{STAR}, a \emph{Stage-attributed Triage and Repair} framework for repairing erroneous RCA traces. STAR explicitly decomposes an RCA workflow into four structured stages, namely \emph{Evidence Package} (EP), \emph{Hypothesis Set} (HS), \emph{Analysis Structure} (AS), and \emph{Decision Report} (DR), and treats agent failure as a stage-localizable reasoning bug rather than a monolithic end-to-end error. Built on top of LangGraph, STAR performs stage-wise auditing, budget-aware \emph{Fast/Slow Routing}, \emph{decisive stage localization via counterfactual candidate evaluation}, and stage-specific patch-and-replay repair.
  We evaluate STAR on a public large-scale benchmark and a real-world production dataset, using two RCA agent workflows and three foundation models. Experimental results show that STAR consistently improves both root cause localization and fault type classification over strong baselines. Moreover, STAR identifies the decisive faulty stage with high accuracy, repairs most initially incorrect traces within one or two replay rounds, and benefits substantially from both Fast/Slow Routing and counterfactual stage evaluation. These results suggest that explicitly modeling \emph{where} an RCA agent fails is an effective path toward reliable, debuggable, and self-repairing agentic RCA systems.

</details>


### [152] [See Before You Code: Learning Visual Priors for Spatially Aware Educational Animation Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15585)
*Yuejia Li,Ke He,Junheng Li,Shutong Chen,Jingkang Xia,Zhiyue Su,Junchi Zhang,Mang Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对大模型生成教育动画中常见的视觉缺陷，OmniManim 通过引入视觉反馈循环和显式视觉规划机制，实现了高质量、符合教育要求的动画代码生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型生成的代码虽可执行，但常出现元素重叠、对齐错误及动画连续性中断等视觉缺陷，且这些缺陷仅在渲染后可见，单纯依靠代码生成难以解决。

Method: 提出名为 OmniManim 的框架，包含：1. Vision Agent（基于粗到细边界框去噪和插值感知优化的视觉规划模块）；2. 共享场景状态管理；3. 结构化渲染后诊断与局部修复机制。此外构建了 ManimLayout-1K 和 EduRequire-500 数据集进行评估。

Result: 在 EduRequire-500 数据集上，OmniManim 在可执行性、教学质量和视觉质量方面均优于现有的单模型基线和多智能体框架。消融实验证实了显式视觉规划（特别是空间先验和边界框优化）的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过引入视觉规划（Vision Agent）、显式场景状态控制、渲染后诊断及局部修复机制，OmniManim 有效解决了大模型生成教育动画时的视觉缺陷问题，其提出的显式视觉规划策略对提升渲染质量至关重要。

Abstract: Large language models can generate executable code for educational animations, but the resulting renders often exhibit visual defects, including element overlap, misalignment, and broken animation continuity. These defects cannot be reliably detected from the code alone and become apparent only after execution. We formalize this problem as render-feedback-aware constrained code generation: given a natural language specification, the model must generate executable code whose rendered output satisfies structured quality criteria that can be evaluated only after rendering. To address this problem, we introduce OmniManim, a render-feedback-aware educational animation generation framework built around a shared scene state, explicit visual planning, structured post-render diagnostics, and localized repair. Within OmniManim, the Vision Agent is a task-specific visual planning module: it predicts sparse keyframe layouts with coarse-to-fine bounding-box denoising and optimizes an interpolation-aware objective to reduce intermediate-frame failures induced by downstream animation interpolation. We further construct two datasets, ManimLayout-1K and EduRequire-500, and provide a reproducible evaluation protocol covering executability, instructional quality, visual quality, and efficiency. On EduRequire-500, OmniManim improves measured render quality over both single-model baselines and existing multi-agent frameworks. Systematic ablation studies further verify that explicit visual planning, especially its coarse spatial prior, bounding-box refinement, and interpolation-aware optimization, is central to these gains.

</details>


### [153] [TopoEvo: A Topology-Aware Self-Evolving Multi-Agent Framework for Root Cause Analysis in Microservices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15611)
*Junle Wang,Xingchuang Liao,Wenjun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TopoEvo 是一种针对微服务的根因分析框架，通过拓扑感知、多模态对齐和多智能体协同推理，消除了复杂系统中的症状放大偏见并适应了系统动态漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微服务根因分析（RCA）在处理多模态数据噪声、级联故障传播引发的“症状放大偏差”以及动态拓扑漂移时表现不佳，且现有 LLM 方案往往忽略拓扑结构。

Method: 1. MOMA（度量正交多模态对齐）：将度量嵌入分解，并与日志、链路追踪对齐以减少冗余；2. 向量量化（VQ）：将拓扑状态离散化为“症状令牌”；3. HET 工作流：基于假设-证据-测试的多智能体协作验证因果关系；4. 自我进化机制：利用 incident 记忆和伪标签实现测试时自适应。

Result: 该框架能够有效区分初始异常与下游传播症状，通过拓扑约束显著提高了根因定位的准确性，并能适应微服务的动态环境变化。

Conclusion: TopoEvo 提出了一种拓扑感知、自我进化的多智能体框架，有效解决了微服务根因分析中因拓扑动态变化和症状放大效应导致的误判问题，在复杂环境下表现出强鲁棒性。

Abstract: Root cause analysis (RCA) in microservices is challenging due to (i) noisy and heterogeneous multimodal observability (metrics, logs, traces), (ii) cascading failure propagation that amplifies downstream symptoms, and (iii) non-stationary topology drift induced by autoscaling and rolling updates. Recent LLM-based RCA agents can generate tool-grounded explanations, yet they often remain topology-agnostic and suffer from \emph{symptom-amplification bias}, misattributing the root cause to salient downstream victims. We propose \textbf{TopoEvo}, a topology-aware self-evolving multi-agent framework that couples graph representation learning with structured, topology-constrained reasoning. TopoEvo first introduces \emph{Metric-orthogonal Multimodal Alignment} (MOMA), which decomposes metric embeddings into complementary subspaces and contrastively aligns logs and traces to reduce modality redundancy and sparsity, yielding stable node representations for graph encoding. It then applies \emph{Vector Quantization} (VQ) to discretize topology-enhanced states into auditable \emph{symptom tokens} with a symptom lexicon, enabling reliable retrieval and token-level evidence grounding. On top of these discrete topology cues, TopoEvo performs a multi-agent \emph{Hypothesis--Evidence--Test} (HET) workflow to explicitly verify propagation-consistent explanations and separate initiating anomalies from amplified downstream symptoms. Finally, a \emph{Self-Evolving Mechanism} refreshes hierarchical incident memory and performs conservative test-time adaptation with high-confidence pseudo-labels to maintain robustness under drift.

</details>


### [154] [ColPackAgent: Agent-Skill-Guided Hard-Particle Monte Carlo Workflows for Colloidal Packing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15625)
*Lijie Ding,Changwoo Do*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ColPackAgent通过集成了MCP工具服务器和领域专用技能，使LLM能够可靠地自主执行胶体堆积的Monte Carlo仿真工作流，弥补了通用智能体在科学模拟任务中“说的多、做得少”的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大语言模型（LLM）智能体通常只能描述科学模拟工作流而无法可靠执行，缺乏针对特定仿真任务（如胶体堆积）的直接操作工具与结构化指令接口。

Method: 开发了ColPackAgent框架，利用MCP（模型上下文协议）工具服务器封装HOOMD-blue仿真库，结合预设的四阶段工作流“智能体技能”。支持交互式、全自动及基于程序文件的“自动研究”三种执行模式，并设计了17项阶段性提示词Benchmark对LLM的表现进行评估。

Result: 成功实现了包括3D立方体、2D二元系统及硬圆盘冻结转变等胶体堆积模拟。Benchmark测试揭示了不同LLM在执行规划、设置及分析工作流时的可靠性差异，验证了该方案在处理复杂科学计算任务时的实用价值。

Conclusion: 通过将领域专用Python包、MCP工具与可移植的智能体技能相结合，构建了一种将传统仿真工具转化为智能体辅助科研工作流的有效范式，显著提升了LLM在复杂模拟任务中的自主执行能力与可靠性。

Abstract: We introduce ColPackAgent, an agent framework that autonomously runs Monte Carlo simulations of colloidal packing through a Model Context Protocol (MCP) tool server and an agent skill, whether as a standalone agent or inside an existing agent system. By harnessing the MCP server and agent skill, ColPackAgent executes a structured workflow for colloidal packing simulations, which are central to studies of phase behavior, self-assembly, and materials design. Without dedicated simulation tools and workflow instructions, general-purpose Large Language Model (LLM) agents tend to describe such workflows rather than execute them reliably. The MCP server exposes a custom-built colpack Python package that wraps HOOMD-blue hard-particle Monte Carlo, and the skill encodes a four-stage workflow contract. ColPackAgent can carry out the workflow interactively with human feedback, autonomously from an end-to-end prompt, or as autoresearch following a provided program file. We demonstrate the system in different modes with several colloidal packing simulation examples such as cube particles in 3D, a binary system of disks and capsules in 2D, and the 2D hard-disk freezing transition using autoresearch. We also compare model performance on this workflow across a panel of LLMs with 17 stage-specific prompts. This benchmark provides a stage-level check of how reliably different models follow the setup, planning, and analysis workflow. Together, these results show that pairing a domain Python package with MCP tools and a portable agent skill provides a practical route for turning a simulation toolkit into an agent-assisted research workflow.

</details>


### [155] [Nudging Beyond the Comfort Zone: Efficient Strategy-Guided Exploration for RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15726)
*Chanuk Lee,Sangwoo Park,Minki Kang,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NudgeRL 提出了一种结构化、多样性驱动的探索方法，通过“策略引导”和分解优化目标，显著提升了 LLM 在 RLVR 中的推理表现和采样效率，无需昂贵的特权监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习验证奖励机制（RLVR）受限于探索不足，依赖大规模采样（rollout）导致计算成本高昂，且现有方法难以有效控制探索方向。

Method: 提出 NudgeRL 框架：1. 策略引导（Strategy Nudging）：在每个 rollout 中引入轻量级策略上下文以诱导多样化推理轨迹；2. 统一优化目标：将奖励信号分解为上下文间（inter-context）和上下文内（intra-context）分量，并结合蒸馏目标将发现的行为迁移回基础策略。

Result: 在五个数学基准测试中，NudgeRL 的表现优于标准 GRPO（即便在使用 8 倍采样预算的情况下），并优于基于特权信息的 Oracle 指导基线。

Conclusion: NudgeRL 通过引入策略引导（Strategy Nudging）和分解式学习目标，证明了结构化、上下文驱动的探索是替代大规模暴力采样和特权信息辅助方法的有效方案，实现了更高的计算效率和推理性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a scalable paradigm for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models. However, its effectiveness is fundamentally limited by exploration: the policy can only improve on trajectories it has already sampled. While increasing the number of rollouts alleviates this issue, such brute-force scaling is computationally expensive, and existing approaches that modify the optimization objective provide limited control over what is explored. In this work, we propose NudgeRL, a framework for structured and diversity-driven exploration in RLVR. Our approach introduces Strategy Nudging, which conditions each rollout on lightweight, strategy-level contexts to induce diverse reasoning trajectories without relying on expensive oracle supervision. To effectively learn from such structured exploration, we further propose a unified objective, which decomposes the reward signal into inter- and intra-context components and incorporates a distillation objective to transfer discovered behaviors back to the base policy. Empirically, NudgeRL outperforms standard GRPO with up to 8 times larger rollout budgets, while outperforming oracle-guided RL baseline on average across five challenging math benchmarks. These results demonstrate that structured, context-driven exploration can serve as an efficient and scalable alternative to both brute-force rollout scaling and feasibility-oriented methods based on privileged information. Our code is available at https://github.com/tally0818/NudgeRL.

</details>


### [156] [Can We Trust AI-Inferred User States. A Psychometric Framework for Validating the Reliability of Users States Classification by LLMs in Operational Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15734)
*Izabella Krzeminska,Michal Butkiewicz,Ewa Komkowska*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了大语言模型在评估用户状态时，大多数指标在个体层面上缺乏可靠性，无法支撑实时自适应系统的决策。研究提出了一个可复制的评估框架，以确保AI系统设计中的度量标准能够经得起可靠性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验大语言模型在评估用户状态时，“度量标准在个体评分水平上具有稳定性和可解释性”这一核心假设的真实性。

Method: 采用复现评估程序，对三种双模态大模型（GPT-4o audio、Gemini 2.0 Flash、Gemini 2.5 Flash）的213个度量标准进行分析，对比了个体评分可靠性与聚合可靠性。

Result: 仅213个度量标准中有31个达到可靠性标准。结果表明高可靠性极为罕见，证实了直接使用AI指标进行实时自适应决策存在显著风险。

Conclusion: 度量标准的可靠性并非默认属性。由于单个评分缺乏稳定性，其无法直接用于实时自适应系统。然而，这些指标在聚合分析（事后研究）中仍具有分析价值。研究提出了一个可复制的评估框架，强调在AI系统设计中必须对度量标准进行明确的可靠性验证。

Abstract: The use of large language models to assess user states in conversational and adaptive systems is based on the assumption that the metrics used for such assessment are stable and interpretable at the level of individual scores. This paper empirically tests this assumption, focusing on the psychometric reliability of artificial intelligence (AI) measures of user states.
  This study employed replication evaluation procedures to assess the repeatability of a broad set of metrics across three different bimodal large language models (GPT-4o audio, Gemini 2.0 Flash, Gemini 2.5 Flash). Analyses include both individual score reliability and aggregated reliability, allowing us to distinguish metrics potentially useful for real-time adaptation from those that retain their value only in aggregated analyses.
  The results demonstrate that metric reliability cannot be considered a default property in interpretive domains. The lack of stability at the level of individual scores precludes the interpretation of such scores as indicators of user state in real-time adaptive systems, even if these metrics demonstrate stability after aggregation. At the same time, the study indicates that individually unstable metrics can retain analytical utility in post-hoc studies, identifying rules governing interactions and their relationships with user experience parameters such as satisfaction, trust, and engagement.
  The main contribution of this work, besides quantifying the severity of the problem (only 31 of 213 metrics met the criteria), is the proposal of a replicable evaluation framework, enabling measurable evaluations of metric applicability. This approach supports more responsible AI design of adaptive systems, in which the interpretation of results requires explicit validation of reliability and monitoring for violations over time.

</details>


### [157] [SaaS-Bench: Can Computer-Use Agents Leverage Real-World SaaS to Solve Professional Workflows?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15777)
*Kean Shi,Zihang Li,Tianyi Ma,Zengji Tu,Jialong Wu,Xinbo Xu,Qingyao Yang,Ruoyu Wu,Weichu Xie,Ming Wu,Jason Zeng,Michael Heinrich,Elvis Zhang,Liang Chen,Kuan Li,Baobao Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对现有智能体基准测试过于简单的问题，本文推出了SaaS-Bench，该基准包含23个真实SaaS系统的106项复杂任务，实验发现现有LLM智能体在处理真实工作流时表现极差，成功率不及4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的网页和GUI智能体基准测试过于简化、任务孤立且仅支持短流程，无法体现现实专业工作流中对复杂交互、跨应用协作及长时依赖的需求。

Method: 提出了SaaS-Bench基准测试，涵盖23个可部署的真实SaaS系统和106项源自实际工作场景的任务；引入了加权验证检查点（weighted verification checkpoints），用于评估任务的最终完成度及中间进度。

Result: 实验表明，即使是目前最强的LLM模型在SaaS-Bench上的端到端任务完成率也不足4%，揭露了现有智能体在应对长时复杂数字工作流时的严重能力局限。

Conclusion: 当前主流的LLM-based智能体在面对复杂的SaaS工作流时表现极其糟糕（端到端完成率低于4%），现有技术在规划、状态跟踪、跨应用上下文维护及错误恢复方面存在显著短板，亟需更具挑战性的评估基准来推动技术研究。

Abstract: Computer-Using Agents (CUAs) are rapidly extending large language models (LLMs) beyond text-based reasoning toward action execution in more complex environments, such as web browsers and graphical user interfaces (GUIs). However, existing web and GUI agent benchmarks often rely on simplified settings, isolated tasks, or short-horizon interactions, making it difficult to assess capabilities of agents in realistic professional workflows. Software-as-a-Service (SaaS) environments are a natural choice for CUA evaluation, as they host a large share of modern digital work and naturally involve dynamic system states, cross-application coordination, domain-specific knowledge, and long-horizon dependencies. To this end, we introduce SaaS-Bench, a benchmark built on 23 deployable SaaS systems across six professional domains, containing 106 tasks grounded in realistic work scenarios. These tasks require long-horizon execution, cover both text-only and multimodal settings, and are evaluated with weighted verification checkpoints that measure strict task completion and partial progress. Experiments show that representative LLM-based agents struggle on SaaS-Bench, with even the strongest model completing fewer than 4% of tasks end-to-end, exposing limitations in planning, state tracking, cross-application context maintenance, and error recovery. Code are available at https://github.com/UniPat-AI/SaaS-Bench for reproduction.

</details>


### [158] [Agentic Discovery of Neural Architectures: AIRA-Compose and AIRA-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15871)
*Alberto Pepe,Chien-Yu Lin,Despoina Magka,Bilge Acun,Yannan Nellie Wu,Anton Protopopov,Carole-Jean Wu,Yoram Bachrach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了AIRA双框架，验证了AI智能体可自主设计并优化高性能基础模型架构，在效率和准确率上均超越了手写基准模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索LLM智能体在无需人类干预的情况下，自主设计超越现有Transformer的基础模型，向递归自我改进迈进。

Method: 提出双框架方法：AIRA-Compose（高层架构搜索，利用11个智能体探索计算原语）和AIRA-Design（低层机制实现，利用20个智能体设计注意力机制与训练脚本）。通过小规模候选模型评估并外推至更大规模进行预训练与测试。

Result: 生成的AIRAformer和AIRAhybrid架构在多项任务中优于Llama 3.2，推理速度提升显著（最高达71%），在长上下文任务中表现接近人类最佳水平。

Conclusion: AI智能体能够自主发现架构优化和基础模型，其表现优于人力设计的基准，证明了递归自我改进范式的可行性。

Abstract: Toward recursive self-improvement, we investigate LLM agents autonomously designing foundation models beyond standard Transformers. We introduce a dual-framework approach: AIRA-Compose for high-level architecture search, and AIRA-Design for low-level mechanistic implementation. AIRA-Compose uses 11 agents to explore fundamental computational primitives under a 24-hour budget. Agents evaluate million-parameter candidates, extrapolating top designs to 350M, 1B, and 3B scales. This yields 14 architectures across two families: AIRAformers (Transformer-based) and AIRAhybrids (Transformer-Mamba). Pre-trained at 1B scale, these consistently outperform Llama 3.2 and Composer-found baselines. On downstream tasks, AIRAformer-D and AIRAhybrid-D improve accuracy by 2.4% and 3.8% over Llama 3.2. Furthermore, AIRA-Compose finds models with highly efficient scaling frontiers: AIRAformer-C scales 54% and 71% faster than Llama 3.2 and Composer's best Transformer, while AIRAhybrid-C outscales Nemotron-2 by 23% and Composer's best hybrid by 37%. AIRA-Design tasks 20 agents with writing novel attention mechanisms for long-range dependencies and high-performing training scripts. On the Long Range Arena benchmark, agent-designed architectures reach within 2.3% and 2.6% of human state-of-the-art on document matching and text classification. On the Autoresearch benchmark, Greedy Opus 4.5 achieves 0.968 validation bits-per-byte under a fixed time budget, surpassing the published minimum. Together, these frameworks show AI agents can autonomously discover architectures and algorithmic optimizations matching or surpassing hand-designed baselines. This establishes a powerful paradigm for discovering next-generation foundation models, marking a clear step toward recursive self-improvement.

</details>


### [159] [Imperfect World Models are Exploitable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15960)
*Logan Mondal Bhamidipaty,Esmeralda S. Whitammer,David Abel,Mykel J. Kochenderfer,Subramanian Ramamoorthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过建立统一的理论框架，证明了强化学习中模型剥削与奖励黑客在本质上均难以避免，并提出了通过“安全时间范围”来缓解模型剥削的新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究未能证明奖励黑客的必然性结论能够直接扩展到模型剥削；此外，通过对比奖励黑客与模型剥削，探究在世界模型中进行安全规划的局限性。

Method: 提出了模型剥削的新定义，建立了关于奖励黑客和模型剥削的统一通用理论，并导出了规避剥削的安全时间范围（Safe Horizon）。

Result: 证明了在大型策略集上模型剥削是不可避免的；指出有限策略集下防止剥削的条件并不存在；确定了在特定条件下可规避模型剥削的安全时间阈值。

Conclusion: 强化学习中的模型剥削（Model Exploitation）在本质上是不可避免的，且与奖励黑客（Reward Hacking）之间存在严谨的理论联系。通过引入“松弛剥削”概念和“安全时间范围”，研究划定了安全规划的边界。

Abstract: We propose a novel definition of model exploitation in reinforcement learning. Informally, a world model is exploitable if it implies that one policy should be strictly preferred over another while the environment's true transition model implies the reverse. We analogize our definition with a prior characterization of reward hacking but show that the associated proof of inevitability does not transfer to exploitation. To overcome this obstruction, we develop a general theory of reward hacking and model exploitation that proves that exploitation is essentially unavoidable on large policy sets and yields the corresponding claim for hacking as a special case. Unfortunately, we also find that the conditions that guarantee unhackability in finite policy sets have no counterpart that precludes exploitation. Consequently, we introduce a relaxed notion of exploitation and derive a safe horizon within which it can be avoided. Taken together, our results establish a formal bridge between reward hacking and model exploitation and elucidate the limits of safe planning in world models.

</details>


### [160] [Deterministic Event-Graph Substrates as World Models for Counterfactual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15967)
*Fabio Rovai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于RDF三元组日志的“事件图基底”世界模型，通过结构化的因果逻辑支持精确的反事实查询，在多个基准测试中超越了现有符号化方案及大语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型在处理反事实推理和任务泛化时往往依赖难以解释或需要大量训练的参数化模型，缺乏对于智能体状态变化的可追溯性和严格的因果处理能力。

Method: 提出了一种基于“仅追加日志（append-only log）”的RDF三元组状态表达方法，通过结构化干预词汇对日志进行分叉（forking）来进行反事实查询；形式化了查询对偶性，并将解释性查询和反事实查询统一归约为因果祖先遍历算法。

Result: 在CLEVRER数据集上，该方法在所有类别上均超过了NS-DR符号预言机（提升最高达20.26个百分点）；在自建的twin-EventLog基准测试中，其准确率显著超越了Llama-3.1-8B模型（提升18.80个点）。

Conclusion: 事件图基底（Event-graph substrates）为复杂推理任务提供了一种可解释且准确的替代方案，特别是通过日志分叉实现反事实推理，证明了显式符号化建模在领域泛化和零样本学习中的优势。

Abstract: We study event-graph substrates: a class of world models that represent agent state as an append-only log of typed RDF triples and answer counterfactual queries by forking the log under a structured intervention vocabulary. Substrates are inspectable at the triple level, support exact counterfactuals, and transfer across domains without learned components. We formalize the class, prove a duality between explanatory and counterfactual queries that reduces both to the same causal-ancestor traversal, and evaluate a 1,400-line CLEVRER-DSL interpreter atop a domain-agnostic substrate runtime at full CLEVRER validation scale (n=75,618). The substrate exceeds the NS-DR symbolic oracle on all four per-question categories (by 9.89, 20.26, 17.65, and 0.80 percentage points), and exceeds the parametric ALOE baseline on descriptive and explanatory while lagging on predictive and counterfactual. We also introduce twin-EventLog, a 500-specification Park-canonical Smallville counterfactual benchmark on which the substrate exceeds Llama-3.1-8B with full context by 18.80 points joint accuracy.

</details>


### [161] [Petri Net Induced Heuristic Search for Resource Constrained Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15983)
*Ido Lublin,Dor Atzmon,Izack Cohen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过将RCPSP映射为Petri网状态空间搜索，并结合启发式A*算法，该方法在求解效率和成功率上超越了传统的MIP求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决RCPSP，并探索相比于传统MIP求解器，基于状态空间搜索的方法在处理复杂调度问题时的性能优势及其局限性。

Method: 将RCPSP建模为带资源的时间转换Petri网的到达性图搜索问题；使用相对延迟令牌（relative-delay tokens）表示调度决策；采用结合关键路径和资源下界的启发式函数引导A*算法进行求解。

Result: 基于PSPLIB基准测试，该方法在成功率和求解时间上均优于主流MIP求解器（如SCIP、CBC）；分析发现A*算法受资源紧缺程度影响，而MIP受模型规模影响，资源强度决定了何种求解器更具优势。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在资源受限项目调度问题（RCPSP）中，基于带有时序语义的Petri网的搜索方法优于传统的混合整数线性规划（MIP）方法，并揭示了启发式搜索与MIP在不同约束条件下的性能退化机制。

Abstract: We formulate the Resource-Constrained Project Scheduling Problem (RCPSP) as optimal search over the reachability graph of a Timed Transition Petri Net with Resources, using relative-delay tokens so that scheduling decisions correspond to transition firings in the induced state space. We solve the resulting problem with $A^*$ guided by a heuristic that combines Critical Path and resource-based lower bounds, and prove that it is consistent under our token-based time semantics. Experiments on the PSPLIB benchmarks show that the approach outperforms strong exact Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MIP) baselines (SCIP, CBC) in both success rate and solve time. Per-instance analysis shows that heuristic search and MIP degrade along independent axes, resource tightness for $A^*$ and formulation size for MIP, with resource strength mediating which solver benefits from scale.

</details>


### [162] [Reasoners or Translators? Contamination-aware Evaluation and Neuro-Symbolic Robustness in Tax Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16052)
*Parisa Kordjamshidi,Samer Aslan,Madhavan Seshadri,Leslie Barrett,Enrico Santus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现大模型在法律推理中存在由数据污染导致的性能虚高问题，通过对比实验证明，结合法定文本形式化表达与符号求解器的“神经符号”框架，能显著提升法律AI的鲁棒性及对未知案例的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于数据污染的存在，目前大型语言模型（LLM）在法律推理任务中的表现难以区分是真正的推理能力还是数据记忆的结果，缺乏对其可靠性的深入研究。

Method: 1. 实施污染检测协议以评估LLM的可靠性；2. 构建包含案例和规则变体的新型测试集；3. 对比单体LLM与将法定文本转换为形式表达并委托给符号求解器的混合系统。

Result: 1. 数据污染确实会导致模型性能虚高；2. 神经符号系统在处理法律推理任务时，其表现优于单体LLM，且在泛化能力和鲁棒性方面具有显著优势。

Conclusion: 法律推理本质上是组合式的，神经符号（Neuro-symbolic）架构在法律AI领域提供了更稳健、可靠的基础，更能适应未见过的法律场景。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have significantly enhanced automated legal reasoning. Yet, it remains unclear whether their performance reflects genuine legal reasoning ability or artifacts of data contamination. We present a comprehensive empirical study of tax law reasoning approaches and implement a contamination detection protocol to rigorously assess LLM reliability. We show that performance can be inflated by contamination. Building on this analysis, we conduct a systematic evaluation, comparing monolithic LLMs with hybrid systems that translate statutory text into formal representations and delegate inference to symbolic solvers. We build a novel test suite designed to probe generalization to unseen documents via case and rule variations. Our findings indicate that legal reasoning is inherently compositional and that neuro-symbolic frameworks offer a more reliable and robust foundation for legal AI, as well as improved generalization to unobserved situations.

</details>


### [163] [Sign-Separated Finite-Time Error Analysis of Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16103)
*Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过误差正负分量分解，揭示了 Q-learning 中导致过估计的非对称动力学机制，并提供了新的有限时间收敛边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Q-learning 有限时间分析多关注整体误差，未能充分解释 Q-learning 中的过估计现象及其带来的收敛速度不对称性。

Method: 基于切换系统（switching-system）表示，将误差分解为分量级的负部分和正部分，分别通过 LTI 系统和线性切换系统进行边界分析。

Result: 推导出了常数步长 Q-learning 的有限时间误差界，证明了负向误差的收敛速度通常快于或等于正向误差，并揭示了 Bellman 最大化算子如何传播正向错误。

Conclusion: Q-learning 中存在最大值诱导的非对称性，正向误差（过估计）受线性切换系统控制，负向误差受更优的 LTI 系统制约，证明了正负误差在动力学上的不对称性是导致过估计的主要原因。

Abstract: This paper develops a sign-separated finite-time error analysis for constant step-size Q-learning. Starting from the switching-system representation, the error is decomposed into its componentwise negative and positive parts. The negative part is dominated by a lower comparison linear time-invariant (LTI) system associated with a fixed optimal policy, whereas the positive part is controlled by a linear switching system. The resulting bounds show that the negative-side LTI certificate is no slower than the positive-side switching certificate and may produce a faster exponential envelope. The analysis identifies a max-induced asymmetry in Q-learning error dynamics. This asymmetry is connected to overestimation: positive action-wise errors can be selected and propagated by the Bellman maximum, whereas negative errors admit an optimal-policy lower comparison. Finite-time bounds are provided for both deterministic and stochastic constant-step-size recursions.

</details>


### [164] [ShopGym: An Integrated Framework for Realistic Simulation and Scalable Benchmarking of E-Commerce Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16116)
*Chinmay Savadikar,Mingyu Zhao,Yuanzheng Zhu,Han Li,Shuang Xie,Alberto Castelo,Tianfu Wu,Lingyun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ShopGym是一个针对电商Web Agent的综合性仿真与评估框架，通过将真实商店自动化转化为可控、可复现的沙盒环境并自动合成测试任务，解决了现有评估标准在真实性与科学可控性之间的平衡难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的电商Web Agent评估方法存在缺陷：真实网站不稳定、不可复现且难以调试；手工构建的沙盒基准虽可控但覆盖范围窄，缺乏多样性。领域内缺乏能兼顾真实性、多样性、可控性与可复现性的评估设置。

Method: 提出ShopGym框架，包含两个核心组件：1. ShopArena：利用匿名化规格和各阶段验证流程，将真实电商网站自动转化为自包含、可重置的沙盒仿真环境；2. ShopGuru：基于转化后的商店构造（包括目录、导航、政策等），合成七类不同技能需求的电商任务。

Result: 通过对224项任务（涵盖合成数据与真实数据迁移）的验证，证明ShopGym生成的沙盒商店保留了真实网站的核心结构特征，且智能体在合成环境中的性能与在真实网站中的表现具有显著的正相关性。

Conclusion: ShopGym解决了电商Web Agent评估中真实性与可控性之间的矛盾，为开发、评估和比较电商智能体提供了一个可扩展、可复制且符合现实逻辑的基准评估框架。

Abstract: Developing and evaluating e-commerce web agents requires environments that preserve meaningful task structure while enabling controllable, reproducible, and scalable scientific comparison. Existing methodologies force a tradeoff: live storefronts provide realism but are non-stationary, difficult to inspect, and irreproducible, while hand-built sandbox benchmarks provide control but cover only a narrow range of layouts, catalogs, policies, and interaction patterns. We argue that the core bottleneck is methodological: the field lacks a scalable way to construct evaluation settings that are simultaneously realistic, diverse, controllable, inspectable, and reproducible. We introduce ShopGym, an integrated framework for realistic simulation and scalable benchmarking of e-commerce web agents. ShopGym is a framework for constructing e-commerce simulation environments and grounded benchmark tasks. Its simulation layer, ShopArena, converts live seed storefronts into self-contained sandbox shops through anonymized shop specifications and a staged, validated generation process. On top of these simulated storefronts, ShopGuru synthesizes benchmark tasks across seven skill categories, grounding each task in the shop's catalog, navigation structure, policies, and interaction affordances. Together, ShopArena and ShopGuru produce self-contained, resettable, inspectable, and stable evaluation artifacts that preserve structural properties and agent-evaluation signals relevant to shopping tasks. We validate the framework through graph-based structural analysis and agent-based behavioral evaluation with 224 generated tasks across six sandbox shops: three constructed with synthetic data and three with real data. Our results show that the synthetic shops preserve key structural properties of live storefronts, with agent performance on synthetic shops positively correlated with performance on live storefronts.

</details>


### [165] [Property-Guided LLM Program Synthesis for Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16142)
*Augusto B. Corrêa,André G. Pereira,Jendrik Seipp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对现有大模型程序合成依赖简单评分导致效率低下的问题，本研究提出一种基于形式化验证的“属性引导”方法，通过向大模型提供错误反例，显著降低了计算成本并提升了生成程序的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于简单数值评分（如测试通过数）的大模型程序合成方法缺乏失败原因的指导，导致需要生成和评估海量候选程序，造成巨大的推理与计算成本。

Method: 提出“属性引导的程序合成”（property-guided program synthesis）。在评估过程中，检查候选程序是否满足预定义的属性，而非仅依赖简单的数值评分。一旦属性被破坏，立即停止评估，并将具体的反例（counterexample）反馈给大模型，辅助其进行程序修复与迭代。在本研究中，具体应用于 PDDL 规划领域，通过验证启发式函数的“直接性”属性来引导生成。

Result: 在十个 PDDL 规划领域及跨分布测试集中，该方法相比现有最优生成方法，平均每个领域的程序生成量减少了 7 倍，在不使用搜索的情况下解决了更多任务，且评估候选程序的计算量降低了几个数量级。

Conclusion: 只要问题具备可验证的属性，属性引导的大模型程序合成方法就能有效降低合成成本并提升程序质量。该方法通过引入形式化验证反馈，为大模型程序合成提供了一种更高效、鲁棒的替代方案。

Abstract: LLMs have shown impressive success in program synthesis, discovering programs that surpass prior solutions. However, these approaches rely on simple numeric scores to signal program quality, such as the value of the solution or the number of passed tests. Because a score offers no guidance on why a program failed, the system must generate and evaluate many candidates hoping some succeed, increasing LLM inference and evaluation costs. We study a different approach: property-guided LLM program synthesis. Instead of scoring programs after evaluation, we check whether a candidate satisfies a formally defined property. When the property is violated, we stop the evaluation early and provide the LLM with a concrete counterexample showing exactly how the program failed. This feedback drastically reduces both the number of program generations and the evaluation cost, and can guide the LLM to generate stronger programs. We evaluate this approach on PDDL planning domains, asking the LLM to synthesize direct heuristic functions: every state reachable by strictly improving transitions has a strictly improving successor. A heuristic with this property leads hill-climbing algorithm directly to a goal state. A counterexample-guided repair loop generates one candidate program, checks the property over a training set, and returns the first case that violates the property. We evaluate our approach on ten planning domains with an out-of-distribution test set. The synthesized heuristics are effectively direct on virtually all test tasks, and compared to the best prior generation method our approach generates seven times fewer programs per domain on average, solves more tasks without using search, and requires several orders of magnitude less computation to evaluate candidates. Whenever a problem admits a verifiable property, property-guided LLM synthesis can reduce cost and improve program quality.

</details>


### [166] [Look Before You Leap: Autonomous Exploration for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16143)
*Ziang Ye,Wentao Shi,Yuxin Liu,Yu Wang,Zhengzhou Cai,Yaorui Shi,Qi Gu,Xunliang Cai,Fuli Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM智能体在陌生环境下的“过早开发”问题，提出了可量化的探索指标及“先探索、后行动”的训练范式，通过解耦信息获取与任务执行，显著提升了智能体的自主探索能力与环境适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型（LLM）的智能体在陌生环境中往往表现出“过早开发”（premature exploitation）倾向，即在未充分掌握环境信息前便急于利用先验知识行动，这限制了智能体在复杂环境中的适应性与泛化能力。

Method: 1. 提出“探索检查点覆盖率”（Exploration Checkpoint Coverage）：一种用于量化智能体对环境关键状态、物体及交互属性发现广度的可验证指标。
2. 提出“先探索、后行动”（Explore-then-Act）范式：将信息获取与任务执行阶段解耦。
3. 开发了一种混合训练策略：交替进行任务执行滚动（task-execution rollouts）与探索滚动（exploration rollouts），并分别应用相应的可验证奖励进行优化。

Result: 实验表明，标准的任务导向强化学习会导致智能体行为狭隘且重复。而采用新的训练策略和“先探索、后行动”范式，能显著提升智能体在未知环境中的信息获取效率和任务解决性能。

Conclusion: 为了构建具备泛化能力和现实世界适应性的智能体，必须将自主探索视为一项核心能力。通过解耦探索与任务执行，能够有效解决LLM智能体在陌生环境下的“过早开发”问题。

Abstract: Large language model based agents often fail in unfamiliar environments due to premature exploitation: a tendency to act on prior knowledge before acquiring sufficient environment-specific information. We identify autonomous exploration as a critical yet underexplored capability for building adaptive agents. To formalize and quantify this capability, we introduce Exploration Checkpoint Coverage, a verifiable metric that measures how broadly an agent discovers key states, objects, and affordances. Our systematic evaluation reveals that agents trained with standard task-oriented reinforcement learning consistently exhibit narrow and repetitive behaviors that impede downstream performance. To address this limitation, we develop a training strategy that interleaves task-execution rollouts and exploration rollouts, with each type of rollout optimized by its corresponding verifiable reward. Building on this training strategy, we propose the Explore-then-Act paradigm, which decouples information-gathering from task execution: agents first utilize an interaction budget to acquire grounded environmental knowledge, then leverage it for task resolution. Our results demonstrate that learning to systematically explore is imperative for building generalizable and real-world-ready agents.

</details>


### [167] [An Algebraic Exposition of the Theory of Dyadic Morality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16153)
*Kush R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究将人类二元道德理论通过结构因果模型转化为严谨的代数框架，为神经符号AI提供了一种既符合道德逻辑又具备心理学可解释性的决策计算方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决二元道德理论（TDM）在复杂社交情境中的扩展性与严谨性问题，并将其转化为适用于AI系统的计算模型，以提升AI在处理道德决策时的准确性与合规性。

Method: 利用结构因果模型（SCM）对二元道德理论（TDM）进行形式化定义，并引入三个核心心理算子：类型映射算子（typecasting operator）、补全算子（completion operator）和价态依赖推理机制（valence-dependent inference mechanism）；同时提出通过“节点坍缩”和“序列处理”机制解决复杂场景的扩展性问题。

Result: 成功构建了TDM的代数模型，证明了该模型能有效处理多节点道德场景；提出了AI策略设计的应用范式，包括冲突义务检测、用户自主性保护以及因果干预式的故障后通信。

Conclusion: 通过代数形式化，该研究弥合了人类道德认知与神经符号人工智能之间的鸿沟，为构建更具人性化、数学严谨性的道德决策AI系统提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: This paper provides an algebraic exposition of the theory of dyadic morality (TDM), a psychological model of moral judgment grounded in a simple two-node template: an intentional agent causing harm to a vulnerable patient. We formalize TDM using structural causal modeling (SCM) notation and identify three psychological operators (typecasting operator, completion operator, and valence-dependent inference mechanism) that extend standard SCM to capture how people compute moral judgments under constraints. We address scalability challenges arising from TDM's dyadic limitation, showing how moral cognition compresses multi-node scenarios through node collapse and sequential processing. Drawing on this algebraic framework, we demonstrate concrete applications to AI policy design: detecting conflicting obligations, structuring helpfulness policies to preserve user agency, and designing post-failure communication as causal interventions. Finally, we recommend scoped, contextual measurement of mind perception over universal averaging to operationalize the theory empirically. This algebraic formalization enables neurosymbolic AI systems to compute morality in a way that is both mathematically rigorous and faithful to human moral cognition.

</details>


### [168] [Formal Methods Meet LLMs: Auditing, Monitoring, and Intervention for Compliance of Advanced AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16198)
*Parand A. Alamdari,Toryn Q. Klassen,Sheila A. McIlraith*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合形式化方法（LTL）与机器学习的监控框架，通过审计与运行时干预显著提升了黑盒 LLM 在复杂时序约束下的合规性与安全性，有效缓解了 LLM 在时间推理上的能力瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对 AI 服务全生命周期（尤其是黑盒大模型）中缺乏有效且严谨的合规性、 safety 和行为准则监控手段的现状，旨在解决大模型在处理需要长程时间一致性（如规范、规则、时序约束）任务时表现出的不可靠性。

Method: 结合形式化方法（特别是线性时序逻辑 LTL）与先进机器学习技术，开发了一套用于 AI 产品全生命周期的离线审计与在线运行时监控框架。包括：基于采样的预测性监测，以及在运行时进行干预以防范冲突的监控器。

Result: 实验表明，基于 LTL 的方法在处理时序行为约束监测方面显著优于基准方法，即便是小型模型结合该方法也能达到或超越顶尖 LLM 的评估能力。此外，该监测与干预机制有效减少了 LLM 智能体的违法率。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效弥补大模型在时间逻辑推理上的缺陷，通过引入基于LTL的审计和防御机制，在大模型应用（如智能体）中实现了显著的安全性提升，同时保持了任务性能。此外，研究揭示了大模型在处理复杂长程时间约束时的推理能力存在显著退化。

Abstract: We examine one particular dimension of AI governance: how to monitor and audit AI-enabled products and services throughout the AI development lifecycle, from pre-deployment testing to post-deployment auditing. Combining principles from formal methods with SoTA machine learning, we propose techniques that enable AI-enabled product and service developers, as well as third party AI developers and evaluators, to perform offline auditing and online (runtime) monitoring of product-specific (temporally extended) behavioral constraints such as safety constraints, norms, rules and regulations with respect to black-box advanced AI systems, notably LLMs. We further provide practical techniques for predictive monitoring, such as sampling-based methods, and we introduce intervening monitors that act at runtime to preempt and potentially mitigate predicted violations. Experimental results show that by exploiting the formal syntax and semantics of Linear Temporal Logic (LTL), our proposed auditing and monitoring techniques are superior to LLM baseline methods in detecting violations of temporally extended behavioral constraints; with our approach, even small-model labelers match or exceed frontier LLM judges. Our predictive and intervening monitors significantly reduce the violation rates of LLM-based agents while largely preserving task performance. We further show through controlled experiments that LLMs' temporal reasoning shows a pronounced degradation in accuracy with increasing event distance, number of constraints, and number of propositions.

</details>


### [169] [Confirming Correct, Missing the Rest: LLM Tutoring Agents Struggle Where Feedback Matters Most](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16207)
*Tahreem Yasir,Wenbo Li,Sam Gilson,Sutapa Dey Tithi,Xiaoyi Tian,Tiffany Barnes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现 LLM 在区分学生复杂思维（次优解与错误解）方面存在架构性缺陷，诊断能力与教学效果脱节，建议将其整合进知识图谱驱动的混合架构中以实现更有效的教学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估现有的 LLM 驱动的智能辅导系统（ITS）在区分学生解题思路（最优、有效但次优、错误）方面的诊断精确度，探讨其是否具备作为有效教育辅导员的能力。

Method: 构建了一个包含 10,836 个“解题步骤-反馈”对的基准测试集；基于命题逻辑，利用知识图谱作为基准真值，评估了 7 种 LLM 智能体在三种反馈条件下的表现。

Result: LLM 在最优解判断上表现近乎完美；但在判断“有效但次优”和“错误”解题时表现较差，存在明显的过高拒绝或误判；且即便诊断准确，也不一定能提供高质量的教学反馈。

Conclusion: LLM 在作为辅导系统时，在诊断精度上存在系统性缺陷（无法区分次优解与错误解），且诊断能力与教学反馈有效性之间存在脱节。因此，建议采用“知识图谱负责诊断 + LLM 负责交互”的混合架构。

Abstract: Effective tutoring requires distinguishing optimal, valid but suboptimal, and incorrect student solutions, a distinction central to intelligent tutoring systems (ITS) but untested for LLM-based tutors. As LLMs are increasingly explored as conversational complements to ITS, evaluating their diagnostic precision is essential. We present a benchmark of seven LLM feedback agents in propositional logic using knowledge-graph-derived ground truth across 10,836 solution--feedback pairs and three feedback conditions. Models achieved near-ceiling performance on optimal steps but systematically over-rejected valid but suboptimal reasoning and over-validated incorrect solutions, precisely where adaptive tutoring matters most. These failures persisted across models regardless of solution context, suggesting architectural rather than informational limits. Moreover, accurate diagnosis did not reliably produce pedagogically actionable feedback, revealing a gap between diagnostic judgment and instructional effectiveness. Our findings suggest that LLMs are better suited for hybrid architectures where KG-grounded models handle diagnosis while LLMs support open-ended scaffolding and dialogue.

</details>


### [170] [Fully Open Meditron: An Auditable Pipeline for Clinical LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16215)
*Xavier Theimer-Lienhard,Mushtaha El-Amin,Fay Elhassan,Sahaj Vaidya,Victor Cartier-Negadi,David Sasu,Lars Klein,Mary-Anne Hartley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文介绍了首个“完全开放”的医疗LLM-CDSS流水线 Fully Open Meditron，填补了医疗大模型在数据溯源、训练过程和验证机制上的透明度缺失，并实现了临床性能的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的医疗临床决策支持系统（CDSS）及基于大语言模型（LLM）的系统缺乏透明度（仅开放权重），导致难以进行严格的审计与验证，医药领域尚缺乏“完全开放”（Fully Open, FO）的端到端训练栈模型。

Method: 构建了包含临床医生审核的训练语料库、可重复的数据构建与训练框架、以及面向用途的一致性评估协议；实施了系统范围的去污染、教师生成的金标准重采样，并由四位医师组成的专家小组进行验证。

Result: Apertus-70B-MeditronFO 在综合医疗基准上提升了6.6个百分点（从47.2%提升至53.8%），确立了新的 FO SOTA；Gemma-3-27B-MeditronFO 在多项对比评价中优于 MedGemma。

Conclusion: Fully Open Meditron 证明了在不牺牲可审计性和可重复性的前提下，构建“完全开放”的LLM-CDSS是可行的，并能达到医学特定领域的SOTA表现。

Abstract: Clinical decision support systems (CDSS) require scrutable, auditable pipelines that enable rigorous, reproducible validation. Yet current LLM-based CDSS remain largely opaque. Most "open" models are open-weight only, releasing parameters while withholding the data provenance, curation procedures, and generation pipelines that determine model behavior. Fully Open (FO) models, which expose the complete training stack end-to-end, do not currently exist in medicine. We introduce Fully Open Meditron, the first fully open pipeline for building LLM-CDSS, comprising a clinician-audited training corpus, a reproducible data construction and training framework, and a use-aligned evaluation protocol. The corpus unifies eight public medical QA datasets into a normalized conversational format and expands coverage with three clinician-vetted synthetic extensions: exam-style QA, guideline-grounded QA derived from 46,469 clinical practice guidelines, and clinical vignettes. The pipeline enforces system-wide decontamination, gold-label resampling of teacher generations, and end-to-end validation by a four-physician panel. We evaluate using an LLM-as-a-judge protocol over expert-written clinical vignettes, calibrated against 204 human raters. We apply the recipe to five FO base models (Apertus-70B/8B-Instruct, OLMo-2-32B-SFT, EuroLLM-22B/9B-Instruct). All MeditronFO variants are preferred over their bases. Apertus-70B-MeditronFO improves +6.6 points over its base (47.2% to 53.8%) on aggregate medical benchmarks, establishing a new FO SoTA. Gemma-3-27B-MeditronFO is preferred over MedGemma in 58.6% of LLM-as-a-judge comparisons and outperforms it on HealthBench (58% vs 55.9%). These results show that fully open pipelines can achieve state-of-the-art domain-specific performance without sacrificing auditability or reproducibility.

</details>


### [171] [Prospective multi-pathogen disease forecasting using autonomous LLM-guided tree search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16238)
*Sarah Martinson,Michael P. Brenner,Martyna Plomecka,Brian P. Williams,Nicholas G. Reich,Zahra Shamsi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于LLM的自动化系统，无需人工干预即可生成高性能、符合科学理论的传染病预测模型，在实际应用中其效果优于CDC专家团队的集成模型，显著提升了预测任务的可扩展性与响应速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传染病概率预测对于公共卫生至关重要，但传统方法高度依赖专家手动构建模型，导致难以在地理颗粒度或新兴病原体方面扩展。

Method: 开发了一种由大语言模型（LLM）引导的树搜索系统，能够迭代生成、测试及优化可执行的流行病预测代码；引入了对数尺度距离度量（Log-scale distance metrics）以防止奖励作弊，并内置自动化评判机制（Judge-in-the-loop）以确保科学理论的契合度。

Result: 在2025-2026年美国呼吸道疾病季节的实地测试中，该系统生成的模型在流感、COVID-19和RSV预测方面达到或超越了美国疾控中心（CDC）专家集成模型的水平；在缺乏历史数据的“冷启动”场景下表现稳健。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了利用大语言模型引导的搜索算法可以实现传染病预测模型的完全自动化生成与优化，能够有效突破传统人工建模的瓶颈，实现高质量、大规模的传染病预测。

Abstract: Probabilistic forecasting of infectious diseases is crucial for public health but relies on labor-intensive manual model curation by expert modeling teams. This bespoke development bottlenecks scalability to granular geographic resolutions or emerging pathogens. Here, we present an autonomous system using Large Language Model (LLM)-guided tree search to iteratively generate, evaluate, and optimize executable forecasting software. In a fully prospective, real-time evaluation during the 2025-2026 US respiratory season, the system autonomously discovered methodologically diverse models for influenza, COVID-19, and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV). Aggregating these machine-generated models yielded an ensemble that consistently matched or outperformed the gold-standard, human-curated Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC) hub ensembles out-of-sample. The system successfully navigated data-scarce "cold start" scenarios for RSV. Moreover, controlled retrospective ablations revealed that optimizing log-scale distance metrics prevents reward hacking, while an automated judge-in-the-loop ensures structural fidelity to complex scientific theories. By autonomously translating epidemiological theory into accurate, transparent code, this framework overcomes the modeling labor bottleneck, enabling rapid deployment of expert-level disease forecasting at unprecedented scales.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [Agent4POI: Agentic Context-Conditioned Affordance Reasoning for Multimodal Point-of-Interest Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15203)
*Jinze Wang,Yangchen Zeng,Tiehua Zhang,Lu Zhang,Yuze Liu,Yongchao Liu,Xingjun Ma,Zhu Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Agent4POI 摒弃了传统的静态 POI 嵌入，引入了一种基于推理的动态多模态框架，利用 LLM 智能体在推荐时刻根据上下文生成可供性表征，显著提升了 POI 推荐的准确性、鲁棒性及对冷启动场景的适应力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态 POI 推荐系统依赖于预计算的静态嵌入，无法捕捉同一地点在不同情境（如工作日办公 vs. 周末聚会）下的动态属性差异，且在冷启动和情境偏移场景下性能表现不佳。

Method: 提出了一种四阶段的 LLM 智能体架构：1) 生成动态的、基于情境的“可供性（affordance）”查询；2) 执行基于图像、评论和元数据的五步跨模态思维链推理；3) 构建不确定性感知且基于吉布森可供性理论的表征；4) 利用语义缓存机制实现低延迟排序。此外，通过理论证明了传统的预计算编码器无法满足上下文敏感的排名需求。

Result: 在三个 POI 基准测试中，Agent4POI 相比最强基线实现了 23.2% 的相对增益；在情境偏移下的性能衰减仅为 7.5%（优于其他方法的 16-17%）；在冷启动场景中，性能最高可达最佳内容基线的 2.4 倍。

Conclusion: Agent4POI 证明了在推荐时动态生成上下文感知的多模态表征对于处理复杂的情境依赖至关重要。通过转向基于推理的表征生成，该框架在标准、冷启动和情境偏移场景下均显著优于传统的静态嵌入方法，为 POI 推荐提供了一种鲁棒且可解释的新范式。

Abstract: We introduce Agent4POI, the first POI recommendation framework that generates context-conditioned multimodal representations at recommendation time, rather than relying on static POI embeddings pre-computed independently of context. Existing multimodal systems encode each POI once as a static embedding, a design that precludes reasoning about why the same cafe affords solo work on Monday but group celebration on Friday evening. We formally prove that no pre-computed encoder can satisfy context-sensitive ranking under standard bilinear scoring, motivating inference-time item-side representation. Agent4POI inverts this computation: given a situational context, a four-phase LLM agent generates dynamic, context-specific affordance queries (Phase 1) and executes a five-step cross-modal chain-of-thought over image, review, and metadata evidence (Phase 2). The resulting uncertainty-aware affordance representation is grounded in Gibsonian affordance theory. These cross-modal verdicts form a structured, uncertainty-adjusted affordance representation (Phase 3), which is aligned with user preferences via a semantic caching system for low-latency ranking (Phase 4). On three POI benchmarks and three evaluation configurations (standard, cold-start, context-shift), Agent4POI achieves a 23.2% relative gain over the strongest baseline and degrades by only 7.5% under context-shift versus 16--17\% for the strongest baselines. In cold-start scenarios, Agent4POI outperforms the best content-based baseline by up to 2.4x, whereas ID-based methods fail to generalize.

</details>


### [173] [An LLM-RAG Approach for Healthy Eating Index-Informed Personalized Food Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15213)
*Yibin Wang,Yanjie Yang,Grace Melo Guerrero,Rodolfo M. Nayga,Azlan Zahid*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一种结合健康饮食指数（HEI）与检索增强生成（RAG）技术的新型AI框架，利用标准化营养数据库驱动大语言模型，为用户提供科学、可解释的个性化饮食改进建议，显著提升了饮食质量表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前食用的推荐系统多依赖于缺乏标准化的食品数据库，且未能与经过验证的营养评估指标建立紧密联系，导致推荐效果受限。因此，需要开发一种基于标准化营养数据且能与验证指标挂钩的个性化推荐机制。

Method: 提出了一种基于健康饮食指数（HEI）的检索增强生成（RAG）框架。该方法结合了NHANES数据库和FPED数据库，通过FPED构建食物层面的嵌入空间，利用RAG管道结合预训练的大语言模型（LLM）进行食物推荐，并通过计算HEI评分指导替代或添加食物的评估。

Result: 仿真实验表明，该系统使受试者的平均HEI评分提高了6.45分；HEI评分超过50分的用户比例从45.12%提升至61.26%；分位数分析显示HEI分布在整体上得到了一致的改善。

Conclusion: 该研究证明，基于HEI（健康饮食指数）和RAG（检索增强生成）的AI系统能够提供更精准、可解释且个性化的营养指导，有效改善用户的饮食质量。

Abstract: Diet quality is a leading determinant of chronic disease risk. Advances in artificial intelligence (AI) have enabled food recommendation systems to adapt suggestions to user preferences and health goals. However, most current systems rely on loosely curated food databases and provide limited connection to a validated index. In this study, we propose a Healthy Eating Index (HEI) informed retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) framework that combines standardized nutrition databases with large language models (LLMs) for personalized food recommendations. Our proposed method anchors retrieval in the National Health and Nutrition Examination Survey (NHANES) and the Food Patterns Equivalents Database (FPED). A food-level embedding space is constructed from FPED-derived textual descriptions. For each entity, the system computes baseline HEI scores, retrieves candidate foods for intake recommendations, and estimates the HEI impact of simple substitutions or additions. A constrained RAG pipeline instantiated with a pretrained OpenAI LLM generates personalized recommendations and sources based on nutrient profiles and HEI contributions. The simulation results showed a mean HEI improvement of 6.45, with the proportion of users HEI over 50 increasing from 45.12 to 61.26. Quantile analysis revealed consistent improved shifts across the HEI distribution. Our findings suggest that the proposed LLM-RAG-based AI systems can support more precise, explainable, and personalized nutrition guidance to improve diet quality.

</details>


### [174] [Fortress: A Case Study in Stabilizing Search Recommendations via Temporal Data Augmentation and Feature Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15299)
*Milind Pandurang Jagre,Jia Huang,Dayvid V. R. Oliveira,Zhinan Cheng,Babak Seyed Aghazadeh,Puja Das,Chris Alvino,Jinda Han,Kailash Thiyagarajan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对搜索与推荐系统中特征引起的模型预测波动问题，本文提出了 Fortress 框架，通过识别并剔除导致不稳定的特征，在提升模型稳定性的同时保持了准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在搜索和推荐系统中，某些特征会引起预测分数的瞬时波动（时间不稳定性），降低模型可靠性，尤其在影响下游决策的多阶段系统中影响更为严重。现有特征（如 LLM/BERT 与基于交互的特征）在覆盖率与稳定性之间存在权衡。

Method: 提出 Fortress 四步法：(1) 收集历史快照；(2) 识别预测不稳定的样本；(3) 隔离并删除引起不稳定的特征；(4) 使用稳定特征重训练模型。该方法旨在抑制高波动性的交互特征影响，同时保留预测价值。

Result: 在某大规模应用商店的“查询-应用相关性”模型中进行验证，离线实验结果显示预测稳定性（由变异系数衡量）显著提高，分类性能（PR-AUC）亦有提升。

Conclusion: Fortress 框架通过识别并剔除导致预测不稳定的特征，成功平衡了模型预测的稳定性与准确性，在生产环境（大规模应用商店）中显著优化了查询-应用相关性任务的性能。

Abstract: In search and recommendation systems, predictive models often suffer from temporal instability when certain input features introduce volatility in output scores. This instability can degrade model reliability and user experience especially in multi-stage systems where consistent predictions are critical for downstream decision making. We introduce Fortress, a general framework for enhancing model stability and accuracy by identifying and pruning features that contribute to inconsistent prediction scores over time. Fortress leverages historical snapshots temporally partitioned datasets capturing score fluctuations for the same entity across periods and follows a four-step process: (1) collect historical snapshots, (2) identify samples with unstable predictions, (3) isolate and remove instability-inducing features, and (4) retrain models using only stable features. While semantic features from LLMs and BERT-based models improve generalization, they often lack full query or entity coverage. Engagement-based features offer strong predictive power but tend to introduce temporal instability. Fortress mitigates this trade-off by suppressing the volatility of engagement signals while retaining their predictive value leading to more stable and accurate models. We validate Fortress on a query-to-app relevance model in a large-scale app marketplace. Offline experiments demonstrate notable improvements in prediction stability (measured by Coefficient of Variation) and classification performance (measured by PR-AUC).

</details>


### [175] [Jobs' AI Exposure Should Be Measured from Evidence, Not Model Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15474)
*Luca Mouchel,Pierre Bouquet,Yossi Sheffi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文批评了当前仅依赖LLM零样本推断AI职业暴露度的方法，提出利用检索增强（RAG）结合实际证据进行动态、可验证的测量，研究证明该方案在准确性及现实匹配度上明显优于当前基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的AI暴露度测量过于依赖LLM的零样本提示，缺乏透明的推理链和外部证据验证，导致测量结果不可靠，可能误导政策制定者、资金分配及劳动力规划。

Method: 提出了一种检索增强（RAG）框架，对比LLM零样本推断，该方法引入外部证据（新闻和学术摘要）来评估O*NET数据库中18,796个职业-任务对的AI暴露度，并通过自动和人工评估进行验证。

Result: 研究发现，实验提出的基于证据的“接地（Grounded）”测量方法在超过72%的冲突案例中优于基准方法，且与现实世界中AI的实际应用情况更为吻合。

Conclusion: AI暴露度的测量应从基于LLM先验的零样本分类，转向基于外部证据的动态评估。为了确保政策导向和劳动力规划的准确性，相关指标必须具备可重复性、外部基础性（Grounding）和可检查性，并随着AI技术的演进而定期更新。

Abstract: This position paper argues that job exposure to AI should be measured with grounded, evidence-based methods, not inferred from LLM priors alone. Current theoretical exposure measures use zero-shot prompting to classify task-level AI exposure, generating labels with no explicit evidence, no transparent chain of reasoning, and no external validation. The stakes of these measurements are too high to rely on such methods, as they influence policy making, where public and private funds are directed, and how workers understand their future prospects. We therefore argue that AI capability claims should meet three standards: reproducibility, external grounding, and inspectability. We propose a retrieval-augmented framework that assigns AI exposure labels to all 18,796 occupation--task pairs in O*NET 30.2, using open-weight reasoning and instruct models with retrieved news articles and academic paper abstracts as evidence of current AI capabilities. Relative to a zero-shot baseline, the grounded condition is preferred in over 72\% of disagreement cases under both automatic and human evaluation, and yields scores that align more closely with observed real-world AI usage. Taken together, these findings suggest that evidence-grounded measurement better captures what current AI systems can plausibly do in practice, rather than what a model asserts without external evidence. Because AI capabilities continue to change, the measurements used to inform policy must evolve with them: theoretical AI exposure scores should be periodically reassessed, not inherited as immutable ground truth.

</details>


### [176] [Generative Long-term User Interest Modeling for Click-Through Rate Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15905)
*Jiangli Shao,Kaifu Zheng,Hao Fang,Huimu Ye,Zhiwei Liu,Bo Zhang,Shu Han,Xingxing Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对长序列CTR预估中传统检索式方法的偏差与计算瓶颈，提出GenLI模型，利用生成式兴趣建模替代目标匹配，实现了更全面、高效的用户兴趣表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两阶段（GSU+ESU）长序列点击率预估模型中，目标导向的GSU容易导致兴趣特征偏差且不完整；同时，基于配对相似度的检索过程在线计算耗时高，且忽略了行为间的交互信息。

Method: 提出了 GenLI 模型，包含三个核心组件：(1)兴趣生成模块(IGM)：采用生成式方法捕捉多维度、目标无关的隐性用户兴趣；(2)行为检索模块(BRM)：摒弃繁琐的匹配计算，采用简单查找法(O(1)复杂度)提取相关行为；(3)兴趣融合模块(IFM)：利用门控机制整合生成兴趣与检索行为。

Result: GenLI 有效提升了长序列下用户兴趣的表示质量，在保证兴趣多样性的同时，通过降低检索复杂度，实现了预测精度与推理速度的最佳平衡。

Conclusion: GenLI通过生成式兴趣建模，不仅提升了CTR预估的准确性，还通过简化检索过程实现了更卓越的在线推理效率，证明了从“匹配式检索”转向“生成式建模”是处理长序列用户行为的有效路径。

Abstract: Modeling long-term user interests with massive historical user behaviors enhances click-through rate (CTR) prediction performance in advertising and recommendation systems. Typically, a two-stage framework is widely adopted, where a general search unit (GSU) first retrieves top-$k$ relevant behaviors towards the target item, and an exact search unit (ESU) generates interest features via tailored attention. However, current target-centered GSU would ignore other latent user interests, leading to incomplete and biased interest features. Additionally, the matching-based retrieval process in GSUs depends on the pairwise similarity score between target item and each historical behavior, which not only becomes time-consuming for online services as user behaviors continue to grow, but also overlooks the interaction information among user behaviors. To combat these problems, we propose a \textbf{Gen}erative \textbf{L}ong-term user \textbf{I}nterest model named GenLI for CTR prediction. GenLI consists of an interest generation module (IGM), a behavior retrieval module (BRM), and an interest fusion module (IFM). The IGM generates multiple interest distributions to indicate different aspects of real-time user interests, which is target-independent and incorporates interaction information among behaviors, ensuring complete and diverse interest features. The BRM selects related behaviors via a simple lookup operation, reducing the time complexity for weighting each behavior to $O(1)$. Finally, the IFM uses delicate gating mechanisms to generate interest features. Based on the generation process, GenLI improves the diversity of user interests and avoids complex matching-based behavioral retrieval, achieving a better balance between accuracy and efficiency for CTR prediction.

</details>


### [177] [MERVIN: A Unified Framework for Multimodal Event Retrieval in Vietnamese News Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16120)
*Anh-Tai Pham-Nguyen,Tung-Duong Le-Duc,Anh-Duy Le,Trung-Hieu Truong-Le*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MERVIN 是一个专门针对越南语新闻视频的多模态检索框架，通过融合视觉、文本及优化后的语音处理技术，配合交互式查询反馈，大幅提升了视频内容的检索效率与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决在线视频平台对于高效、语义准确的事件检索的需求，特别是在越南语视频数据处理中受限于语音识别噪声和语义对齐的挑战。

Method: 构建了一个包含多模态处理的统一框架：利用 Gemini 1.5 Flash 优化语音转录文本，使用 Perception Encoder 提取视觉特征并结合越南语语言模型生成语义嵌入，通过 Milvus 数据库进行相似度索引，并配套 React 交互界面实现查询迭代优化。

Result: 在 AI Challenge HCMC 2025 资格赛中获得 88 分中的 79 分，并在决赛环节成功检索出所有查询的相关结果。

Conclusion: MERVIN 框架在越南新闻视频检索任务中表现优异，不仅在学术测评中取得显著成绩，且在实际应用中展示了卓越的召回能力。

Abstract: The growth of online video platforms drives the need for effective, semantically grounded event retrieval. We present MERVIN, a unified multimodal framework for Vietnamese news videos that integrates keyframes, transcripts, and video summaries. Transcript quality is enhanced via Gemini 1.5 Flash, reducing noise from accents, background sounds, and recognition errors. Visual features are extracted with Perception Encoder, while a Vietnamese language model produces textual embeddings; both are indexed in Milvus for efficient similarity-based retrieval. In addition, a React-based interface enables iterative query refinement across modalities, improving semantic alignment. Experimental results on Vietnamese news videos demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed system, with MERVIN achieving 79 out of 88 points in AI Challenge HCMC 2025 qualification phase and successfully retrieved all results for every query in the final round.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [Exploration of $k$-edge-deficient temporal graphs in linear time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15833)
*Ivan Lahtin,Viktor Zamaraev*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该研究解决了 $k$-边缺失恒连通时间图的探索时间上限问题，证明了在近静态限制下，时间图探索可实现与静态图同级的线性时间复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然静态图的探索可以在线性时间内完成，但时间图的时间限制可能显著增加探索时间。研究旨在探索为何即使在快照均连通且与静态图差异仅为 $k$ 条边的条件下，时间图探索仍比静态图复杂，并寻求改进探索时间的上限。

Method: 通过理论分析和算法构造，证明了任何恒连通的 $k$-边缺失时间图都存在长度为 $O(nk \log k)$ 的探索调度，且该调度可在多项式时间内计算得出。

Result: 将探索所需的时间复杂度从之前的 $O(kn \log n)$ 优化至 $O(nk \log k)$，消除了对顶点数量 $n$ 的额外对数依赖，实现了近乎最优的探索效率。

Conclusion: 对于恒定 $k$ 的情况，该研究给出了 $Θ(n)$ 的最优探索时间，证明了在该近静态环境下，时间图探索本质上保留了静态图遍历的线性时间特性。

Abstract: We study the Temporal Exploration problem, where an agent must visit all vertices of a temporal graph while traversing at most one available edge per time step. Unlike static graphs, which can be explored in linear time, temporal constraints can substantially increase exploration time even when every snapshot of the graph is connected.
  To better understand the source of this complexity, we focus on a near-static setting and consider always-connected $k$-edge-deficient temporal graphs, in which each snapshot is connected and differs from a fixed underlying $n$-vertex graph by at most $k$ edges. Although such graphs are structurally close to static graphs, they can still exhibit non-trivial temporal behaviour. Prior work showed that these graphs can be explored in $O(kn \log n)$ time steps and established a lower bound of $Ω(n \log k)$, leaving open whether linear-time exploration in $n$ is possible.
  We resolve this question by showing that any always-connected $k$-edge-deficient temporal graph admits an exploration schedule of length $O(nk \log k)$. Moreover, given such a temporal graph, the corresponding exploration schedule can be computed in polynomial time. The obtained bound is linear in the number of vertices up to a factor depending only on $k$, removes the extraneous logarithmic dependence on $n$, and is nearly optimal. In particular, for constant $k$, our result yields an order-optimal $Θ(n)$ exploration time, showing that temporal exploration in this near-static regime essentially retains the linear-time character of static graph traversal.

</details>


### [179] [Complexity of Non-Log-Concave Sampling in Fisher Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15859)
*Sinho Chewi,Andre Wibisono*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了非对数凹采样的查询复杂度，通过结合近端采样器和改进的受限高斯预言机，实现了具有优化维度依赖性的相对 Fisher 信息采样，并建立了非对数凹采样与高精度对数凹采样难度之间的理论联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究从非对数凹（non-log-concave）、对数平滑（log-smooth）分布中进行采样时，获取相对 Fisher 信息保证的查询复杂度问题，即寻找优化中近似平稳点的采样模拟。

Method: 采用基于近端采样器（Proximal Sampler）的算法，该采样器是 Langevin 扩散的隐式离散化，并通过实现受限高斯预言机（RGO）来执行向后步骤；利用了近期在 Rényi 散度下对数凹采样的高精度保证结果。

Result: 该方法在相对 Fisher 信息上实现了更好的复杂度保证，继承了与对数凹采样相同的维度依赖性。

Conclusion: 作者证明了所提出的算法在相对 Fisher 信息方面达到了与对数凹采样相同的维度依赖性，且优于先前的非对数凹采样结果。同时，作者通过反向归约证明了非对数凹采样维度依赖性的任何改进都将提升高精度对数凹采样的维度依赖性。

Abstract: We study the query complexity of obtaining a relative Fisher information guarantee for sampling from a log-smooth non-log-concave distribution; this is a sampling analog of finding an approximate stationary point in optimization. Our algorithm is based on the proximal sampler, which is an implicit discretization of the Langevin diffusion, and requires an implementation of the backward step known as the restricted Gaussian oracle (RGO). We show that by leveraging the recent results for log-concave sampling with high-accuracy guarantees in Rényi divergence, we can obtain an approximate RGO implementation that -- when used with the proximal sampler -- yields a complexity guarantee in relative Fisher information that inherits the same dimension dependence as log-concave sampling, and improves upon prior work for non-log-concave sampling. We also show a converse reduction that any improvement in the dimension dependence in relative Fisher information for non-log-concave sampling will yield an improved dimension dependence for high-accuracy log-concave sampling.

</details>


### [180] [On the parameterized complexity of Broadcast Independence and Broadcast Packing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16001)
*Joanne Dumont,Edouard Nemery,Anthony Perez,Florian Sikora*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了独立广播和广播填充问题的参数化计算复杂性，证明了它们在树宽和直径参数下是 FPT 的，并给出了一系列 hardness 结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究广义广播问题（Broadcast Domination）的两个变体——独立广播（Independent Broadcast）和广播填充（Broadcast Packing），并系统探究这些问题的参数化计算复杂性。

Method: 使用基于好的树分解（nice tree decomposition）的动态规划算法进行 FPT 算法设计，并通过归约证明 W[1]-hardness。

Result: 证明了上述问题在不同参数（树宽、直径、路径宽、顶点覆盖数）下的复杂性分类，填补了该领域的部分理论空白。

Conclusion: Broadcast Independence 和 Broadcast Packing 在树宽（treewidth）和直径（diameter）参数下是 FPT 的，且在树宽参数下是 XP 的；Broadcast Independence 对于路径宽（pathwidth）参数是 W[1]-hard；带权重的版本对于顶点覆盖（vertex cover）参数是 W[1]-hard；此外，还为 Broadcast Independence 提供了一个树宽参数下的常数因子近似算法。

Abstract: A broadcast on a connected graph is a function f that assigns each vertex v an integer f(v) with 0 <= f(v) <= ecc(v) where ecc(v) denotes the eccentricity of v. A vertex u hears a broadcasting vertex v (with f(v)>0) if u is at distance at most f(v) from v. Beyond the classical broadcast domination problem, where every vertex is required to hear at least one vertex, two variants raise intriguing combinatorial and algorithmic questions. In an independent broadcast, no broadcasting vertex hears another broadcasting vertex, while a broadcast packing requires that every vertex hears at most one broadcasting vertex. The corresponding problems Broadcast Independence and Broadcast Packing ask for broadcasts of values at least k under these constraints, where the value is the sum of the broadcast values. We initiate a systematic study of the parameterized complexity of such problems. We prove that Broadcast Independence and Broadcast Packing are FPT parameterized by the treewidth plus the diameter of G, with a family of dynamic-programming algorithms over nice tree decompositions. We obtain as a corollary that both problems are FPT parameterized by k and the treewidth of G and XP for treewidth only. The latter result shows that the known algorithm for trees (Bessy and Rautenbach, DAM 2022) can indeed be extended to bounded treewidth graphs. On the negative side, we show that Broadcast Independence is W[1]-hard parameterized by the pathwidth of G. Note that this result completes the picture for parameter k and treewidth for Broadcast Independence since it is known to be W[1]-hard for k only. We complement these results by showing that a weighted version of both problems, where the input comes with a weight function on the edges, is W[1]-hard parameterized by the vertex cover of G. Finally, we provide a constant-factor approximation algorithm parameterized by treewidth for Broadcast Independence.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [181] [Fairness-Aware Retrieval Optimization for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15790)
*Yingqi Zhao,Vasilis Efthymiou,Jyrki Nummenmaa,Kostas Stefanidis*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对检索增强生成（RAG）中检索过程导致的偏差传播问题，本文提出了一种名为FARO的公平性感知检索框架，通过重排序优化与高效求解算法，在保证内容相关性的前提下有效缓解了生成偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统在引入外部知识以提升可靠性的同时，检索过程可能引入并传播偏见，尤其是在Top-k设置中，多个文档共同影响生成结果，缺乏有效的机制来建模和控制这种偏见。

Method: 提出了一种公平性感知检索框架，该方法结合了通过重排序进行的受控偏差注入、位置感知偏差传播模型，以及基于“通过双超平面逼近的二次公平性”（FARO）的优化公式，利用问题分解实现高效求解。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法能够有效减轻生成过程中的偏差，同时保持良好的相关性。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种针对RAG系统的公平性感知检索框架，通过FARO算法实现了在保持检索相关性的同时有效缓解生成偏差，为提升RAG系统的公平性提供了原则性的解决方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) improves reliability of large language models by incorporating external knowledge, but the retrieval process can introduce bias that propagates to generated outputs. This issue is particularly challenging in top-k settings, where multiple documents jointly influence generation. We propose a fairness-aware retrieval framework that models and controls this bias. Our approach combines controlled bias injection via reranking, a position-aware model of bias propagation, and an optimization formulation that balances relevance and fairness. We further introduce a scalable solution based on Quadratic Fairness via Dual Hyperplane Approximation (FARO), which enables efficient optimization through problem decomposition. Experimental results show that our method effectively mitigates generation bias while preserving relevance. This work provides a principled approach for fairness-aware retrieval in RAG systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [182] [PhysBrain 1.0 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15298)
*Shijie Lian,Bin Yu,Xiaopeng Lin,Changti Wu,Hang Yuan,Xiaolin Hu,Zhaolong Shen,Yuzhuo Miao,Haishan Liu,Yuxuan Tian,Yukun Shi,Cong Huang,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysBrain 1.0通过将大规模人类第一人称视频转化为物理常识问答监督，有效提升了机器人的物理理解能力及具身控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作模型仅依赖机器人轨迹数据，在学习广泛的物理理解方面覆盖面有限，难以处理复杂的现实环境。

Method: 构建了一个数据引擎，从大规模人类第一人称视频中提取场景元素、空间动态、动作执行和深度关系，并转化为问答（QA）监督信号训练PhysBrain VLM；随后通过一种保持能力且对语言敏感的适配设计，将物理先验迁移至视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略中。

Result: 在多个多模态问答（ERQA、PhysBench）和具身控制任务（SimplerEnv-WidowX、LIBERO、RoboCasa）中达到了当前最优（SOTA）水平，且在SimplerEnv中展现出极强的域外泛化性能。

Conclusion: 通过大规模人类第一人称视频学习物理常识，能够有效弥补机器人轨迹数据在物理理解上的局限性，是实现具身智能的一种高效方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models have advanced rapidly, but robot trajectories alone provide limited coverage for learning broad physical understanding. PhysBrain 1.0 studies a complementary route: converting large-scale human egocentric video into structured physical commonsense supervision before robot adaptation. Our data engine extracts scene elements, spatial dynamics, action execution, and depth-aware relations, then turns them into question-answer supervision for training PhysBrain VLMs. The resulting physical priors are further transferred to VLA policies through a capability-preserving and language-sensitive adaptation design. Across multimodal QA benchmarks and embodied control benchmarks, including ERQA, PhysBench, SimplerEnv-WidowX, LIBERO, and RoboCasa, PhysBrain 1.0 achieves SOTA results and shows especially strong out-of-domain performance on SimplerEnv. These results suggest that scaling physical commonsense from human interaction video can provide an effective bridge from multimodal understanding to robot action.

</details>


### [183] [Diffusion Policy for Coordinated Control of a Nonholonomic Mobile Base and Dual Arms in Door Opening and Passing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15352)
*Shangqun Yu,Matthew En,Daniel Wu,Sangjun Park,Ziyi Zhou,Seyed Fakoorian,Donghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于扩散模型的端到端控制策略，使机器人能够自主完成包括转动把手、拉门、身体通过在内的复杂开门任务，实现了双臂操作与移动底座的高度协同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于状态机的方法在机器人开门任务中鲁棒性差，难以推广。现有模仿学习方法尚未解决非完整底座与双臂在复杂开门与穿越任务中的高水平实时协同问题。

Method: 采用基于扩散模型（Diffusion-based）的视觉运动控制策略（Visuomotor Control Policy），实现机器人双臂操作与底座移动的实时端到端协同控制。

Result: 该策略在处理带阻尼的拉门任务中实现了高成功率，且对外部扰动表现出较强的鲁棒性，超越了传统工程化方法的表现。

Conclusion: 基于扩散模型的端到端视觉运动控制策略能够有效解决机器人开门这一复杂的高约束长期任务，证明了在无需人工定义状态机的情况下，协同操作与移动导航的可行性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Opening heavy, self closing doors, especially those that require pulling remains a long standing challenge in robotics. Humans naturally employ both arms in a dexterous manner, rotating the handle, widening the gap, holding the door, switching arms when needed, and moving through while maintaining clearance. To replicate such behaviors, a robot must perform a long sequence of motions spanning multiple stages and interactions with different parts of the door. Traditional approaches rely on state machines that transition between manually defined stages (e.g., pulling after the knob is rotated, passing after the gap is sufficiently wide). While intuitive, these methods lack robustness, as hand crafted trajectories fail to generalize to the diversity of real world conditions without extensive engineering effort. Recent advances in imitation learning offer a scalable alternative, yet no existing visual action model has demonstrated simultaneous coordination of a nonholonomic base and dual arms for the complete door opening and passing task. In this paper, we tackle this complex, highly constrained problem using a diffusion based visuomotor control policy. Our results demonstrate that a single end to end policy can be learned to execute long horizon tasks requiring tight coordination between manipulation and locomotion. The resulting policy not only achieves a high success rate in opening and traversing damped pull doors but also demonstrates strong robustness to external disturbances capabilities that are difficult to realize with traditional methods.

</details>


### [184] [Where to Perch in a Tree: Vision-Guidance for Tree-Grasping Drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15430)
*Alex Dunnett,Leonie Bottomley,Mirko Kovac,Basaran Bahadir Kocer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合计算机视觉算法的自主无人机树木栖息点选择方法，通过评估分支几何特征，在测试集中实现了76%的目标识别成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决视觉引导自主无人机在复杂的树木环境中识别并选择最佳适航栖息点的问题，以提高无人机的生存能力和任务效率。

Method: 结合了机器学习、图像分割和二值图像形态学算法；根据分支宽度、坡度（水平倾角）和曲率对潜在栖息分支进行评估，而非仅寻找最近分支。

Result: 在包含超过10,000张城市树木图像的数据集上进行测试，该方法成功识别出了76%的可行栖息目标（满足分支直径要求及空间尺寸要求）。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的视觉引导树枝栖息方法是可行的，为实现自主无人机在复杂树木环境下的精确栖息奠定了基础，未来通过引入深度感知和姿态传感器可进一步增强通用性和准确性。

Abstract: This study demonstrates a method to locate an ideal perch location on a tree for vision-guided autonomous tree-perching drones. Various image processing algorithms, including those used for machine learning, image segmentation and binary image morphology, are implemented to assess the shape and structure of a tree.
