Method: 提出 Asteria 运行时系统：1. 将优化器状态解耦并动态分布在 GPU、CPU 及 NVMe 中；2. 利用训练钩子（hooks）实现异步计算（将昂贵的逆根运算分流至宿主机）；3. 采用有界陈旧（bounded-staleness）协议以优化分布式场景下的同步效率。

Result: 在 1B 参数模型（单卡 GB10）和 7B 参数模型（多节点 GH200）上测试，Asteria 显著降低了优化器开销和延迟尖峰，缩短了墙上时间（wall-clock time），并在内存受限及分布式环境下保持了收敛优势。

Conclusion: 通过系统层面的架构重构（解耦优化逻辑、异构存储与异步计算），Asteria 成功解决了二阶优化在 LLM 训练中的系统资源瓶颈，证明了系统化管理是实现高效二阶优化的关键。

Abstract: Second-order methods offer an attractive path toward more sample-efficient LLM training, but their practical use is often blocked by the systems cost of maintaining and updating large matrix-based optimizer states. We introduce \textbf{Asteria}, a runtime system designed to remove this bottleneck by separating second-order optimization logic from the critical GPU training path. Rather than keeping all preconditioner state on the accelerator, Asteria dynamically distributes optimizer state across GPU memory, CPU memory, and optional NVMe storage according to architectural constraints and runtime pressure. It further uses training hooks to prepare shadow states in advance, allowing expensive inverse-root computations to proceed asynchronously on the host while GPU computation continues. For distributed training, Asteria employs a bounded-staleness protocol that limits synchronization frequency while preserving optimizer effectiveness through topology-aware coordination.
  We evaluate Asteria on both memory-constrained and distributed training settings. On a DGX Spark platform with a single GB10 GPU and 128GB unified memory, Asteria supports second-order training for a 1B-parameter language model. On multi-node GH200 systems, it lowers visible optimizer overhead, reduces recurring latency spikes, accelerates convergence in wall-clock time, and maintains the optimization advantages of SOAP and KL-Shampoo in a 7B-parameter language model. Our results suggest that second-order LLM training can be made practical not by simplifying the optimizer alone, but by rethinking how optimizer state, background computation, and distributed synchronization are managed at the runtime level.

</details>


### [221] [Designing Datacenter Power Delivery Hierarchies for the AI Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16255)
*Grant Wilkins,Fiodar Kazhamiaka,Alok Gautam Kumbhare,Chaojie Zhang,Ricardo Bianchini*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对AI算力激增带来的功率密度挑战，本文提出了一种评估数据中心电力配送设计的框架，指出在AI时代，规划的核心指标应从“安装功率”转向“长期可部署容量”，以应对资源闲置并优化长期成本与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI加速器对机架功率密度需求的激增（预计2027年接近1MW），传统的功率配送设计面临挑战。功率密度不匹配会导致电力资源“闲置”（stranded），而在AI时代电网容量稀缺的情况下，如何跨越多个硬件周期设计高效的功率配送架构变得极其复杂。

Method: 开发了一个综合评估框架，考虑吞吐量、功耗和成本指标。该框架结合了GPU、计算和存储的预测模型，并利用微软Azure的实际生产数据进行建模，以模拟真实的到达、超额认购和退役序列。

Result: 研究量化了多资源闲置对可部署容量、资本支出（CAPEX）和系统交付性能的影响，揭示了机架级和Pod级AI系统功率密度上升对设计产出的具体驱动作用。

Conclusion: AI数据中心的设计目标不应仅是装机容量（MW），而应是长期的可部署容量。多资源闲置（stranding）对容量、资本支出和性能有重大影响，且随着AI系统功率密度的提升，该问题日益严峻。

Abstract: Demand for AI accelerators is rapidly increasing rack power density, with projections approaching 1MW per deployment by 2027. This poses a major challenge for datacenter power delivery designers. As power densities increase, a datacenter designed for a different target density may strand power, i.e., may be unable to use all the power that its delivery hierarchy has provisioned. Designs must remain efficient over long datacenter lifetimes and multiple hardware generations. Power utilization is particularly important as grid power capacity is a scarce resource in the AI era.
  Designing an efficient power delivery hierarchy for the long run is difficult because rack placement feasibility, workload impact, and cost depend jointly on electrical topology, deployment granularity, placement policy, power oversubscription, and workload mix. Moreover, each of these factors evolve over time, have inter-dependencies across multiple resource dimensions, and generally do not lend themselves to closed-form analysis.
  To address this challenge, we develop a framework for evaluating datacenter power delivery designs using throughput, power, and cost metrics over realistic arrival, oversubscription, and decommissioning sequences. The framework combines projection models for GPU, compute, and storage deployments with operational factors grounded in production data from Microsoft Azure. Our results show that multi-resource stranding materially changes deployable capacity, effective capital expenditure, and delivered performance, and quantify how rising density from rack- and pod-scale AI systems shapes these outcomes. For AI datacenter design, the relevant planning objective is not installed megawatts, but deployable capacity over time.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [222] [GenAI-Driven Approach to RISC-V Supply Chain Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15223)
*Nenad Petrovic,Andre Schamschurko,Yingjie Xu,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合LLM、VLM和MDE的综合工作流，通过从异构数据中提取关系并构建知识图谱，实现对RISC-V供应链风险与依赖关系的智能化分析与形式化验证，显著提升了供应链的韧性评估效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对RISC-V及半导体供应链中数据碎片化、异构化（文本与图像混合）以及复杂依赖关系难以系统化分析的挑战，旨在提升供应链透明度与风险响应能力。

Method: 提出一种融合LLM（语义理解）、VLM（视觉文档/图表提取）和MDE（知识图谱构建与约束模型验证）的综合工作流，并引入人机交互（Human-in-the-loop）机制进行专家验证。

Result: 在RISC-V生态系统场景中进行了实验验证，证明了该方法能够成功提取关键实体、识别供应链瓶颈、评估风险，并能够为复杂供应链提供可执行的洞察。

Conclusion: 该方法通过结合大语言模型、视觉语言模型和模型驱动工程，能够有效处理异构、非结构化的半导体供应链数据，为复杂供应链的弹性评估和风险决策提供了一种高效、透明且可验证的分析方案。

Abstract: This paper presents an LLM-empowered workflow for RISC-V supply chain analysis, integrating Vision-Language Models (VLMs) and Model-Driven Engineering (MDE) to enable comprehensive, multimodal data-driven insights. The proposed approach addresses the challenges of heterogeneous and unstructured supply chain data by leveraging LLMs for textual understanding and VLMs for extracting information from visual artifacts such as diagrams, tables, and scanned documents. These models collaboratively identify key entities and relationships, which are then organized into a knowledge graph representing supply chain components and their interdependencies. For analytical reasoning, the workflow incorporates MDE techniques and constraint-based modeling to enable formal validation of dependencies, detection of bottlenecks, and assessment of risks. The synergy between LLM- and VLM-based semantic understanding and MDE-based formal analysis supports both exploratory and systematic evaluation of supply chain resilience. A human-in-the-loop mechanism further enables interactive querying and expert validation. The approach is evaluated in RISC-V ecosystem scenarios, demonstrating its effectiveness in generating actionable insights, enhancing transparency, and supporting decision-making in complex semiconductor supply chains.

</details>


### [223] [ITHICA: Intra-Thread Instruction Checking Approach for Defect-Induced Silent Data Corruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15638)
*Ioanna Vavelidou,Subho S. Banerjee,Eric X. Liu,Mike Fuller,Subhasish Mitra,Caroline Trippel*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: ITHICA是一种能自动将任意程序转化为高性能CPU缺陷检测测试的方法，通过利用指令复制与对比技术，有效捕捉由制造缺陷导致的、受上下文影响的静默数据损坏（SDC）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超大规模数据中心（Hyperscaler）报告的静默数据损坏（SDC）现象愈发严重，且被认为是由硅片制造缺陷引起的，现有的功能测试方法不足以应对这些隐蔽的硬件缺陷。

Method: 提出了ITHICA框架，通过在任意程序中自动插入指令级的线程内错误检查（主要采用指令复制和输出对比）来生成功能测试。利用了核心洞察：即硅片缺陷会导致受上下文影响的不一致错误（同一指令在不同执行上下文下产生不同结果）。

Result: 在超过3000台CPU服务器上的评估显示，ITHICA能够将工业级测试程序、数据中心工作负载和常用库转换为功能测试；其检测能力比基准测试原生的检查高出39%。

Conclusion: ITHICA通过自动化生成功能性测试，有效地提高了检测CPU物理缺陷引起的静默数据损坏（SDC）的能力。实验表明，其检测效果优于传统的原生检查，并揭示了关于缺陷行为的新见解，挑战了既往研究的结论。

Abstract: Hyperscaler reports of silent data corruptions (SDCs), presumed to be caused by silicon manufacturing defects, have motivated the development of functional tests for detecting defective CPUs. We present ITHICA, an approach for automatically generating functional tests for defect-induced errors from arbitrary programs by inserting intra-thread, instruction-level error checks, primarily leveraging instruction duplication and output comparison. Our key insight is that the most pernicious defects cause inconsistent errors: two executions of the same instruction within the same thread, given the same inputs, can produce different architectural outputs depending on the execution context in which they run. By exploiting this insight, ITHICA enables arbitrary programs to serve as tests and identifies affected instructions upon error detections. We use ITHICA to transform industrial hyperscaler test programs (our baseline), datacenter workloads, and common libraries into functional tests, and evaluate them on over 3,000 CPU servers. ITHICA error checks detect 39% more defective servers than native checks within the ITHICA tests derived from our baseline programs, and enable novel findings on defect behavior that challenge conclusions drawn by prior hyperscaler fleet studies.

</details>


### [224] [ICP: Exploiting Instruction Correlation for Prefetching Irregular Memory Accesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15645)
*Mengming Li,Chenlu Miao,Buqing Xu,Qijun Zhang,Xiangfeng Sun,Ceyu Xu,Yuan Xie,Wenkai Li,Shang Liu,Zhiyao Xie*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于指令相关性的预取技术（ICP），通过挖掘指令级数据依赖关系而非内存地址递归，实现了高效且低开销的不规则内存访问预取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间预取器在处理弱递归或无递归的不规则内存访问（如间接访问）时效果受限，且通常需要巨大的元数据存储开销。旨在寻找一种更高效、更轻量级的机制来应对这些不规则访问挑战。

Method: 提出指令相关预取（Instruction-Correlation Prefetching, ICP）。该方法由地址递归转向指令相关性，通过学习指令间的持久数据依赖关系，利用前驱指令的执行结果来推测计算并预取后续不规则内存访问。

Result: 在不规则SPEC CPU和GAP基准测试中，ICP的性能分别比Triangel（时间预取器）高出14.0%，比DMP（间接预取器）高出6.0%，且硬件存储开销仅为2.1 KB，比时间预取器小了三个数量级以上。

Conclusion: ICP作为一种基于指令相关性的新型预取机制，通过利用指令间的稳定数据依赖关系取代传统的地址递归，成功克服了不规则内存访问预取的局限性。它在显著降低硬件存储开销的同时，实现了比现有最先进的预取器（如Triangel和DMP）更高的性能提升，证明了利用指令级相关性进行不规则访问预测的有效性。

Abstract: Irregular memory accesses pose challenges for effective and efficient data prefetching. While temporal prefetchers have recently shown promise for irregular memory access patterns, their effectiveness fundamentally depends on temporal address recurrence and large metadata storage. When memory addresses exhibit weak or no recurrence, as in indirect memory accesses, temporal prefetchers achieve limited performance gains while incurring substantial storage overhead.
  This paper proposes Instruction-Correlation Prefetching (ICP), a new hardware prefetching mechanism that exploits instruction-level correlations rather than memory-address correlations to handle irregular memory accesses. ICP observes that although memory addresses may not repeat, the instructions generating them often recur with stable data-dependency relationships. By learning these persistent instruction correlations, ICP speculatively computes and prefetches future irregular accesses using the execution results of their correlated predecessors. Across irregular SPEC CPU and GAP benchmarks, ICP outperforms the state-of-the-art temporal prefetcher Triangel by 14.0% and the indirect prefetcher DMP by 6.0%, while requiring only 2.1 KB of hardware storage, over three orders of magnitude smaller than temporal prefetchers.

</details>


### [225] [ADS-IMC: Accelerating Data Sorting with In-Memory Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16213)
*Narendra Singh Dhakad,Santosh Kumar Vishvakarma*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种业界首个基于6T SRAM的存内排序（In-Memory Sorting）架构，无需将数据搬运至处理器即可完成排序，相比忆阻器方案延迟降低了3.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统排序操作需要频繁地在主存与处理器之间进行数据搬运，导致了巨大的延迟和能量开销（冯·诺依曼架构瓶颈）。需探索一种通过在内存内部原地执行排序来消除数据搬运开销的方法。

Method: 提出了一种基于6T SRAM的存内计算（IMC）排序架构，直接在内存架构内部处理采用加权二进制基数格式的数据，避免了传统数据在存储器与处理器之间的频繁搬运。

Result: 该架构在延迟方面相较于传统的基于忆阻器的存内计算（IMC）排序方案实现了3.4倍的性能提升。

Conclusion: 该研究成功证明利用标准6T SRAM进行存内排序的可行性，打破了传统架构带来的数据搬运性能瓶颈，为存内计算领域提供了一种全新的高性能排序方案。

Abstract: Sorting is a fundamental operation across numerous computational domains. Traditionally, this process involves transferring data from main memory to a processing unit for sorting, followed by writing the sorted data back to memory. This conventional approach incurs substantial latency and energy overheads due to the extensive data movement between memory and processing components. To mitigate these overheads, this paper introduces novel architectures for executing sorting operations directly within the memory fabric, eliminating the need for off-chip data transfer. To our knowledge, this work represents the first exploration of in-memory sorting using 6T SRAM. The proposed architecture is designed to operate on data represented in the standard weighted binary radix format commonly used in digital systems. The proposed architecture achieves a significant 3.4x reduction in latency compared to memristor-based IMC sorting.

</details>


### [226] [TTP: A Hardware-Efficient Design for Precise Prefetching in Ray Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16253)
*Yavuz Selim Tozlu,Anshul Naithani,Huiyang Zhou*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对光线追踪的硬件预取器 TTP，通过重用硬件遍历栈信息进行高效的 BVH 节点预取，缓解了内存延迟瓶颈，显著提升了光线追踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光线追踪中的 BVH 遍历是内存密集型任务，GPU 线程频繁因等待树节点数据而陷入停顿，造成性能瓶颈。传统的预取方案难以有效应对这种复杂的树结构访问模式。

Method: 提出了一种名为树遍历预取器（TTP）的硬件预取方案。该方案利用 RT 单元中现有的遍历栈信息，在遍历过程中（特别是从栈中连续弹出节点时）预测并预取后续节点，从而隐藏内存延迟。

Result: 在 Vulkan-sim 2.0 模拟器上，TTP 实现了平均 1.48 倍（最高 1.89 倍）的加速，L1 预取准确率高达 98.92%，覆盖率为 31.54%，且硬件开销极小。

Conclusion: TTP 是解决光线追踪中 BVH 遍历内存带宽限制的有效方案，通过充分利用硬件遍历栈信息实现高精度预取，在低硬件开销下显著提升了渲染性能。

Abstract: Ray tracing (RT) is a 3D graphics technique that offers highly realistic visuals. It is becoming prominent and accessible as GPU vendors have integrated dedicated ray tracing acceleration hardware. However, tracing millions of rays through 3D scenes consisting of high numbers of triangles in real time is challenging and requires expensive hardware. The main bottleneck in RT workloads is the expensive Bounding Volume Hierarchy (BVH) traversal task, which is a large tree structure that encodes the 3D scene. BVH traversal is a memory-bound problem, as the GPU threads spend most of their time reading tree node data from memory. In this work, we attack the memory latency bottleneck of ray tracing through prefetching. We propose a novel hardware prefetcher, named Tree Traversal Prefetcher (TTP), for ray tracing. The main idea is to leverage the existing tree traversal stack in the RT units for highly accurate prefetching. In particular, TTP prefetches nodes using the addresses already available on the hardware traversal stacks of each thread. For DFS (Depth-first search) based traversal, prefetches are generated when nodes are being popped consecutively from the traversal stack, potentially corresponding to upward traversal through the tree. We evaluate TTP on a cycle-level simulator, Vulkan-sim 2.0, and show that it achieves 1.48x speedup on average (up to 1.89x) compared to the baseline, with nearly negligible hardware overhead. TTP achieves 98.92% average L1 accuracy, which is the ratio of the prefetched blocks being actually referenced by demand loads. The coverage, computed as the ratio of L1 miss reduction over baseline L1 misses, is 31.54%, correlating well with the achieved speedup.

</details>
